### [1] [Mixture of Geodesic Factor Analyzers on Riemannian Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06971)
*Hengchao Chen,Yuanyao Tan,Chao Huang,Hongtu Zhu,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了黎曼齐次空间上的测地因子分析器混合模型（MGFA），在每个混合成分中使用测地因子模型，比黎曼径向分布混合更强大，可实现具有各向异性子群体的流形值数据聚类。作者建立了MGFA最大似然估计的根号n一致性，提出了迭代估计算法并在球面、形状空间和双曲空间上实现，实验表明其在规范设定下显著优于竞争方法且在模型误设定下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形值数据聚类方法（如黎曼径向分布混合模型）表达能力有限，无法捕捉各向异性的子群体结构，缺乏更灵活、更具表达力的聚类模型以及相应的理论保证。

Method: 在黎曼齐次空间上定义MGFA，每个混合成分采用测地因子分析模型。建立MLE的根号n一致性理论，提出迭代估计算法，并在球面、形状空间和双曲空间上实现。

Result: 数值实验表明MGFA在规范设定下显著优于竞争方法，在模型误设定下保持稳健；在胼胝体和左海马体形状数据集上的案例研究证明了其在2D轮廓和3D形状分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: MGFA是一种表达力更强、具有理论保证的流形值数据聚类方法，填补了黎曼径向分布混合模型的理论空白，并在实际形状分析中展示了广泛适用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces Mixtures of Geodesic Factor Analyzers (MGFA) on Riemannian homogeneous spaces. MGFA uses a geodesic factor model within each mixture component, providing greater expressiveness than mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions and enabling clustering of manifold-valued data with anisotropic subpopulations. We establish root-$n$ consistency for the MGFA maximum likelihood estimator (MLE), thereby filling a theoretical gap for mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions as a special case. We also propose an iterative estimation algorithm and implement it on spheres, shape spaces, and hyperbolic spaces. Numerical experiments show that MGFA substantially outperforms competing methods in well-specified regimes while remaining robust under model misspecification. Finally, case studies on corpus callosum and left hippocampus shape datasets demonstrate MGFA's effectiveness for both 2D contour and 3D shape analysis.

</details>


### [2] [Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Minimization Using Samples: Algorithms, Convergence Rates, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07113)
*Sumedh Gupte,Prashanth L. A.,Sanjay P. Bhat*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了优化Optimized Certainty Equivalent (OCE)风险的通用框架，通过将OCE与utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR)建立联系，给出了OCE估计器及其梯度估计器的MSE非渐近界，并基于随机梯度算法实现OCE的高效优化，应用于金融组合优化和机器学习中的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCE风险度量涵盖熵风险、均值-方差风险和CVaR平滑变体等众多广泛使用的风险度量，但其优化问题（尤其涉及无界随机变量和梯度估计）缺乏系统的理论处理。现有工作多针对特定风险度量（如CVaR）进行优化，缺少一个统一且可扩展的OCE优化框架，且对估计器的误差分析和优化算法的收敛性保证不足。

Method: (1) 建立OCE扩展至无界随机变量的充分条件，并给出OCE与UBSR之间的等价刻画；(2) 利用该刻画，基于UBSR的经典样本平均近似(SAA)构造OCE估计器，并推导其均方误差(MSE)界的非渐近表达式；(3) 基于OCE-UBSR联系推导OCE梯度的解析表达式，据此构造梯度估计器并给出其MSE非渐近界；(4) 将梯度估计器嵌入随机梯度(SG)算法以优化OCE，并推导算法的非渐近收敛率；(5) 通过三个实验（组合优化、不确定性量化）验证算法有效性。

Result: 理论上给出了OCE估计器和梯度估计器在MSE意义上的非渐近界，以及随机梯度算法的收敛率保证。实验上在投资组合优化和不确定性量化任务中验证了所提出OCE优化算法的有效性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文为OCE风险优化提供了一套完整的理论-算法-实验体系：通过OCE与UBSR的联系简化了估计与求导过程，并提供了严格的非渐近误差与收敛性保证，从而为金融风险管理与机器学习不确定性量化中的风险敏感优化提供了通用且可靠的工具。

Abstract: We consider the optimization of the Optimized Certainty Equivalent (OCE) risk, with applications including portfolio optimization in finance, and uncertainty quantification, classification, and regression in machine learning. Our contributions cover popular special cases of OCE, such as entropic risk, mean-variance risk, and smooth variants of Conditional Value-at-Risk. Our treatment sets out the conditions that facilitate the extension of OCE to unbounded r.v.s.. We provide a useful characterization of OCE that links OCE to utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR). Our characterization enables us to form an OCE estimator from the classic sample-average approximation (SAA) of UBSR. We derive mean-squared error (MSE) bounds for our proposed OCE estimator. For OCE optimization, we first derive an expression for the OCE gradient using the characterization linking OCE to UBSR. This expression serves as the basis for a gradient estimator for the OCE. We derive non-asymptotic bounds on the MSE for the proposed OCE gradient estimator. We incorporate the aforementioned gradient estimator into a stochastic gradient (SG) algorithm to optimize OCE and quantify its convergence rate using non-asymptotic bounds that we derive. Finally, we present three experiments that use our OCE optimization algorithm to solve portfolio optimization and uncertainty quantification problems.

</details>

### [3] [Predictive beam-lattice reduction for higher-order topological modes in a 2D SSH phononic crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06395)
*Eloi Perez Compte,Michele Brun,Giorgio Carta,Nicola M. Pugno,Antonio S. Gliozzi,Federico Bosia*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于欧拉-伯努利梁理论的二维拓扑声子晶体简化力学模型，包含12个自由度的厄米动力学矩阵，保留面内平移、旋转和韧带偏心效应。通过二聚化韧带宽度构建机械2D SSH晶格，实现全带隙和量子化体极化，并通过带状/有限胞计算、有限元模拟和3D打印实验验证了边缘与角态的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量质量-弹簧SSH模型无法捕捉声子晶体中旋转和韧带偏心率等关键效应，而全有限元模拟计算代价高昂且难以解释。作者希望开发一个介于两者之间的、力学上忠实的简化模型，既保留足够的物理细节又保持计算高效性和可解释性，为拓扑弹性超材料设计提供验验证的路线。

Method: 利用欧拉-伯努利梁理论，将刚性方质量块通过细长弹性韧带连接，推导12自由度厄米动力学矩阵（含面内平移、旋转和韧带偏心）。通过二聚化韧带宽度构造机械2D SSH晶格，用带状结构和有限胞计算分析带隙内的边缘态和角态，并用定位度量量化，最终通过有限元模拟和3D打印实验验证。

Result: 模型成功实现全带隙和量子化体极化，二聚化符号控制平凡/非平凡相变，韧带偏心提供额外的纯几何拓扑调控机制。有限元模拟确认了带隙内的边缘和角态，3D打印实验显示非平凡结构在预测带隙内具有增强的边界/角响应，而平凡结构表现出衰减响应。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种经过验证的拓扑弹性超材料设计路线，使用基于连续介质启发的高效离散模型，替代理想化质量-弹簧网络或暴力数值优化，兼顾物理保真度和计算可及性。

Abstract: We develop a mechanically faithful reduced model for a two-dimensional topological phononic crystal composed of rigid square masses connected by slender elastic ligaments. Exploiting Euler Bernoulli beam theory, we derive a Hermitian 12 degree of freedom dynamical matrix that retains in-plane translations, rotations, and ligament eccentricity. This reduction captures effects that are absent from scalar mass spring SSH models while remaining computationally much more tractable and more easily interpretable than full finite element simulations. Dimerizing the ligament widths produces a mechanical 2D SSH lattice with a full band gap and a quantized bulk polarization. The sign of the dimerization controls the transition from trivial to non trivial phases, while ligament eccentricity provides an additional purely geometric mechanism for changing the topology. Ribbon and finite cell calculations predict in gap edge and corner modes, quantified by localization measures and confirmed by finite element simulations. Measurements on 3D printed samples show an evanescent response in the trivial structure and enhanced boundary/corner response in the non trivial structure within the predicted gap. The results provide a validated route for designing topological elastic metamaterials using a continuum informed discrete model rather than either idealized mass spring networks or brute force numerical optimization.

</details>


### [4] [Adjoint shape optimization of oscillatory rarefied gas flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06910)
*Pengshuo Li,Lei Wu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种快速收敛且渐进保持的伴随形状优化方法，用于振动微机电系统（MEMS）中多尺度气体流动的减阻设计。通过宏观合成方程加速玻尔兹曼动力学方程的收敛，Fourier稳定性分析表明谱半径小于0.5，在振荡圆柱和梳状谐振器上的数值实验验证了伴随灵敏度的精度和优异的减阻性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振动MEMS中气体流动涉及从连续介质到稀薄气体的多尺度问题，传统动力学迭代方法收敛速度慢，难以高效进行形状优化减阻设计，需要一种既能保持动力学精度又具备快速收敛特性的优化方法。

Method: 提出一种渐进保持的伴随形状优化方法：利用包含连续极限项和高阶动力学修正项的宏观合成方程加速玻尔兹曼方程收敛，并在此基础上推导伴随灵敏度。采用Fourier稳定性分析验证迭代收敛性质，并通过振荡圆柱和梳状谐振器数值模拟验证方法性能。

Result: Fourier稳定性分析显示谱半径小于0.5，即每次迭代数值偏差减半。数值实验表明该方法在不同Knudsen数和Strouhal数下均具有高精度的伴随灵敏度和优异的减阻性能，与传统动力学迭代方法相比，可在数十次迭代内获得收敛的原问题和对偶问题解。

Conclusion: 该方法兼具近连续极限一致性和稀薄流区域的高动力学精度，具有渐进保持特性，允许空间网格尺寸远大于分子平均自由程，从而显著提升振动MEMS的设计效率。

Abstract: A fast-converging and asymptotic-preserving adjoint shape optimization method is proposed for drag reduction of multiscale gas flows in vibrating micro-electro-mechanical systems. The convergence of the Boltzmann kinetic equation is accelerated by macroscopic synthetic equations, whose constitutive relations integrate continuum-limit terms and high-order kinetic corrections to faithfully characterize spatiotemporal rarefaction effects. As such, this method maintains near-continuum limit consistency while retaining high kinetic accuracy in rarefied flow regimes. Fourier stability analysis performed in an infinite domain demonstrates that the present method yields a spectral radius below 0.5, indicating that the numerical deviation from the converged solution is halved per iteration. Numerical simulations are conducted on an oscillating cylinder and a comb-shaped resonator. The results verify the high accuracy of the derived adjoint sensitivities and the excellent drag reduction performance of the proposed method across various Knudsen and Strouhal numbers. Compared with conventional kinetic iteration methods, the present method produces convergent primal and adjoint solutions within dozens of iterations and features asymptotic preserving behavior, permitting spatial cell sizes far larger than the molecular mean free path. This facilitates efficient design of vibrating micro-electro-mechanical systems.

</details>

### [5] [Mean field error estimate of the random batch method for vortex blob dynamics for the 2D Navier--Stokes Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06533)
*Zhenyu Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维Navier-Stokes方程的涡度形式，提出了随机批次涡团法（random batch vortex blob method），将粒子系统的计算复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N)，并给出了量化的平均场误差估计，其依赖涡团半径的部分保持代数阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡团法是求解二维Navier-Stokes方程的重要数值方法，但其基于N体相互作用的计算复杂度为O(N²)，难以处理大规模问题。作者希望引入随机批次方法降低计算复杂度，同时建立严格的定量误差估计，特别是对涡团半径保持代数阶依赖，避免指数级误差放大。

Method: 作者将两种主要误差机制分开处理：(1) 随机批次误差通过局部耦合辅助划分、对称律比较和Fisher信息耗散来控制；(2) 平均场涨落利用Biot-Savart核的奇性和无散结构进行估计。对光滑、严格正初始涡度，证明了归一化相对熵界的有限时间估计。

Result: 在任意有限时间区间上，得到归一化相对熵界 O(ε⁻⁴τ² + N⁻¹)，其中常数独立于粒子数N、批次刷新间隔τ和涡团半径ε。由此可得，当N→∞且ε⁻²τ→0时，固定粒子边际分布在L¹意义下强收敛到正则化涡度解的张量积。

Conclusion: 随机批次涡团法成功将计算复杂度降为O(N)，同时保持了代数阶的误差依赖关系，为大规模二维流体模拟提供了理论保障。随机批次误差与平均场涨落两类误差的分离分析是该工作的核心贡献。

Abstract: We propose and analyze the random batch vortex blob method for the 2D Navier--Stokes equation in vorticity form on the whole plane. The vortex blob method is based on an interacting particle system of $N$ particles with computational complexity of $O(N^2)$, which is reduced to $O(N)$ by the random batch method \cite{JinLiLiu2020}. Our main result is a quantitative law level mean field error estimate whose dependence on the blob radius remains algebraic. We treat the two main error mechanisms separately. The random batch error is controlled through a locally coupled auxiliary partition, a symmetric law comparison, and Fisher-information dissipation. The mean field fluctuation is estimated by exploiting the oddness and divergence-free structure of the Biot--Savart kernel. For smooth, strictly positive initial vorticity, we prove on every finite time interval a normalized relative-entropy bound of order $ O\!\left(\varepsilon^{-4}τ^2+N^{-1} \right), $ with constants independent of $N$, $τ$, and $varepsilon$. Here $τ$ is the batch refreshing interval and $\varepsilon$ is the blob radius. As a consequence, the fixed-particle marginals converge strongly in $L^1$ to tensor products of the regularized vorticity solution when $N\to\infty$ and $\varepsilon^{-2}τ\to 0$.

</details>


### [6] [The error estimate of entropy-stable discontinuous Galerkin methods for hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06642)
*Xu-Kun Chen,Yong Liu,Chi-Wang Shu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为Chen和Shu提出的熵稳定间断Galerkin（ESDG）方法建立了严格的先验误差估计，填补了此前仅停留在截断误差级分析的空白。在多项式重构假设和L∞先验界下，证明该方法在基于求积的范数下达到O(h^k)的误差阶，并进一步将该框架推广到熵稳定无振荡DG（ESOFDG）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chen和Shu（2017）提出的统一框架能够构造满足任意熵不等式的熵稳定DG方法，但其误差分析仅停留在截断误差水平，缺乏严格的收敛性理论，这一关键缺口阻碍了熵稳定格式的理论完备性。

Method: 采用有限差分类型的相容性-稳定性论证，直接在节点层面进行分析。该方法适用于一般非结构网格上的标量方程和方程系统，基于多项式重构假设和L∞先验界给出误差估计。

Result: 在多项式重构假设和L∞先验界下，证明了基于求积范数（与有限维重构空间上的分片L²范数等价）的O(h^k)误差估计。该框架推广到ESOFDG方法时，额外阻尼项不降低收敛阶。数值实验表明实际收敛率可能比理论预测高半阶。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了熵稳定DG方法在一般非结构网格上的严格收敛理论，弥合了熵稳定格式从启发式设计到严格误差分析的鸿沟，并为ESOFDG等改进格式提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Entropy inequalities are fundamental to the well-posedness of hyperbolic conservation laws, providing the essential criterion for selecting the physically admissible solution among infinitely many weak solutions. Chen and Shu [J. Comput. Phys. 345 (2017)] proposed a unified framework for constructing high-order discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods that satisfy entropy inequalities for any given entropy via specific numerical quadrature; however, their accompanying error analysis was limited to the truncation error level, leaving a critical gap in the rigorous convergence theory for these entropy-stable schemes. This paper closes that gap by establishing rigorous a priori error estimates for semi-discrete entropy-stable DG (ESDG) methods on general unstructured meshes for hyperbolic conservation laws. The analysis applies to both scalar equations and systems, and is built upon a finite-difference-type consistency-stability argument carried out directly at the nodal level. Under a polynomial-reconstruction hypothesis and an $L^\infty$ a priori bound, we prove an $O(h^k)$ error estimate in a quadrature-based norm, which is equivalent to the broken $L^2$ norm on the finite-dimensional reconstruction space. We further extend this framework to the entropy-stable oscillation-free DG (ESOFDG) method introduced by Liu, Lu, and Shu [SIAM J. Sci. Comput. 46 (2024)], demonstrating that the additional damping terms do not degrade the convergence order. Numerical experiments suggest that the observed convergence rates may exceed the theoretical prediction by up to half an order.

</details>


### [7] [CT-PIKAN: Coordinate-Transformed Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Network with Autograd-Based Metric Evaluation for Solving PDEs in Curvilinear Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06660)
*Mohammad E. Heravifard,Kazem Hejranfar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出坐标变换物理信息Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（CT-PIKAN），通过坐标变换将任意形状的物理区域映射为规则计算域，并利用自动微分计算雅可比矩阵、度量张量和变换后的微分算子，从而在弯曲坐标系下高效求解偏微分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIKAN方法主要针对笛卡尔域设计，无法自然处理弯曲/曲线域带来的几何复杂性。传统变换PINN方法需要手动推导度量系数，实现繁琐且灵活性差，因此迫切需要一种几何感知的PIKAN框架来求解任意形状域上的PDE。

Method: CT-PIKAN首先构建基于B样条的数据无关PIKAN用于验证，然后通过光滑坐标映射将物理域映射到规则计算域，在物理信息损失函数中直接施加变换后的控制方程。利用自动微分从坐标映射直接计算雅可比矩阵、度量张量和变换后的微分算子，避免手动推导度量系数。

Result: 在二维对流方程上验证了基础PIKAN框架，随后在极坐标和波浪弯曲坐标下分别对Poisson方程（椭圆型）、热方程（抛物型）和对流方程（双曲型）等代表性基准问题进行评估，证明CT-PIKAN能在复杂域上获得高效精确的PDE解。

Conclusion: CT-PIKAN提供了一种将微分几何与PIKAN相结合的通用且可扩展的方法论，能够高效、准确地求解复杂区域上的偏微分方程，消除手动推导度量系数的负担。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks have recently emerged as an effective class of neural solvers for partial differential equations, combining the expressive power of spline-based Kolmogorov-Arnold representations with physics-informed learning. However, existing PIKAN formulations are primarily developed for Cartesian domains and cannot naturally accommodate the geometric complexity introduced by curvilinear domains. In this work, we propose Coordinate-Transformed Physics-Informed Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (CT-PIKAN), a geometry-aware framework for solving PDEs on arbitrarily shaped domains through coordinate transformation. A smooth mapping transforms the physical domain into a regular computational domain, while the transformed governing equations are enforced directly within the physics-informed loss. Unlike conventional transformed PINN approaches that require manually derived metric coefficients, CT-PIKAN employs automatic differentiation to evaluate Jacobians, metric tensors, and transformed differential operators directly from the coordinate mapping, eliminating analytical derivations and improving implementation flexibility. To establish the proposed framework, a data-free B-spline-based PIKAN is first constructed and validated on the two-dimensional advection equation. The CT-PIKAN methodology is subsequently assessed on representative elliptic, parabolic, and hyperbolic benchmark problems, including the Poisson, heat, and advection equations formulated in polar and wavy curvilinear coordinates. The proposed framework provides a general and extensible methodology for integrating differential geometry with physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, enabling efficient and accurate PDE solutions on complex domains.

</details>


### [8] [A model and a finite element approximation of the mixed-dimensionality diffusion problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06976)

If $\underline h$ denotes the antipodal separation distance of the network parameters, then \begin{equation*} \|v_n\|_{\mathcal H^r(\mathbb S^d)}\lesssim\underline h^{-(r-s)}\|v_n\|_{\mathcal H^s(\mathbb S^d)},\qquad 0\leq s<r<k+\tfrac12. \end{equation*}

Abstract: We propose a mesh-based filtering approach to alleviate the severe timestep restrictions arising in explicit Runge--Kutta discontinuous Galerkin (RKDG) methods formulated in spherical-polar coordinates. This equivalence implies that the filtered method inherits the accuracy and stability properties of the corresponding RKDG discretization on the merged mesh. We apply the mesh-based filter to an existing RKDG method for the Euler equations in spherical-polar coordinates and demonstrate, through selected two- and three-dimensional examples, its effectiveness in accelerating simulations through significantly larger stable timesteps.

Abstract: The Walk-on-Spheres algorithm, introduced by M. E. We also design an efficient numerical scheme, based on finite elements, to approximate the model, proving its well-posedness and stability both for regular and singular potentials. In dimensions $d=2,3$ with singular potential, the convergence analysis of the finite element approximation proves the local-in-time existence and uniqueness of weak solutions satisfying the physical constraint $φ\in (-1, 1)$. Abstract: In this work, we formally investigate the dealiasing properties of split form discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretizations for the one-dimensional Burgers problem. By generalizing the proof of Blaisdell et al. for spectral discretizations, we show that split form DG schemes achieve dealiasing in the weakly underresolved range through integration error cancellation on the conservative and non-conservative forms. As a corollary, we identify quadrature- and order-dependent pairs of splitting coefficients that eliminate the dominant component of the aliasing error. While these ``optimized'' splitting coefficients minimize integration errors on the numerical scheme, we show that the skew-symmetric aliasing pattern resulting from the entropy stable split is required to maintain long-term stability of the numerical solution. The proposed framework provides an alternative proof for the entropy stability of the DG discretization of the (1/3, 2/3) split formulation introduced by Gassner which clarifies the connection between DG aliasing errors and entropy stability. Finally, we also show that the entropy stable split is associated with lower aliasing errors in the weakly underresolved range compared to the conservative form.

</details>

Abstract: A scientific theory of deep learning, comprising learning dynamics and statistical properties of learned models, is rapidly gaining attention. One of the corner stones of this development are analytically solvable toy models, allowing for the fully tractable analysis of the learning dynamics. Here we analytically investigate such a toy model using the regularization strength as a tunable external parameter - akin to external fields in statistical physics. In previous studies, (i) an onset of learning transition was predicted analytically and (ii) it was phenomenologically/numerically established that tuning the regularization strength can result in a cascade of phase transitions. The number of those transitions was linked to the geometry of the loss landscape determined by the model complexity. Setting up a rigorous framework underpinning the previous numerical observations, our investigation reveals a precise connection between those cascades of phase transitions, learnable features and the underlying geometry. We provide analytic predictions of these phase transitions as well as tractable order parameters related to learned features. At the level of the minimal model, we connect this macroscopic perspective (that can be condensed into an effective description) to the microscopic perspective in terms of the geometry of the loss landscape characterized by the Hessian spectrum. Thus, the presented model provides a platform to explore and sharpen advances made in the scientific theory of deep learning rooted in statistical physics concepts.

</details>

Abstract: In numerous physical, chemical, and biological systems the dynamics can be reduced to the motion of state variables in a complex potential landscape. In case the manifold is known, the motion and response of the embedded object can be described deterministically up to stochastic effects usually associated with a noise. In contrast, if the manifold is unknown, the static and dynamic response of the state variable may be used as a spectroscopic tool to characterize the potential landscape. Inspired by a seminal work of L.\ Embon and co-workers, [Sci.\ Rep.\ \textbf{5}, 7598 (2015)] we investigate the statistical properties of potential minima, in particular, their stability to thermal and quantum activation. For Gaussian random manifolds, we derive an algebraic expression to evaluate the statistical probability of the potential character (value, slope, curvature, ...). With this tool, we compute the expectation value for the rate of thermal and quantum activation and link these findings to the principal characteristics of the Gaussian potential, i.e., its Green's function. This link provides the opportunity to access information on the potential's Green's function by studying the activation behavior of an object in this manifold.

Abstract: We aim at calculating an explicit expression for the finite-size probability distribution of the radius of gyration $R$ of an Interacting Self-Avoiding Walk (ISAW), as a function of chain length $N$ and monomer-monomer interaction energy $\varepsilon$. We first derive the explicit free energy expression for a non-interacting Self-Avoiding Walk, introducing a new natural scale variable $t = ρ^g$ expressed as a power of the density $ρ$. Then, thanks to a cumulant expansion approach introduced by Lhuillier, Victor and coworkers, we extend it to the interacting case, capturing both the coil-globule transition and finite-size corrections to scaling, including entropic and surface-energy contributions. The radius of gyration distribution determined by the new free energy expression is then compared, using Bayesian inference to estimate the model parameters, to results from extensive Monte Carlo simulations of three-dimensional ISAWs, showing excellent agreement except very close to the $Θ$-point, where finite chain lengths limit the accessible scaling regime. This validates our resulting free energy expression and allows us, as an application, to construct a phase diagram of the coil-globule transition.

Abstract: Animals inhabiting diverse environments by moving across different spatial scales, from insects to birds, marine predators, mammals and even humans, often display apparently random movement paths. Over the past decade, novel biologging technologies have recorded these patterns in increasing detail, generating a wealth of experimental data. A central challenge is to understand such complex patterns by constructing data-driven mathematical models. Many animal movements depart from Brownian motion, as described by correlated random walks, Lévy walks, or active particle dynamics. Yet, these movement models do not incorporate long-term non-Markovian memory extracted from experimental trajectories. Here, we construct a stochastic generalised Langevin equation from satellite tracking data for loggerhead sea turtles (Caretta caretta) foraging off the coast of West Africa. We find that these turtles exhibit active movement characterised by large-scale loops that are not explained by ocean currents or chirality. These loops maintain movement within a specific foraging region and, over intermediate timescales, generate superdiffusion similar to Lévy walks. We thus identify a loop-based form of active anomalous search related to foraging patterns observed across a wide range of animal species, which may inspire robotic search strategies and AI-based metaheuristic optimisation algorithms.

</details>

Abstract: Generating entanglement rapidly and reliably is essential for quantum information processing, communication, and metrology. Dissipative preparation is attractive because engineered reservoirs robustly drive a system toward an entangled target, yet relaxation can carry a substantial time cost. Here we formulate the entanglement Mpemba effect, whereby an initially less entangled state overtakes a more entangled state under the same open-system dynamics. This effect turns initial-state engineering into a route for faster preparation without altering the dissipative protocol. We derive a general criterion for the reversal from the relaxation spectrum, applicable even when entanglement evolves nonmonotonically. A reversal of deterministic local operations and classical communication (LOCC)-reachability preorder provides a measure-independent certificate of reversed entanglement order. Exactly solvable models show that initial-state selection can substantially shorten the time required to reach high entanglement. We further propose an experimentally relevant trapped-ion protocol that can realize the entanglement Mpemba effect.

</details>

Abstract: For any $(p,q)$, we proved the existence of a rational unicuspidal curve with one Puiseux pair $(p,q)$ in an algebraic surface, with a self-intersection which realizes the upper bound $m_{p,q}$ conjectured by the first-named author in \cite{C}, thus strengthening the symplectic version of the result in \cite{C}. These ``optimal" curves were obtained by examining two infinite families of rational bicuspidal curves, one in $CP^2$ and one in $CP^1\times CP^1$. In order to facilitate the computations, we derived certain recursive identities, and as a byproduct, we obtained a new formula for the bound $m_{p,q}$, which is more amenable to computations and gives us better insight concerning the nature of the bound. As a byproduct of this investigation, a formula for the last entry of the multiplicity sequence of the singularity was also found.

Abstract: We study the Fujita phenomenon for semilinear heat inequalities generated by variable-speed Laplacians on infinite weighted graphs. Assuming that the graph carries a proper adapted path metric, we establish an integral volume-growth criterion forcing every nonnegative global classical supersolution on the open cylinder $(0,\infty)\times V$ to vanish, without assuming an initial value or trace. We prove that a nontrivial supersolution of this kind exists if and only if the equation has a positive global Cauchy solution for some nonzero point-source datum. A complementary heat-kernel construction gives global Cauchy solutions for all sufficiently small point-source data when the same volume integral converges and a matching anchored heat-kernel upper bound is available. This proves sharpness on integer lattices and on a family of logarithmically perturbed weighted half-lines; in the latter examples, even the exponent of an iterated logarithm can determine the existence--nonexistence alternative. A finer nonexistence criterion couples intrinsic volume growth with the capacity of intrinsic annuli. Its proof combines parabolic testing, a Laplace--resolvent reduction, and a pathwise decomposition of resolvent currents. The nonexistence results require no volume-doubling property, Poincaré inequality, heat-kernel bound, or stochastic completeness.

Independently, for every $n\ge2$ and $p\ge1$ we establish global height and Lorentz-factor bounds that remain valid in the critical and supercritical regimes. In dimension two, a logarithmic-capacity argument completes the proof. For $n\ge3$, we combine two weighted trace-free tensor identities whose quadratic form is coercive exactly in the subcritical range and degenerates at the Sobolev exponent. This extends radial nonexistence to arbitrary entire solutions and yields a geometric half-space rigidity theorem for complete spacelike hypersurfaces.

</details>

*Jungang Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Siegel域上临界Geller方程，通过Cayley共轭将其与复双曲空间上的Brezis-Nirenberg方程联系起来，完整确定了最优Sobolev常数、所有等号情形、非负有限能量解的分类、无辅助集中中心的剖面对分解以及Bianchi-Egnell稳定性不等式。对于吸引性Hardy微扰，构造了光滑正的最小作用解并证明了全局Palais-Smale紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界Geller方程是Siegel域上U(ℓ)-不变的Folland-Stein临界方程，可通过Cayley共轭与复双曲空间上的Brezis-Nirenberg方程对应。作者旨在系统建立该方程的理论框架，包括最优常数、解的完全分类、紧性与稳定性分析，并推广到带Hardy微扰或排斥势的情形。

Method: 主要方法包括：(1) 利用Cayley共轭建立与Brezis-Nirenberg方程的等价性；(2) 使用不变扇区中的形域识别、法切片唯一性（非移动平面法）证明对称性；(3) 通过Busemann重心-尺度连接论证构造正解；(4) 利用谱分析计算精确的Hessian-能量比和法向形变。

Result: 确定了最优Sobolev常数与全部等号情形；固定扇区的最优商严格低于局部和两气泡阈值且可达；气泡在不变扇区非退化，法向Hessian-能量比为2/(n+ℓ+4)。对ℓ>1及0<λ<(ℓ-1)²的Hardy微扰，构造了光滑正最小作用解并证明全局PS紧性与精确作用量子；小耦合下正规化极小元构成单一全纯等距轨道，基态模全纯等距非退化。对小排斥势，通过链接论证得到作用介于一个和两个量子之间的正解。

Conclusion: 本文系统完成了Siegel域上临界Geller方程及其Hardy微扰的完整分析，建立了与复双曲空间上Brezis-Nirenberg方程的深层联系，给出了最优常数、解的完全分类、紧性、稳定性及对称性等核心结果，其方法（如无移动平面的对称性证明）具有方法论上的创新意义。

Abstract: For each integer $\ell\geq 1$, the critical Geller equation on the Siegel domain $\mathcal U=\mathcal H^n\times(0,\infty)$ is the $U(\ell)$-invariant sector of the critical Folland--Stein equation on $\mathcal H^{n+\ell}$, and its smooth equation is exactly Cayley-conjugate to a Brezis--Nirenberg equation on complex hyperbolic space $\mathbb H_{\mathbb C}^{n+1}$. For $\ell>1$, the conjugation also identifies the completed form domains. We determine the sharp Sobolev constant and all equality cases, classify nonnegative finite-energy solutions, prove a profile decomposition with no auxiliary concentration center, and establish a Bianchi--Egnell stability inequality. Its optimal fixed-sector quotient is strictly below both the local and two-bubble thresholds and is attained. The bubble is nondegenerate in the invariant sector, and the exact normal Hessian-to-energy ratio is $2/(n+\ell+4)$. For $\ell>1$ and an attractive Hardy perturbation in the coercive range $0<λ<(\ell-1)^2$, we construct a smooth positive least-action solution and prove global Palais--Smale compactness with an exact action quantum. At sufficiently small coupling, the positive normalized minimizers form a single orbit under holomorphic isometries, every positive intrinsic minimizer is geodesically radial, and the ground state is nondegenerate modulo holomorphic isometries. The symmetry follows from equivariant normal-slice uniqueness, without a moving-plane argument. We also determine the first normal deformation and the second-order expansions of the sharp quotient and action quantum. Finally, for a small repulsive potential, a Busemann barycenter--scale linking argument yields a positive solution with action strictly between one and two quanta.

</details>

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对对数拉普拉斯算子建立了Schauder型估计，证明了当非齐次项为κ-Hölder连续时，内解也具有κ-Hölder正则性（实际上略高于κ-Hölder，带对数修正），并证明了非负解的Harnack不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数拉普拉斯算子（logarithmic Laplacian）是一类非局部算子，在分数阶偏微分方程和相关领域中具有重要应用。此前对该算子的正则性理论尚缺乏系统的Schauder型估计和Harnack不等式，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过对对数拉普拉斯算子的核函数和积分算子性质进行精细分析，利用势论和拟微分算子技术建立解的逐点估计，从而推导出内正则性结果；同时通过构造适当的比较函数和barrier方法证明Harnack不等式。

Result: 建立了对数拉普拉斯算子的Schauder型估计：κ-Hölder连续的非齐次项保证内解为κ-Hölder连续，且正则性可略高于κ阶（含对数修正项）；此外证明了非负解的Harnack不等式成立。

Conclusion: 本文为对数拉普拉斯算子的正则性理论奠定了重要基础，所得估计和不等式可用于进一步研究该算子的边值问题、特征值问题及其他应用。

Abstract: In this article, we establish Schauder-type estimates for the logarithmic Laplacian. We show that for a $κ$-Hölder inhomogeneous term, the solution is also $κ$-Hölder in the interior. In fact, the interior regularity slightly exceeds $κ$-Hölder smoothness up to a logarithmic correction. Additionally, we prove a Harnack inequality for non-negative solutions.

</details>

### [65] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激活工程的虚假信息检测框架LaFaCt，通过在主语言模型的残差流中通过对比激活添加（CAA）原理提取"虚假信息方向"，推理时仅需将最后token的激活投影到该方向并输入MLP分类器即可完成检测，无需微调、外部知识检索或任务特定监督。在Gemma、Llama和Qwen系列的11个模型（270M到12B参数）上，该方法在LIAR和FACTors基准上达到或超过零样本/少样本提示基线，在小模型上增益最大，验证了真实性在大模型潜在空间中具有结构化、线性可分的几何特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测方法主要依赖表面语言特征或外部知识检索，缺乏可扩展且可解释的解决方案。作者试图探索语言模型表示空间中真实性的几何属性，寻找一种无需微调、无需外部证据检索的轻量级检测方法。

Method: 该方法基于激活工程：通过对真/假陈述的配对激活进行均值差对比（遵循CAA的difference-in-means原理），在transformer残差流中提取一个"虚假信息方向"。推理时，将未见声明的最后token激活投影到此方向上，再将投影后的表示输入一个MLP分类器进行二分类。整体流程无需微调主干模型，无需外部证据检索，仅需对比语料对来估计方向。

Result: 在Gemma、Llama、Qwen三个系列的11个模型（270M至12B参数）上，对AVeriTeC、LIAR和FACTors三个事实核查基准进行评估。虚假信息方向在各模型规模和架构族中均可恢复；最后token投影方法在LIAR和FACTors上达到或超越零样本和少样本提示基线，小模型增益最大；在AVeriTeC上性能受限，归因于其基于证据标注的方案。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明真实性在预训练语言模型的潜在空间中是一个结构化且线性可分的概念，基于可解释性的虚假信息检测可以作为检索式管道的实用补充方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [66] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应拓扑DeepONet框架，将输入函数编码从固定离散点采样推广为Hausdorff局部凸空间连续对偶中的线性泛函，结合系数空间两步过程和训练时解码器/正则化，在多个算子学习任务上验证了紧致、可解释、离散化可迁移的坐标表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DeepONet通过固定离散化上的点值编码输入函数，受限于离散化质量且坐标表示缺乏可解释性与可迁移性。Ismailov的拓扑DeepONet框架提供了理论基础，但需要进一步开发实用的测量系统、误差分析以及自适应优化方法，以支持非范数化局部凸输入空间上的算子学习。

Method: 将输入函数编码为来自Hausdorff局部凸空间(V,{p_α})连续对偶中的连续线性泛函（测量），其拓扑由点分离的半范数族而非单一范数生成；开发固定与自适应两种测量系统；将测量与Lee和Shin的系数空间两步过程结合；采用仅训练时解码器和正则化稳定自适应坐标；推导离散误差分解（区分测量误差、输出基误差和神经逼近误差）以及Barron速率细化。

Result: 在异质Darcy问题中功能模型在未见过网格上保持约5.5-5.6%的近似分辨率无关误差；受控问题中自适应测量将平均误差降至1.2%以下；固定时间Navier-Stokes问题中自适应拓扑DeepONet达到1.685%±0.017%的平均相对L2误差（128个功能坐标），优于其他DeepONet变体，但低于FNO的0.832%±0.172%（FNO需完整64x64输入场、两倍训练时间和10.7倍峰值GPU内存）。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在连续对偶V'中构造紧致、可解释且离散化可移植的坐标表示是可行的，即使对于非范数化输入空间也能有效。虽然自适应拓扑DeepONet在精度上不及同等规模FNO，但在内存效率和输入紧凑性方面具有显著优势，为算子学习的坐标设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep Operator Networks (DeepONets; arXiv:1910.03193) typically encode an input function through point values on a fixed discretization. Building on the Topological DeepONet framework of Ismailov (arXiv:2603.11972), we replace point samples by continuous linear functionals drawn from the continuous dual of a Hausdorff locally convex space $({V},\{p_α\}_{α\in A})$, whose topology is generated by a point-separating family of seminorms rather than a single norm, and develop fixed and adaptive functional measurement systems. Measurements are combined with the coefficient-space Two-Step procedure of Lee and Shin (arXiv:2309.01020), while a training-only decoder and regularization stabilize the adaptive coordinates. We derive a discrete error decomposition separating measurement, output-basis, and neural-approximation errors, together with a Barron-rate refinement. The framework is evaluated on the antiderivative operator, a non-normable locally convex input space, heterogeneous Darcy flow, a controlled operator, and fixed-time and time-evolving Navier-Stokes vorticity operators. In the heterogeneous Darcy problem, the functional models retain nearly resolution-independent errors of 5.5-5.6% on unseen grids, while in the controlled problem adaptive measurements reduce the mean error below 1.2%. For the fixed-time Navier-Stokes problem, the Adaptive Topological DeepONet is the most accurate DeepONet-based model, attaining a mean relative $L^2$ error of 1.685% +/- 0.017% using 128 functional coordinates. A comparably sized Fourier neural operator (FNO; arXiv:2010.08895) achieves the lower error 0.832% +/- 0.172%, but requires the full 64x64 input field, twice the training time, and 10.7x greater peak GPU memory. The formulation provides compact, interpretable, and discretization-portable coordinates in the continuous dual $V'$, including for non-normable input spaces.

</details>


### [67] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于人工生命的捕食者-猎物觅食模型，研究在噪声感知条件下的决策问题。实验表明，盲目信任感知标签在噪声增加时会导致灾难性失败，而不确定性感知策略能显著提升生存率并减少致命错误，且行为会随不确定性从探索性转向保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统的感知本质上是含噪声的，生物需要在不确定性下做出决策，而错误分类可能代价高昂甚至致命。现有的噪声标签学习研究多聚焦于算法层面，缺乏从生物/人工生命视角探索不确定性感知决策机制的具身化、可解释的模型。

Method: 构建了一个人工生命捕食者-猎物觅食模型，在对称和非对称两类感知噪声条件下进行受控实验，比较多种考虑噪声预测的策略（包括盲目信任标签的策略和不确定性感知策略）下智能体的表现，并分析行为模式的定性转变。

Result: 盲目信任感知标签会在噪声增大时导致灾难性失败；不确定性感知策略显著提升生存率并减少致命错误；观察到了从探索性到保守性策略的定性行为模式转变（相变）。

Conclusion: 模型将风险敏感觅食、生态信息使用与人工生命联系起来，表明在感知不可靠时显式的信息收集能提升鲁棒性，为噪声标签鲁棒学习提供了可解释的人工生命类比，强调了不确定性感知决策的重要性。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [68] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现给LLM裁判（judge）更多计算资源并不会必然使其检查更多需求：当单次调用需要返回多个判定时，部分决策缺少充分证据支撑。研究发现分片（sharding）——将需求分组并各分配独立调用的方法——能有效缓解此问题，甚至在总预算固定时，分片的弱裁判可媲美甚至超越更强大的整体裁判。分片还对对抗性攻击具有鲁棒性，但对针对每个判据单独说服的攻防需结合辩论式（debate-style）对抗才更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型监督（model-based oversight）中，LLM裁判需要对大量需求逐项判断通过与否。现有做法常将所有需求塞入单次调用并分配大量token或工具预算，但作者观察到：判定数量增多时，裁判输出质量随每个调用判定数量增加而下降，即使输入是同一份证据。这一现象广泛存在于专家标注的研究复现、法律工作和临床试验评估中。作者希望找到一种在不增加总预算的前提下提升裁判可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出"分片"（sharding）干预策略：将需检查的需求划分为若干较小分组，每组分配一次独立的LLM调用，最后汇总各调用的判定结果。实验设置包括：对比单次完整调用（拥有面板总预算）与多次分片调用（总预算相同）；使用专家标注的科研复现、法律文档和临床试验评估数据集进行协议一致性测评；还设计了对抗性实验，考察best-of-N对手通过仅改变表述方式（不改变实际工作）使超载裁判错误接受未满足标准的程度。

Result: 分片显著提升了与专家判定的一致性，且在模型、证据、总预算和单决策预算均固定的前提下优于单一整体调用。更关键的是：分片的弱裁判可以超越能力更强的整体裁判，甚至在后者获得全部面板预算时也能与之匹敌。对抗实验中，best-of-N对手可在超载裁判中将未满足标准的错误接受率提高数倍；分片在降低基线错误的同时消除了这一对抗性优势。但针对每个判据分别说服的对抗性攻击，分片单独无法抵御，需要叠加辩论式对抗（debate-style opposition）才能抵抗自适应重优化。

Conclusion: 在模型监督场景下，"更多计算"不等于"更好的判定"；需求数量驱动的裁判过载（overload）是系统性失败模式。分片是一种简单高效、无需增加总体计算预算的干预，既提升了对齐一致性又增强了对抗鲁棒性。然而分片并非万能：对逐项说服型攻击，需以辩论式对抗作为补充。研究为LLM评估管线设计提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [69] [Robust Average-Reward Markov Decision Processes: Minimax-Optimal Learning via Plug-in Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06545)
*Yuepeng Yang,Yuxin Chen,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（DRMDP）在平均奖励准则下的样本复杂度，识别出σH₀作为高低容忍度区域的扰动尺度分界，并给出了匹配的上下界极小极大样本复杂度，同时提出了两种基于归约的plug-in算法（已知跨度参数的span-informed和从数据校准的span-agnostic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒MDP为模型不确定性下的序贯决策提供了原则性框架，但在平均奖励准则下，学习ε-最优鲁棒策略所需的最小样本量尚不明确。理解该问题的极小极大样本复杂度对于实际部署鲁棒决策算法至关重要。

Method: 作者建立了匹配的上下界分析，将样本复杂度刻画为关于σH₀的分段函数。在算法层面，提出了归约式plug-in方法：span-informed过程利用已知跨度参数选择归约方式（名义或鲁棒）及折扣因子；span-agnostic过程则从数据中同时校准这两个选择。复杂度由线性跨度项（类似名义AMDP结果）和仅在低容忍度区域出现的鲁棒特定项组成。

Result: 极小极大总样本复杂度（忽略对数因子）为：在高容忍度区域（ε≳σH₀）时为SA·min{H₀,Hσ}/ε²，在低容忍度区域（ε≲σH₀）时为SA·(min{H₀,Hσ}+σHσ²)/ε²。上下界匹配，证明了该复杂度是最优的。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了平均奖励准则下分布鲁棒MDP的样本复杂度，揭示了σH₀作为分界阈值的关键作用，并提供了达到最优速率的两类实用算法，为鲁棒强化学习中的样本效率分析建立了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Distributionally robust Markov decision processes provide a principled framework for sequential decision making under model uncertainty. We study how many samples are necessary and sufficient to learn an $\varepsilon$-optimal robust policy under the average-reward criterion. A generative model provides samples from the nominal transition kernel, whereas policy performance is evaluated over $(s,a)$-rectangular total-variation uncertainty sets of radius at most $σ$.
  Let $H_0$ and $H_σ$ denote the nominal and robust optimal bias spans, respectively. We identify $σH_0$ as the perturbation scale separating high- and low-tolerance regimes. Our matching upper and lower bounds show that, up to logarithmic factors, the minimax total sample complexity is $$ NSA \asymp \frac{SA}{\varepsilon^2}\begin{cases} \min\{H_0,H_σ\}, & \varepsilon\gtrsimσH_0,\\ \min\{H_0,H_σ\}+σH_σ^2, & \varepsilon\lesssimσH_0. \end{cases} $$ Here $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, and $N$ is the number of samples per state-action pair. The sample complexity consists of a linear-span term that resembles the nominal AMDP results and a robustness-specific term that appears only in the low-tolerance regime. We attain these rates using reduction-based plug-in procedures that select the reduction---nominal or robust---and its discount factor: a span-informed procedure that makes these choices using known span parameters, and a span-agnostic procedure that calibrates both choices from data.

</details>


### [70] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗式因果干预证伪（ACIF）框架，在结构因果生成器与对抗性实验者之间的序贯博弈中，通过干预分布而非仅观测分布来识别因果关系，并给出了均衡存在性、有限样本收敛保证及线性高斯示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能在匹配观测分布的同时编码错误的因果结构，现有对抗生成框架的判别器仅区分真实与合成样本，无法判别因果有效性。需要一种能通过干预来主动证伪候选因果模型的框架。

Method: 将结构因果生成器与对抗性实验者建模为序贯博弈：生成器提出观测分布和干预分布，实验者选择干预以最大程度证伪生成器。提出ACIF的oracle与可实现版本，将对抗目标精确化简为最坏干预积分概率度量（IPM），并区分观测拟合、干预等价性与结构因果模型点识别三个概念。

Result: 对有限模型和干预类证明：（i）对抗目标可精确化简为最坏干预IPM；（ii）在分离性干预族下可实现到干预等价性的识别及点识别；（iii）混合策略均衡存在；（iv）有限样本一致收敛和基于间隔的模型选择保证；（v）在平衡分离条件下，基于分歧的序贯设计具有对数消除保证。另给出完整线性高斯示例，单个精心选择的干预即可分离两个观测不可区分的因果方向。

Conclusion: 该框架明确了对抗式因果判别器能够和不能证明的内容，为因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计之间提供了原则性桥梁。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [71] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDecVAEs框架，用于多模态可穿戴计算中的解耦表示学习，支持任意数量模态的全栈处理（分类、解耦表示、融合与生成），在高达30个模态的HAR任务中实现准确率与生成质量的全面提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态可穿戴计算需要学习解耦表示以开发通用可持续模型，但现有方法无法作为全栈可穿戴处理器，即不能同时满足任务分类性能、解耦可解释表示学习、多模态融合及异构时序数据的生成建模。

Method: OmniDecVAEs扩展DecVAEs，通过多视图自监督分解损失学习模态条件下的时频潜在子空间，并采用共享非对称自编码器（AE）架构，以统一可扩展的方式高效学习多用途表示。

Result: 在多达30个模态的Omni-modal HAR设定下，相比Transformer和VAE方法，活动识别准确率提升1.01%，身份识别提升6.75%；重建MAE改善76.84%，真实与合成数据分布相似性MMD改善13.85%；模型仅4.1M参数，具备实时延迟。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAEs作为轻量级模型具有智能边缘可穿戴设备与临床医疗应用潜力，通过增强的表示能力、模态不变的模型规模与实时推理，在单一模型中统一了多模态处理的需求与能力。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [72] [MiGHT-EHR: A Multi-task Graph Transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06430)
*Anirudh Rayas,Yuan Wang,Pavan Turaga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MiGHT-EHR是一种多任务图Transformer框架，通过构建异构图统一建模电子健康记录（EHR）中的异构临床实体、纵向患者轨迹和跨任务统计依赖，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR学习方法仅能捕捉异构性、时间性和多任务共享依赖中的部分特性，无法在统一框架中同时建模所有三种关键属性，限制了临床预测性能。

Method: 从EHR数据构建异构图，节点代表临床实体（患者、就诊、诊断、处方、手术），边通过归一化点互信息（NPMI）连接统计关联的实体；采用多任务图Transformer统一建模异构临床交互、时间轨迹和跨任务依赖。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，MiGHT-EHR在药物推荐、住院时长预测、死亡率和再入院率预测四个任务中平均优于最先进方法，尤其在死亡率和再入院预测上提升显著。

Conclusion: MiGHT-EHR学习到的表示支持多样化预测任务，同时保持临床可解释结构：患者邻域按临床结局组织、显著医学概念可作为表示空间中的线性方向恢复、任务概率校准良好。

Abstract: Learning from Electronic Health Records (EHRs) has gained significant attention due to its potential to improve clinical prediction. However, effective learning remains challenging because EHRs encode heterogeneous, temporally ordered clinical interactions. In particular, EHRs contain: (i) heterogeneous clinical entities, including patients, visits, diagnoses, prescriptions, and procedures, together with their heterogeneous interactions, (ii) longitudinal patient trajectories across hospital visits and (iii) shared statistical dependencies across related clinical prediction tasks. Existing EHR learning methods capture only a subset of these properties. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-task Graph transformer for Heterogeneous Temporal EHRs (MiGHT-EHR), which jointly models all three within a unified representation learning method. MiGHT-EHR constructs a heterogeneous graph from EHRs in which nodes represent clinical entities and edges connect statistically associated entities identified via normalized point-wise mutual information. Across MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV datasets, MiGHT-EHR outperforms state-of-the-art methods on average across four tasks: drug recommendation, prediction of length-of-stay, mortality, and readmission, with particularly strong improvements in mortality and readmission prediction. Furthermore, a post-hoc analysis of the learned representations reveals that patient neighborhoods are organized by clinical outcomes, salient medical concepts are recoverable as linear directions in the representation space, and task probabilities are well calibrated. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that MiGHT-EHR representations support diverse prediction tasks while preserving clinically interpretable structure.

</details>


### [73] [SNI-GNN: SmartNIC-Assisted Full-Graph GNN Training with In-Network Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06441)
*Guofan Yu,Sitian Chen,Zhenheng Tang,Xiaowen Chu,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNI-GNN是一个基于SmartNIC辅助的全图GNN训练系统，通过在网内预测远程嵌入来减少跨节点通信开销，在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现21-45%的通信削减和1.3-3.6倍的端到端加速，同时保持精度损失不超过0.01。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全图GNN训练在多服务器集群上精度高但扩展性差，主要瓶颈在于节点间大量且不规则的高频嵌入交换通信。现有的分区和压缩技术无法完全解决这一通信瓶颈，需要新的机制来在保持精度的同时大幅降低通信开销。

Method: SNI-GNN采用三项关键技术：(1) 在SmartNIC上部署轻量级线性趋势预测器，对缓存的远程嵌入进行细化预测，通过在网内（in-network）预测来减少实际通信量；(2) 基于重要性的边界节点采样策略，选择性传输关键节点嵌入；(3) 异步DPU-GPU数据流水线配合中间结果复用，提升整体吞吐。同时提供误差界和收敛界理论分析，证明在二阶动态有界条件下预测器偏差可控，并达到标准非凸收敛。

Result: 在NVIDIA BlueField-3上实现，与现有一流全图系统集成后：通信量削减21-45%，相对BNS-GCN获得1.3-3.6倍端到端加速，相对基线SANCUS获得最高1.29倍加速，精度损失不超过0.01，可扩展到16个GPU且支持数千万条边的图规模。

Conclusion: 基于SmartNIC的在网预测是一种实用的通信高效方案，可作为现有分区与压缩技术的补充，用于大规模通信高效的全图GNN训练，在性能和精度之间实现了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Full-graph GNN training delivers high accuracy but scales poorly on multi-server clusters due to heavy, irregular inter-node embedding exchanges. We present SNI-GNN, a SmartNIC-assisted full-graph training system that reduces communication while preserving accuracy by predicting remote embeddings in-network. SNI-GNN deploys a lightweight linear-trend predictor on SmartNICs to refine cached historical embeddings, coupled with an importance-based boundary-node sampling policy and an asynchronous DPU--GPU data pipeline with intermediate-result reuse. We provide error and convergence bounds showing that predictor bias remains controlled under bounded second-order dynamics and yields standard non-convex convergence with inexact gradients. Implemented on NVIDIA BlueField-3, SNI-GNN integrates with state-of-the-art full-graph systems, cuts communication by 21--45\%, achieves 1.3--3.6$\times$ end-to-end speedups over BNS-GCN and up to 1.29$\times$ over baseline SANCUS, with accuracy loss $\leq 0.01$, and scales efficiently to 16 GPUs on graphs with up to tens of millions of edges. These results indicate SmartNIC-based in-network prediction is a practical complement to partitioning and compression techniques for communication-efficient full-graph GNN training at scale.

</details>


### [74] [ED-CSP: Crystal Structure Prediction from Electron Diffraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06448)
*Germain Poloudenny,Yaël Frégier,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ED-CSP是一个从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射(ED)观测中预测三维晶体结构的机器学习框架，结合关系集合编码器、排列不变多视图聚合和周期流生成器，联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上达到57.49% MR@5，优于PXRDGen(52.92%)，扩展到百万结构训练数据后提升至66.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、未索引的电子衍射观测中恢复周期性三维晶体结构是一个极具挑战性的生成式逆问题。现有基于ED的学习方法主要局限于预测晶体学标签、从索引反射重建结构或从有限结构库中检索候选，缺乏真正的生成能力。

Method: ED-CSP由三部分组成：(1)关系集合编码器用于编码多探测器平面的ED斑点集合；(2)置换不变多视图聚合用于融合来自不同视角的衍射信息；(3)周期流生成器用于联合预测晶格参数和分数原子坐标。同时构建了ED-CS数据集，包含485万模拟多视图ED晶体结构，经七个材料数据库去重并过滤CHILI-100K重叠。

Result: 在2,075个CHILI-100K材料上，ED-CSP仅用CHILI训练达到57.49% MR@5，超过PXRDGen(52.92%)；用百万结构预训练后提升至66.27% MR@5；在训练检索库外的1,024种组成上仍达53.52% MR@5；用非同构等组成衍射替换目标ED后MR@5下降22.09个百分点，验证了预测依赖输入衍射而非组成。

Conclusion: ED-CSP和ED-CS为从稀疏ED观测进行生成式晶体结构预测建立了基准，展示了超越精确公式检索的真实生成能力，并为未来迁移到实验数据奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering a periodic 3D crystal structure from sparse, unindexed electron diffraction (ED) observations is a challenging generative inverse problem. Existing ED-based learning methods mainly predict crystallographic labels, reconstruct structures from indexed reflections, or retrieve candidates from finite structure libraries. Here, we introduce ED-CSP, a machine learning framework that predicts crystal structures from chemical composition, atom count, and multiple detector-plane ED spot sets. ED-CSP combines a relational set encoder, permutation-invariant multi-view aggregation, and a periodic flow generator to jointly predict lattice parameters and fractional atomic coordinates.
  To train the model, we construct ED-CS, a dataset of 4.85 million simulated multi-view ED crystal structures, deduplicated across seven materials repositories and filtered to exclude CHILI-100K overlaps. On 2,075 held-out CHILI-100K materials, ED-CSP trained only on CHILI achieves a structural match rate of 57.49% MR@5, outperforming PXRDGen (52.92%), a state-of-the-art crystal structure prediction model conditioned on powder X-ray diffraction. Scaling training data further improves performance: initializing from a one-million-structure precursor raises MR@5 to 66.27%. On 1,024 compositions absent from the training retrieval library, the model still achieves 53.52% MR@5, demonstrating true generative capability beyond exact-formula retrieval. Replacing target ED observations with diffraction from non-isomorphic structures of identical composition decreases MR@5 by 22.09 percentage points, confirming that predictions depend on the input diffraction patterns rather than composition alone. ED-CSP and ED-CS establish a benchmark for generative crystal structure prediction from sparse ED observations and provide a foundation for future transfer to experimental data.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Attention: Signed Integrated Gradients Attribution in a BiomeGPT-Style Microbiome Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06486)
*Oren Nelson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对特征标记化Transformer（如BiomeGPT）的可解释性问题，指出[CLS]注意力权重非负且发生在标记融合之后，无法区分疾病支持与健康支持信号。作者提出使用积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）方法，并设计一个基于物种来源的基线T' = S + A_0，从而获得带极性的归因结果，能显式分离致病与保护性微生物信号，并通过二阶积分海森矩阵揭示微生物群落的交互规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于[CLS]注意力权重的解释方法存在两个关键局限性：(1) 注意力权重非负，无法区分支持疾病与支持健康的证据；(2) 它在特征融合之后生效，模糊了输入来源（物种S和丰度A）各自对输出的贡献。这严重限制了在BiomeGPT等特征标记化模型中对下游分类结果进行准确解读的能力。

Method: 论文提出一种基于积分梯度（Integrated Gradients）的、带符号且融合感知的属性归因方法。核心创新是提出了一个基于来源的基线T' = S + A_0，该基线在保持物种身份作为固定生物学坐标的同时，隔离出丰度变化的影响。将该方法应用于疾病与健康决策边界，可获得明确区分致病与保护性微生物信号的极性归因。此外，推荐使用二阶积分海森矩阵（Integrated Hessians）来暴露微生物群落成员间的交互规则。

Result: 实验表明，基于梯度的归因方法能够揭示物种-丰度方向性关系以及[CLS]注意力权重完全无法捕获的敏感性诊断信息。二阶积分海森矩阵进一步揭示了群落内成员的交互模式，包括一个成员的扰动如何改变模型对另一成员的敏感性，以及哪些物种在特定丰度水平下驱动模糊样本偏向疾病或健康。

Conclusion: 该方法为BiomeGPT提供了原则性的可解释性框架，且可推广到其他平滑、可微的特征标记化Transformer模型。源代码已公开。

Abstract: In a feature-tokenized transformer (arXiv:2106.11959) such as BiomeGPT (doi:10.64898/2026.01.05.697599), each input token is built by fusing a fixed identity with a sample-specific measurement: a fixed species and a variable abundance, T = S + A. To interpret downstream classification in such models, prior work inspects the attention weights of the special [CLS] token (arXiv:2106.11959, arXiv:1810.04805, BiomeGPT) to rank sample tokens by importance. These weights have two critical limitations: they are nonnegative, so they cannot separate disease-supporting from health-supporting evidence (arXiv:2201.12114), and they act after token fusion, obscuring how the input sources S and A each affect the output.
  To address this we use Integrated Gradients (arXiv:1703.01365), a signed, fusion-aware attribution method, and propose a source-derived baseline T' = S + A_0 for feature-tokenized models such as BiomeGPT, which preserves species identity as a fixed biological coordinate while isolating the effect of abundance variation. Applied to a disease-versus-health decision margin, it yields polarity that explicitly separates pathogenic from protective microbial signals. We show that this gradient-based approach uncovers species-abundance directional relationships and sensitivity diagnostics entirely obscured by unsigned [CLS] attention weights. We further recommend second-order Integrated Hessians (arXiv:2002.04138) to expose microbiome community interaction rules: how a perturbation in one member alters the model's sensitivity to another, and which other species drive ambiguous cases toward disease or health at a given abundance level. This provides a principled approach to explainability in BiomeGPT that generalizes to other smooth and differentiable feature-tokenized transformers. Code is available at https://github.com/nohren/token-source-attribution

</details>


### [76] [Toward Reliable Context Compression for Long-Horizon Agents: An Empirical Study of Execution Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06503)
*Guanghui Min,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Chen Chen,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，通过验证器引导的成对闭环延续评估来优化智能体上下文压缩提示，解决递归上下文压缩导致近期交互影响减弱、阻塞动作增多和跨运行不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归上下文压缩虽然能控制长时程智能体的上下文增长，但其行为影响尚未被充分理解。初步实证研究发现压缩会削弱近期交互的影响，导致阻塞动作增多、重复探索和跨运行不稳定，因此需要更可靠的压缩方法。

Method: TRACE是一种验证器引导的框架：通过从相同环境状态出发的成对闭环延续来评估每次压缩事件，并利用摘要偏好优化自然语言压缩提示，同时保持所有模型冻结（即不微调模型）。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的初步结果显示，TRACE在任务性能、多运行可靠性和上下文-执行效率方面均优于现有压缩基线。

Conclusion: 这些发现为"边界局部评估"作为可靠智能体上下文压缩的可行方向提供了早期证据。

Abstract: Recurrent context compression controls context growth in long-horizon agents, but its behavioral effects remain poorly understood. In this preliminary empirical study, we show that compression can weaken the influence of recent interactions, increasing blocked actions, repeated exploration, and instability across runs. Motivated by these observations, we introduce TRACE, a verifier-guided framework that evaluates individual compaction events through paired closed-loop continuations from the same environment state and uses summary preferences to optimize a natural-language compression prompt while keeping all models frozen. Initial results on AppWorld show improvements over existing compression baselines in task performance, multi-run reliability, and context--execution efficiency. These findings provide early evidence for boundary-local evaluation as a promising direction for reliable agent context compression.

</details>


### [77] [Unmasking Removal-Budget Confounding: A Matched Operating-Point Evaluation Framework for Adaptive Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06511)
*Wei-Hsiang Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Chen-Hsuan Fang,Jung-Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自适应数据清洗方法中由于分区粒度变化导致的"移除预算混淆"评估偏差，并提出了操作点感知的评估框架（匹配预算和匹配召回控制，结合AUROC/AUPRC阈值无关指标），在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上验证了多数朴素评估中的性能提升在操作点对齐后消失或缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应数据清洗方法用数据驱动的分区替代人工过滤阈值，但改变分区粒度会隐式移动决策边界、改变总移除样本数，导致"移除预算混淆"——性能指标的提升反映的是更小的移除预算而非更优的腐败样本判别能力，从而产生评估偏差。

Method: 提出操作点感知评估框架，使用匹配预算（matched-budget）和匹配召回（matched-recall）控制作为对照，并结合阈值无关指标（AUROC和AUPRC）；设计了一个多层自适应清洗器，包含重加权学习难度线索、辅助欧氏距离线索和更细的分区粒度，并采用误报分解分析清洁但困难样本在不同腐败率下的误差贡献。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet-100上的实验表明，朴素评估声称的重设计性能提升在操作点对齐后消失；清洁但困难样本在低腐败率下主导误差、中等腐败率下呈阈值依赖性、严重腐败下贡献可忽略；真实排序优势仅在低流行率场景和严重腐败下的高召回区域保留。

Conclusion: 自适应数据清洗方法必须在匹配的操作点下进行基准测试，才能使性能提升真实反映腐败样本判别能力，而非移除预算的差异。

Abstract: Adaptive data-cleaning methods replace manual filtering thresholds with data-driven partitions. However, changing the partition granularity, the number of groups used to segment samples by estimated corruption risk, can implicitly shift the decision boundary and alter the overall number of removed samples. This creates a bias known as removal-budget confounding, where apparent gains in metrics like precision or false-positive rate reflect a smaller removal budget rather than superior corruption discrimination. To address this evaluation bias, we introduce an operating-point-aware evaluation framework that evaluates methods using matched-budget and matched-recall controls alongside threshold-independent metrics (AUROC and AUPRC). We test this framework on a multi-cue adaptive cleaner redesign featuring a reweighted learning-difficulty cue, an auxiliary Euclidean-distance cue, and increased partition granularity intended to isolate clean-but-difficult samples. While naive evaluations (assessing configurations at their own induced operating points) suggest substantial performance improvements for the redesign, these gains disappear once operating points are equalized. False-positive decomposition reveals that clean-but-difficult samples primarily drive error counts at low corruption rates, become threshold-dependent at moderate corruption, and contribute negligibly under severe corruption. Experiments on CIFAR-10 and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that most performance differences observed in naive evaluation shrink or vanish at low-to-moderate corruption when operating points are matched. True ranking advantages only remain in specific low-prevalence settings and in high-recall regions under severe corruption. These findings highlight that adaptive cleaning methods must be benchmarked at matched operating points to ensure performance gains reflect genuine corruption discrimination.

</details>


### [78] [Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation: Experimental Design for Heterogeneous Treatment Effects under Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06512)
*Hoang Dang,Luan Pham,Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TWNA（Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation）两阶段分层设计，利用试点估计的组-臂结果方差来分配最终阶段的样本量和处理概率，以优化目标加权组平均处理效应（GATE）的估计精度，并扩展至应对部署组成不确定性和异常结果的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机实验通常在一个总体中进行但在另一个总体中部署，而按实验比例分配会浪费预算在部署中少见的组上，按部署比例分配则对难以精确测量的组抽样不足。现有方案无法同时平衡部署重要性与统计难度。

Method: 提出TWNA两阶段分层设计：使用试点阶段的组-臂结果方差估计，分配最终阶段的样本量及处理概率以优化目标加权GATE精度。Oracle规则具有闭式解，能平衡部署权重与统计难度；plug-in规则在试点方差估计稳定时恢复Oracle规则。扩展版本处理部署组成不确定性的鲁棒性，并区分权重鲁棒性与针对偏态、稀有事件或污染结果的试点鲁棒变体。

Result: 模拟实验和真实协变量基准测试表明，当组既在部署中重要又难以测量时，TWNA能带来最大的增益。

Conclusion: TWNA提供了一种系统化、理论与实践统一的分层分配框架，能够同时权衡部署重要性与统计测量难度，并具备对部署组成不确定性和数据质量问题的鲁棒扩展能力。

Abstract: Randomized experiments are often run in one population to guide decisions in another. Allocating by experimental proportions wastes budget on groups that rarely appear in deployment, whereas allocating by deployment proportions under-samples groups that are hard to measure precisely. We propose \textbf{TWNA} (Target-Weighted Neyman Allocation), a two-stage stratified design that uses pilot estimates of group--arm outcome variances to allocate final-stage sample sizes and treatment probabilities for target-weighted group average treatment effect (GATE) precision. The oracle rule has a closed form and balances deployment importance with statistical difficulty; the plug-in rule recovers it as pilot variance estimates stabilize. We also extend TWNA to handle uncertainty about deployment composition, remaining robust whether the target mix is roughly known or entirely unknown. Finally, we distinguish this weight robustness from a pilot-robust variant for skewed, rare-event, or contaminated outcomes. Simulations and real-covariate benchmarks show the largest gains when groups are both deployment-important and difficult to measure.

</details>


### [79] [CertBind from Multimodal Connectivity to Certifiable Retrieval Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06516)
*Shuheng Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Weijia Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Jiaxin Liu,Xiangyu Zeng,Haotian Geng,Fan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertBind提出了多尺度可认证组合理论，用于冻结多模态编码器连接图的任务决策层面。通过节点、边、路径和查询四个尺度的认证机制，实现跨模态组合路由的可信决策，包括Direct、Recovery、Certified和Abstain四种输出状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级连接器虽然使冻结多模态编码器在表示层面可组合，但在部署时存在任务决策层面的问题：连接路径可能扩展跨模态覆盖范围，同时损害原有的原生检索能力（如CLIP R@1从0.524降至0.290）。需要一种机制来保证组合路由的可靠性和安全性。

Method: CertBind采用多尺度（multiscale）理论框架：(1) 节点尺度：native anchors确立精确的任务识别边界；(2) 边尺度：契约感知的conformal ranks提供图级family-wise误差控制；(3) 路径尺度：重叠感知预算与干净校准提供有限样本恢复半径；(4) 查询尺度：覆盖的top-k候选集在大小为k时成为点证书。据此将路由分为Direct、Recovery、Certified和Abstain四种决策。

Result: 在C-MCR共享路由上，原生CLIP R@1从0.524降至0.290；生产环境回退策略恢复了0.963±0.002的干净检索性能；通过分支记录了1.000的无伤害值（no-harm value）。

Conclusion: CertBind将多模态组合性从连接表示层面扩展到可认证的任务决策层面，在保持跨模态能力的同时提供可证明的安全保障和决策可解释性。

Abstract: Lightweight connectors make frozen multimodal encoders composable at the representation level. Deployment exposes a second problem at the level of task decisions. A connected route can expand cross-modal reach while changing an established native retrieval capability. We introduce CertBind, a multiscale theory of certifiable composition for frozen multimodal connector graphs. At the node scale, native anchors establish the exact task identification boundary under the stated chart model. At the edge scale, contract-aware conformal ranks provide graph-wide family-wise error control. At the path scale, an overlap-aware budget and clean calibration yield a finite-sample recovery radius under declared conditions. At the query scale, this radius yields a covered top-k candidate set that becomes a point certificate when its size equals k. CertBind therefore retains supported routes as Direct, sends only flagged routes to recovery, returns Certified for decisive recovery, and returns Abstain for unresolved queries. The evaluated C-MCR shared route reduced native CLIP R@1 from 0.524 to 0.290. The production fallback recovered 0.963 +- 0.002 of clean retrieval, while the passing branch recorded a no-harm value of 1.000. CertBind extends multimodal composability from connected representations to certifiable task decisions.

</details>


### [80] [Online Security Learning in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems under Hidden Byzantine Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06520)
*Ximing Sun,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统在拜占庭攻击下的在线合作控制问题。学习者观测计划行动、公开奖励和状态，但无法观测被篡改后的执行动作。作者证明了攻击者信息决定了稳健MDP的几何结构，识别了安全学习的信息论极限（安全遗憾精确分解为回报遗憾与累积响应差距之和），并提出了阶段绑定的稳健估计到决策学习算法，遗憾界为Õ(H²S√(AK))+E[D_K]。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在现实部署中面临拜占庭攻击的安全威胁：固定但未知的子集智能体在观察计划后可以秘密覆盖其自身坐标。学习者只能看到计划动作和公开状态/奖励，无法观测真实执行动作。现有文献缺乏对这种攻击模型下安全学习理论极限与算法设计的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 建模：将拜占庭攻击下的合作控制问题形式化为攻击者观察计划动作时产生精确的(s,a)-矩形稳健MDP（行是覆盖诱导的公开结果律的凸包），而盲目攻击者产生s-矩形模型。2) 理论：证明安全遗憾精确分解为返回遗憾（针对生成数据的响应）加上累积响应差距D_K；构造两个不可区分的地平线一实例，证明Ω(K)期望安全遗憾的下界，显示对D_K的依赖不可避免。3) 算法：设计阶段绑定的稳健估计到决策（stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions）学习器。

Result: 证明了攻击者观察计划动作与盲目攻击分别诱导(s,a)-矩形和s-矩形稳健MDP；建立了安全遗憾的精确分解定理，给出Ω(K)的信息论下界；提出阶段绑定稳健估计到决策学习算法，获得Õ(H²S√(AK))+E[D_K]的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 该研究为拜占庭攻击下多智能体系统的安全学习提供了全面的理论和算法基础，明确了信息论极限与可达遗憾界之间的匹配关系。

Abstract: We study online cooperative control of a multi-agent system under Byzantine attacks. Namely, an unknown, fixed subset of agents are Byzantine comprised and can stealthily overwrite its own coordinates of the team's planned joint action after observing that plan. The learner observes planned actions, public rewards, and public states, but neither the overwrite nor the executed joint action. Our objective is security: to optimize the team performance against the worst overwrites and achieve the optimal security value. We first show that the attacker's information determines the geometry. An attacker that observes the planned action induces an exact $(s,a)$-rectangular robust Markov decision process (MDP) whose rows are convex hulls of overwrite-induced public-outcome laws, whereas a blind attacker induces an $s$-rectangular model. We then identify the information-theoretic limit of security learning, showing that the security regret decomposes exactly into return regret against the response generating the data and a cumulative response gap $D_K$. Two indistinguishable horizon-one instances force $Ω(K)$ expected security regret while return regret is zero, showing that dependence on $D_K$ is unavoidable. Finally, we develop a stage-tied robust estimation-to-decisions learner and prove a regret bound of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left(H^2S\sqrt{AK}\right)+\mathbb E[D_K]$. Our studies thus provide comprehensive theoretical and algorithmic foundations of reliable multi-agent systems under Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [81] [Newton-Schulz Retraction-Based Inference Enables Hidden Quantum Markov Models to Outperform Classical HMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06554)
*Ning Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-RIS算法，利用Newton-Schulz正交化在Stiefel流形上学习迹保持的隐藏量子马尔可夫模型（HQMMs），避免了昂贵的矩阵分解，并首次证明HQMM能在非量子生成的数据上显著优于EM训练的HMM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HMM在处理复杂隐藏动态时能力受限，HQMM作为其量子泛化理论上更具表达力，但现有HQMM学习方法在非量子过程生成的数据上未能持续优于EM训练的HMM，限制了其实际应用价值。因此需要一种可扩展且性能可靠的HQMM学习算法。

Method: NS-RIS算法在Stiefel流形上进行推理，使用Newton-Schulz正交化计算极分解搜索方向，在保持流形可行性的同时避免昂贵的矩阵分解。作者还给出了在平滑性、随机梯度和有限Newton-Schulz精度等标准假设下的有限时间平稳性保证。

Result: 在合成HMM基准上，NS-RIS平均提升38.5%（最高50.6%），优于EM和最先进的HQMM方法COSM；在合成HQMM基准上，较COSM提升测试指标18.9%且运行时间减少12.0%；在真实Splice分类基准上，潜伏维度6和8分别降低平均分类误差17.9%和14.9%。这是首个HQMM在非量子生成数据上显著超越EM训练HMM的实验证据。

Conclusion: NS-RIS使HQMM超越了HMM的理论泛化定位，确立其作为科学序列数据实用且具表达力的模型。该方法在合成和真实基准上均优于现有方法，为HQMM的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Hidden Markov models (HMMs) are widely used probabilistic models for discrete sequential data but can be limited when hidden dynamics are complex. Hidden quantum Markov models (HQMMs) generalize HMMs by replacing probability vectors with density matrices and stochastic transitions with quantum operations, enabling richer latent representations. However, existing HQMM learning methods have not consistently outperformed Expectation--Maximization (EM)-trained HMMs on data not generated by quantum processes, limiting their practical applicability. We introduce NS-RIS, Newton--Schulz Retraction-based Inference on the Stiefel manifold, a scalable algorithm for learning trace-preserving HQMMs. NS-RIS uses Newton--Schulz orthogonalization to compute a polar-factor search direction while preserving Stiefel-manifold feasibility, avoiding costly matrix decompositions. We further establish a finite-time stationarity guarantee under standard assumptions on smoothness, stochastic gradients, and finite Newton--Schulz accuracy. Empirically, NS-RIS provides the first benchmark evidence that an HQMM can significantly outperform an EM-trained HMM on data not generated by a quantum model. On synthetic HMM-generated benchmarks, NS-RIS outperforms both EM and the state-of-the-art HQMM method COSM, improving the evaluation metric by an average of 38.5% and by up to 50.6%. On a synthetic HQMM benchmark, it improves the test metric over COSM by 18.9% while reducing runtime by 12.0%. On the real-world Splice classification benchmark, NS-RIS also surpasses both EM and COSM in higher-dimensional latent regimes, reducing mean classification error by 17.9% for latent dimension 6 and 14.9% for latent dimension 8 relative to COSM. These results move HQMMs beyond a theoretical generalization of HMMs and establish them as practical and expressive models for scientific sequence data.

</details>


### [82] [Bootstrap-Conditioned Action Selection with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06559)
*Devansh Gupta,Shiv Tavker,Dmitry Efimov,Suchitra Sathyanarayana,Gitanjali Bhutani,Boris N. Oreshkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-ICL方法，利用预训练表格基础模型通过上下文学习实现在线决策，将自举重采样与冻结的ICL模型结合，引入臂上下文条件架构以增强跨臂统计共享，在标准上下文赌博机基准上取得了优于既有基线的早期后悔度和整体遗憾表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文赌博机是样本高效个性化的自然框架，但在稀疏、有偏的交互数据、不可靠的不确定性估计以及严重冷启动场景下实际部署困难。作者希望探索预训练表格基础模型能否通过上下文学习被转化为随机化策略，从而应对这些挑战。

Method: BC-ICL方法在每个回合中：(1) 对交互历史进行自举重采样；(2) 将冻结的预训练ICL模型以该重采样为条件；(3) 对所有动作进行打分；(4) 选择得分最高的动作。同时引入臂上下文条件架构，促进跨动作的统计强度共享，避免孤立臂赌博机中常见的自举失败模式。

Result: 在标准上下文赌博机套件上，BC-ICL在严格在线协议下展现出较强的早期回合后悔度和整体后悔性能，优于既有的基准方法。

Conclusion: 预训练表格基础模型结合ICL的自举条件动作选择可以作为上下文赌博机的有效随机化策略，为解决数据稀疏和冷启动等实际部署挑战提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Contextual bandits offer a natural framework for sample-efficient personalization, but practical deployment remains difficult under sparse, biased interaction data, unreliable uncertainty estimates, and severe cold starts. We study whether pre-trained tabular foundation models with in-context learning can be turned into randomized policies for online decision making. We propose BC-ICL (Bootstrap-conditioned action selection using ICL), which at each round draws a bootstrap resample of the interaction history, conditions a frozen pre-trained ICL model on that resample, scores all actions, and selects the action with the highest sampled score. We further introduce an arm-context conditioning architecture that promotes shared statistical strength across actions and helps avoid common bootstrap failure modes of isolated-arm bandits. Empirically, this policy delivers strong early-round regret and regret performance on standard contextual bandit suites, outperforming established baselines under a strict online protocol.

</details>


### [83] [Theoretical Foundations of Communication-Efficient, Robust, and Practical Distributed and Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06563)
*Grigory Malinovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习与分布式优化中理论-实践交叉的七大挑战，提出ProxSkip、方差缩减ProxSkip、部分客户端参与下的本地步骤加速、服务端步长与无放回采样、梯度差分压缩、拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与并发达成、基于随机非对称链的低秩自适应等系列算法，建立了新颖的收敛性保证并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大规模训练依赖经典优化原理，但分布式系统的约束要求重新审视这些基础。联邦学习与分布式优化中，本地梯度步骤加速通信等广泛使用的启发式方法缺乏理论支撑，且真实场景（异构性、部分参与、拜占庭攻击、通信瓶颈）下的收敛性保证尚不完善。

Method: 论文系统性地提出了多项算法贡献：(1) ProxSkip证明本地梯度步骤可加速通信；(2) 方差缩减ProxSkip消除随机本地更新的邻域误差；(3) 证明部分客户端参与下本地步骤仍保持通信加速；(4) 证明服务端步长与无放回采样在异构场景中改善收敛；(5) 对随机重排提出压缩梯度差分而非梯度本身；(6) 利用梯度差分裁剪同时实现拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与；(7) 基于随机非对称链建立首个低秩自适应的理论框架。

Result: 七项贡献均建立了在真实假设下的锐利收敛性保证（sharp guarantees），并辅以数值实验验证理论与实践的一致性。具体包括本地步骤的通信加速理论奠基、邻域误差消除、异构场景收敛改善、梯度差分压缩的优势证明、拜占庭鲁棒性与部分参与的并发达成，以及低秩自适应的首个理论框架。

Conclusion: 该论文系统性地弥合了机器学习优化理论与实践之间的鸿沟，为联邦学习中的核心瓶颈提供了统一的算法框架和理论基础，特别是在通信压缩、异构性处理、鲁棒性保障和模型微调等方向开辟了新的研究路径。

Abstract: Machine learning and optimization have advanced together, with practical demands motivating new theory and theoretical breakthroughs enabling new applications. Modern large-scale training relies on classical optimization principles, but the constraints of distributed systems require these foundations to be reconsidered. This thesis addresses seven challenges at the intersection of theory and practice, focusing on key bottlenecks in federated learning and distributed optimization. First, we introduce ProxSkip and prove that local gradient steps can accelerate communication, providing a theoretical foundation for this widely used heuristic. Second, we develop Variance Reduced ProxSkip, which eliminates the neighborhood error of stochastic local updates while balancing communication and local computation. Third, we show that local steps retain their communication acceleration under partial client participation. Fourth, we prove that server-side stepsizes and sampling without replacement improve convergence in heterogeneous settings. Fifth, for Random Reshuffling, we demonstrate that compressing gradient differences rather than gradients yields better theoretical and practical performance. Sixth, we establish that Byzantine robustness and partial participation can be achieved simultaneously using gradient-difference clipping. Finally, we develop the first theoretical framework for low-rank adaptation based on randomized asymmetric chains, providing new insights into fine-tuning large models. Across these contributions, we introduce novel algorithmic frameworks, establish sharp guarantees under realistic assumptions, and support the theory with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [84] [Quantization Damage Is Multiplicative, Not Additive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06564)
*Zekun Wu,Swati Dhiman,Adriano Koshiyama*

Main category: cs.LG
