TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型量化过程中决策受损的问题，提出"边际收缩"（margin shrinkage）理论：量化并非给模型决策添加固定大小的噪声，而是对决策边际施加一个随比特宽度收缩的乘性因子（4比特中位数0.86，3比特0.33，2比特0.00）。该收缩使大边际带来的保护失效，模型自身的小偏差决定失败方向。实验覆盖16个模型、8个模型族、3种量化方法及2-8比特宽度，发现该关系在统计上优于加性噪声假设，并能校准地预测翻转概率（ECE 0.004），且每模型的常数不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设量化在模型输出上添加固定大小的噪声，据此推断高置信度的决策在量化后仍然安全。然而实际部署中，量化后的模型会静默地停止调用工具、失去一半安全拒绝能力，而基准分数几乎不变。作者旨在直接测量决策本身的变化（而非仅看整体系分数），以理解哪些决策会在量化后改变、以及为什么高置信度不能提供保护。

Method: 作者引入两向决策的边际定义（所选选项得分减最佳备选得分），在量化前后追踪该边际。使用16个模型（来自8个模型族）、3种量化方法和8到2比特的位宽，进行系统地边际追踪。通过统计拟合比较加性噪声模型与乘性收缩模型，用留出决策预测翻转概率并评估校准误差，同时测量每模型每比特宽度的配对边际集以估计决策损坏。

Result: 量化不是给边际加固定噪声，而是乘以随比特宽度收缩的因子（4比特中位数0.86，3比特0.33，2比特0.00）。在受损的工具调用和安全决策上，加性噪声模型在统计比较中从不胜出。拟合关系在留出决策上预测翻转率的中位误差仅1.8个百分点；逐决策的翻转概率是校准的不确定性估计（在131,758个预测上ECE为0.004）。该形式在每模型中均成立，但常数因模型而异、不可迁移。在成本匹配测试下，增加一个比特比任何修复手段都更便宜有效。

Conclusion: 量化导致的模型决策损坏并非随机噪声效果，而是可预测的边际收缩现象。大边际并不保护决策不受量化影响，收缩方向由模型自身的小偏好决定（如3比特时工具调用倾向崩溃为不行动，而工具选择不受影响）。这意味着需要每模型、每位宽地进行个体化决策风险评估，且最简单的缓解方案——增加一个比特——在成本效率上优于所有修复方法。

Abstract: Quantization is how large language models are actually deployed, and below four bits it is known to hurt. What nobody can say is which of the model's decisions will change at a given bit-width. The damage is silent: a compressed agent stops calling its tools, then loses half its safety refusals, yet benchmark scores barely move. Prior work assumes quantization adds noise of a roughly fixed size, which would make confident decisions safe. We measure the decision itself instead. The margin of a two-way decision is the model's score for the option it picks minus the score of its best alternative; we track it before and after quantization across 16 models from 8 model families, three quantization methods, and bit-widths from 8 down to 2. Quantization does not add fixed-size noise to the margin. It multiplies the margin by a factor that collapses with bit-width (median 0.86 at 4 bits, 0.33 at 3, 0.00 at 2); we call this margin shrinkage. This contraction reduces the protection a large margin affords; the model's own small biases pick the direction of failure: at 3 bits the decision to call a tool collapses toward inaction while the choice of which tool is untouched. In fitted statistical comparison, additive-noise accounts never win on the damaged tool and safety decisions. The fitted relation predicts flip rates within a median of 1.8 percentage points on held-out decisions, though no flip was used in the fit; per decision, the predicted flip probabilities are calibrated uncertainty estimates (expected calibration error 0.004 over 131,758 predictions). The same form holds in every model we measure, but the constants are each model's own and do not transfer. A small paired margin set, measured per model and bit-width, estimates which decisions break without full generative evaluation; under our cost-matched tests, nothing repairs damage more cheaply than one more bit.

</details>


### [85] [CrystalGRPO: Target-Aligned and Coverage-Preserving Reinforcement Learning for Flow-Based Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06582)
*Kaixiang Su,Hongfei Xue,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CrystalGRPO，一种面向晶体结构预测的强化学习后训练框架，将策略扩展至坐标-晶格联合状态，结合MACE能量与结构匹配恢复分数，提供Q与C两种工作模式，在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上相比坐标-only强化学习显著提升Top-1与Top-20目标恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的生成模型虽能高效生成晶体结构候选，但其预训练目标不直接优化下游目标恢复。现有强化学习后训练主要依赖能量奖励和仅坐标的随机策略，存在两个问题：预测能量无法识别参考多晶型，且奖励驱动的集中化会降低Top-N恢复所需的候选覆盖度。

Method: CrystalGRPO将已有的ODE到SDE策略构建扩展到联合坐标-晶格状态空间。奖励设计结合MACE预测能量与基于StructureMatcher的恢复分数，提供两种模式：CrystalGRPO-Q优先单次采样恢复；CrystalGRPO-C结合完整轨迹参考正则化与覆盖感知的组优势函数，以保持有限预算下的目标恢复能力。

Result: 在MP-20和MPTS-52数据集上，使用PXRDGen和OMatG两种骨干网络，两种变体在全部四组骨干-数据集设置中均降低了单样本与二十样本的RMSE。CrystalGRPO-Q在所有设置中稳定提升Top-1，而CrystalGRPO-C在所有设置中取得更高的Top-20。

Conclusion: CrystalGRPO证明了在联合坐标-晶格状态空间中整合能量与恢复奖励的强化学习后训练，能够同时优化单次采样精度与有限预算下的候选覆盖恢复，为晶体结构预测的后训练对齐提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models can efficiently produce candidate structures for crystal structure prediction (CSP), but their pretrained objectives do not directly optimize downstream target recovery. Reinforcement-learning post-training offers a flexible solution, yet existing approaches rely primarily on energy rewards and coordinate-only stochastic policies. Predicted energy does not identify the reference polymorph, while reward-driven concentration can reduce the candidate coverage required for Top-N recovery. We introduce CrystalGRPO, a CSP-aligned post-training framework that extends existing ODE-to-SDE policy constructions to the joint coordinate--lattice state. CrystalGRPO combines MACE-predicted energy with a StructureMatcher-based recovery score and provides two operating modes: CrystalGRPO-Q, which prioritizes single-draw recovery, and CrystalGRPO-C, which combines full-trajectory reference regularization with a coverage-aware group advantage to preserve finite-budget target recovery. Across MP-20 and MPTS-52 with PXRDGen and OMatG backbones, both variants reduce one- and twenty-sample RMSE relative to coordinate-only reinforcement in all four backbone--dataset settings. CrystalGRPO-Q consistently improves Top-1, whereas CrystalGRPO-C achieves a higher Top-20 across all settings.

</details>


### [86] [Flowing Through States: Neural ODE Regularization for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06595)
*Mohamed Ghanem,Bernd Finkbeiner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经ODE的正则化方法，通过显式建模潜在状态转移的动态（类比MDP轨迹与ODE流），使深度强化学习智能体的潜在表示与环境动态保持一致，并在Atari基准（A2C）和网格世界（PPO）上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习智能体通常依赖环境状态的潜在表示，但环境动态决定了语义状态如何演化，对应的潜在转移却往往被隐式处理，导致潜在表示与实际环境动态之间可能存在错位（misalignment），影响学习效率和性能。

Method: 将MDP轨迹与ODE流进行类比，两者都满足"当前状态完全决定后继状态"的性质；在此基础上构建基于神经ODE的正则化约束，强制潜在嵌入遵循一致的ODE流动态，从而将表示学习与环境动态对齐；该正则化方法可广泛适用于深度学习智能体，并被集成到Actor-Critic算法（A2C和PPO）中验证有效性。

Result: 该方法在多种标准Atari基准上对A2C算法带来了显著的性能提升，在网格世界环境中对PPO算法同样取得了主要性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模潜在动态并施加ODE流一致性约束，能够有效对齐表示学习与环境动态，从而提升深度强化学习智能体的决策性能，且该方法具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Neural networks applied to sequential decision-making tasks typically rely on latent representations of environment states. While environment dynamics dictate how semantic states evolve, the corresponding latent transitions are usually left implicit, creating a potential misalignment between the two. We propose to model latent dynamics explicitly by drawing an analogy between Markov decision process (MDP) trajectories and ordinary differential equation (ODE) flows: in both cases, the current state fully determines its successors. Building on this view, we introduce a neural ODE-based regularization method that enforces latent embeddings to follow consistent ODE flows, thereby aligning representation learning with environment dynamics. Although broadly applicable to deep learning agents, we demonstrate its effectiveness in reinforcement learning by integrating it into Actor-Critic algorithms. Our approach yields major performance gains across various standard Atari benchmarks for A2C and gridworld environments for PPO.

</details>


### [87] [Retrofitting Linear Attention into Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06628)
*Jinha Kim,Younghun Roh,Jaeyeon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出块混合注意力（block-hybrid attention），将线性注意力应用于扩散语言模型（dLLM）的先前块，保留活跃去噪块内的精确softmax注意力，从而加速推理。通过对LLaDA 2.1（16B）仅替换20层中的6层注意力层（约60小时后训练），在保持基准性能的同时将解码吞吐量提升高达1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型的块状半自回归解码中，每个去噪步骤尽管使用KV缓存，仍需对所有先前块执行前缀注意力，重复产生计算开销。作者质疑是否可通过将先前块的注意力线性化来进一步加速dLLM推理，以解决这一性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出块混合注意力机制：活跃去噪块内保留精确的softmax注意力，而对先前块使用线性注意力。该方法可被改造（retrofit）到预训练的dLLM中，仅需最小后训练。具体实现上，在LLaDA 2.1（16B开源dLLM）中用6/20层替换为混合注意力层，主要遵循LoLCAT方法，经约60小时转换训练。

Result: LLaDA-Hybrid在基准测试上性能基本保持：HumanEval 72.0% vs 75.6%，MBPP+ 63.0% vs 57.7%，CMATH 86.7% vs 88.3%。基于Triton实现，解码吞吐量最高提升1.7倍，支持更多并发请求且内存消耗更低。

Conclusion: 该工作证明预训练的dLLM可以通过注意力线性化被高效改造以加速推理，在几乎不损失基准性能的前提下显著提升解码吞吐量和并发能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive models by accelerating inference through parallel decoding. Recent dLLMs commonly use blockwise semi-autoregressive decoding, generating blocks autoregressively while denoising tokens within each active block in parallel. However, despite KV caching, each denoising step still attends to all previous blocks, repeatedly incurring prefix-attention cost. Motivated by this bottleneck, we ask whether dLLM inference can be further accelerated by linearizing attention over previous blocks. We introduce block-hybrid attention, which retains exact softmax attention within the active denoising block while applying linear attention over previous blocks. We show that this hybrid attention can be retrofitted into a pretrained dLLM with minimal post-training: LLaDA-Hybrid replaces 6 of the 20 attention layers in LLaDA~2.1, a 16B open-source dLLM, largely following LoLCAT (Zhang et al, 2024). The conversion takes only approximately 60 hours while preserving benchmark performance: 72.0% vs. 75.6% on HumanEval, 63.0% vs. 57.7% on MBPP+, and 86.7% vs. 88.3% on CMATH. With a Triton implementation, LLaDA-Hybrid achieves up to $1.7\times$ higher decoding throughput and supports more concurrent requests before exhausting memory, showing that pretrained dLLMs can be efficiently linearized for faster inference. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Diuven/LLaDA-Hybrid.

</details>


### [88] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中强化学习代理失败的根本原因。通过一个可闭式求解的线性二次问题，作者发现即使最优策略可表示、价值函数足够表达，标准actor-critic算法仍会收敛到远差于最优的策略，原因是critic存在系统性偏差，而非actor表达能力受限。文中推导了策略、代价及解除该问题的关键设计选择（lookahead距离）的闭式解，并通过深度强化学习实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，强化学习代理在部分可观测环境中失败是因为策略无法充分表达（表达受限）。作者想挑战这一直觉：即使策略和价值函数表达能力足够，学习过程本身仍可能因critic的偏差而失败。理解这一偏差有助于找到消除困难的实际设计杠杆。

Method: 作者选取了一个部分可观测的线性二次控制问题，使其能闭式求解标准actor-critic学习的动态。分析critic在无法区分可观测与不可观测状态变异时的学习偏差，推导出最终策略、代价和影响结果的单一设计参数（lookahead步数）的闭式表达式。随后用深度强化学习实验验证理论预测。

Result: 默认设置下，最优可表示策略比理想全观测控制器（成本视为基准）高10.4%的代价，但学习得到的策略比这个最佳可表示策略还差35%。不能帮助代理记忆过去观测，但改变lookahead距离可以消除问题。深度强化学习实验与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 部分可观测性下强化学习失败的核心原因不是策略表达能力不足，而是critic将不可观测状态造成的变异误认为价值函数中的尖锐曲率，产生系统性偏差，引导actor远离最优。通过调整lookahead距离可以解除该偏差，而增加记忆观测却无济于事。这为设计更鲁棒的POMDP学习算法提供了明确方向。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [89] [The Sparsity Whisperer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06630)
*Linghao Kong,Inimai Subramanian,Micah Adler,Dan Alistarh,Dan Gutfreund,Nir Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类"差异感知"的剪枝方法（Wisp、Wisp+、Whisper），其核心思想是不仅保留激活值，还保留MLP层中上投影和门投影对相似输入产生不同输出的能力。Whisper使用轻正则化的差异Hessian作为二阶重建目标，在Llama 2/3.1（7B-405B）上持续优于SparseGPT等强基线，Wisp/Wisp+在约束场景下优于Wanda等激活感知基线，并可增强RIA和ALPS等更强技术，以极小代价扩展准确率-运行时间前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有剪枝方法主要保留大激活值或重建层输出，忽略了MLP上投影和门投影中某些对稀疏性特别敏感的神经元执行的关键计算——将相似的输入分离为不相似的输出。这一盲区导致现有准则未能保留此类输出差异信息。

Method: 提出了差异感知剪枝家族：（1）Wisp：一阶、无更新方法，使用输入差异范数对权重打分；（2）Wisp+：在神经元级别用每个神经元分离最强的输入对来细化Wisp的分数；（3）Whisper：二阶方法，使用轻正则化的差异Hessian作为重建目标。

Result: 在Llama 2和3.1（7B到405B参数）上，二阶变体（Whisper）持续优于强重建基线；无更新变体（Wisp/Wisp+）优于激活感知基线（如Wanda），尤其在受限场景下。改进在结构化稀疏、下游评估及其他模型家族中均成立；将差异感知准则增强到RIA和ALPS上可进一步改善准确率-运行时间权衡，且额外成本可忽略。

Conclusion: 保留输出差异是后训练LLM稀疏化中一种广泛适用且可组合的信号，为现有剪枝方法提供了新维度的补充。

Abstract: Pruning reduces the inference cost of large language models, but existing criteria primarily preserve large activations or reconstruct layer outputs. We argue that this overlooks a key computation performed by particularly sparsity-sensitive neurons in the MLP up and gate projections: separating similar inputs into dissimilar outputs. This suggests that effective pruning should preserve not only activations, but also the differences between outputs more broadly. We introduce a family of difference-informed pruning methods built upon this principle. Wisp is a first-order, update-free method that scores weights using input-difference norms, and Wisp+ refines this score neuronwise using the input pairs each neuron separates most strongly. Finally, Whisper is a second-order method that uses a lightly regularized difference Hessian as its reconstruction objective. Across Llama 2 and 3.1 models from 7B to 405B parameters, our second-order variant consistently improves over strong reconstruction-based baselines, while our update-free variants improve over activation-aware baselines, especially in constrained settings. The improvements over Wanda and SparseGPT extend to structured sparsity, downstream evaluations, and other model families. Augmenting stronger techniques such as RIA and ALPS with our difference-informed criteria yields further improvements, shifting the overall accuracy-runtime frontier outward at negligible additional cost. These results suggest that preserving output differences is a broadly useful and composable signal for post-training LLM sparsification.

</details>


### [90] [Cryptanalytic Extraction of Isolated Bias-Free GLU Feed-Forward Blocks by Antipodal Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06631)
*Chunhui Shi,Xinwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无偏置门控线性单元（GLU）前馈块的前向查询恢复原语，通过有限差分曲率提取门方向候选，并利用x与-x配对观测分离门幅度、方向与值分支耦合，在多种模型（Qwen六层、Llama 8,192单元子问题、Gemma全维块）上达到亚百分比级中位验证误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密码分析提取方法已覆盖ReLU网络、GELU/SiLU等分量激活网络及Transformer最终投影矩阵，但无法恢复现代语言模型中广泛使用的无偏置GLU前馈块——其内部每个隐藏单元将激活后的线性投影与第二个学习到的线性投影相乘，这种双分支结构超出了现有方法所处理的网络类别与最终层设置。

Method: 采用构造性、多阶段前向查询恢复原语：首先通过有限差分曲率提供门方向候选，然后在x与-x处的配对观测分离门幅度、方向与值分支耦合，逐步重建完整的无偏置GLU块参数。

Result: 在高精度目标（六个Qwen层、8,192单元Llama子问题、全维Gemma块）上均达到亚百分比中位验证误差；四种有限精度配置保持低于5%中位误差，但没有一种配置能复现所有存储权重。

Conclusion: 这些孤立块实验并非端到端模型API攻击，因为从最终模型输出推导所需内部块响应的问题仍未解决，需要在后续工作中进一步研究。

Abstract: Cryptanalytic extraction has been demonstrated for ReLU networks, for networks using componentwise activations such as GELU or SiLU, and for a Transformer's final projection matrix. These methods do not recover the bias-free Gated Linear Unit (GLU) feed-forward blocks used in many modern language models. Such a block multiplies an activated linear projection by a second learned linear projection within each hidden unit, a two-branch structure absent from the network classes and final-layer setting addressed by those methods. We give a constructive, multi-stage forward-query recovery primitive for isolated bias-free GLU blocks. Finite-difference curvature supplies gate-direction candidates, and paired observations at x and -x separate gate magnitude, orientation, and value-branch coupling. Across high-precision targets, six Qwen layers, an 8,192-unit Llama subproblem, and a full-dimensional Gemma block all reach sub-percent median validation error. Four finite-precision configurations remain below 5 percent median error, but none reproduces every stored weight. These isolated-block experiments are not an end-to-end model-API attack: deriving the required internal block responses from final model outputs remains unsolved.

</details>


### [91] [Bypassing Krum: Selection-Aware Backdoor Attacks in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06637)
*Srinivasan Subramanian,Md. Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan,Kazi Aminul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Krum-Proxy攻击，一种选择感知的后门注入策略，能够持续绕过Krum和Multi-Krum等基于距离的拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法。该方法通过两阶段优化程序构造恶意更新，使其不仅与良性更新相似，还被优化到聚合过程中受青睐的更新空间区域，同时通过投影机制保持隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Krum和Multi-Krum等距离型聚合规则假设良性更新形成紧凑簇，基于几何特性选择离多数最近的更新。然而这些几何特性可以被自适应对手利用，现有攻击方法（如朴素缩放或约束）不足以有效绕过这些鲁棒聚合机制。

Method: 提出Krum-Proxy攻击，核心是两阶段优化程序：第一阶段分离任务特定的攻击目标，第二阶段进行几何感知的精炼，使用最近邻代理、随机参考建模和锚点引导对齐来构造恶意更新。同时引入投影机制，将对抗更新约束在现实范数和方差范围内以保持隐蔽性。

Result: 在标准联邦学习基准上的实验表明，Krum-Proxy在保持干净准确率的同时实现了更高的攻击成功率，揭示了基于距离的聚合方法对选择感知对手的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 距离型拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法在面对选择感知的自适应攻击者时是不安全的。Krum-Proxy通过主动优化恶意更新进入良性分布的密集核心区域，持续绕过鲁棒聚合，表明仅依赖几何相似性假设的防御机制存在根本性漏洞。

Abstract: Robust aggregation methods are widely used in federated learning to mitigate the impact of adversarial client behavior. Distance-based aggregation rules, such as Krum and Multi-Krum, select updates that are closest to the majority under the assumption that benign updates form a compact cluster. However, these methods rely on geometric properties that can be exploited by adaptive adversaries. We introduce the Krum-Proxy attack, a selection-aware backdoor injection strategy that consistently bypasses Byzantine-robust aggregation. Rather than relying on naive scaling or constraining, our method actively optimizes malicious updates to infiltrate the dense core of the benign distribution. The proposed method constructs adversarial updates that are not only similar to benign updates but are also optimized to lie in regions of the update space that are favored during aggregation. This is achieved through a two-stage optimization procedure that separates task-specific attack objectives from geometry-aware refinement, using a nearest-neighbor proxy, stochastic reference modeling, and anchor-guided alignment. To maintain stealth, we introduce a projection mechanism that constrains adversarial updates within realistic norm and variance bounds. Experiments on standard federated learning benchmarks show that Krum-Proxy achieves higher attack success while preserving clean accuracy, highlighting the vulnerability of distance-based aggregation to selection-aware adversaries.

</details>


### [92] [Dirichlet Follow-the-Leader Closes the Gap in Simultaneous Multiclass U-Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06656)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个单行的预测器（基于Dirichlet分布的贝叶斯自助法），同时关闭了最优fragret率的两个gap：对任意有界proper loss达到最优的O(√KT) regret，对任意β-光滑proper loss达到O(β(1+log T))的regret，且无需事先知道损失类别或任何超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的self-concordant perturbation方法虽然取得了进展，但在有界proper loss上的regret存在K^{5/4}√T的维度gap，且在β-光滑损失上额外付出了β√K log K的代价。作者希望构造一个简单统一的predictor，同时达到两个最优率。

Method: 方法极其简洁：观察到类别计数c_{t-1}后，在已见过的类别面上从Dir(c_{t-1})分布中采样作为下一个预测。核心分析工具是一个精确恒等式：在Dir(α)下对任意有界proper loss求平均等价于对Dirichlet平均的Bayes风险的离散导数。这使be-the-perturbed-leader项telescope为一个非正的Jensen gap；再通过一步似然比（one-count likelihood ratio）将稳定性用该类计数的平方根倒数来界定。

Result: 单个无水平（horizon-free）算法同时满足：对任意有界proper loss，sup E[Reg] ≤ 4√(S_T T) ≤ 4√(KT)；对任意β-光滑proper loss，E[Reg] ≤ (5/2)β(1+log T)。其中S_T为观测到的类别数。已知下界表明两个rate在其非平凡区间内均为最优。

Conclusion: 该工作通过一个极简的贝叶斯自助法预测器同时闭合并优化了有界与光滑proper loss的regret gap，证明覆盖了不可微损失和活跃单纯形面变化的情况，理论结果达到已知下界，是最优的。

Abstract: Can one forecaster attain the optimal regret rate for every bounded proper loss and also adapt to every smooth proper loss? Recent work answered this up to a dimension gap. Its self-concordant perturbation gives roughly $K^{5/4}\sqrt{T}$ worst-case regret and incurs an additional $β\sqrt{K}\log K$ for $β$-smooth losses. We close both gaps with a one-line forecaster. After observing class counts $c_{t-1}$, draw the next prediction from $\operatorname{Dir}(c_{t-1})$, on the face of classes seen so far. This is a fresh Bayesian bootstrap of the outcomes. The analysis rests on an exact identity: averaging any bounded proper loss under $\operatorname{Dir}(α)$ equals a discrete derivative of its Dirichlet-averaged Bayes risk. The identity makes the be-the-perturbed-leader term telescope to a nonpositive Jensen gap. A one-count likelihood ratio then bounds stability by the inverse square root of that class's count. The resulting single, horizon-free algorithm satisfies $\sup_{\ell}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Reg}_{\ell}\leq 4\sqrt{S_T T}\leq 4\sqrt{K T}$ and $\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Reg}_{\ell}\leq \frac{5}{2}β(1+\log T)$ for every $β$-smooth proper loss. Here $S_T$ is the number of observed classes. Known lower bounds show that both rates are optimal in their nontrivial regimes. The proof covers nondifferentiable losses and changes of the active simplex face.

</details>


### [93] [EpiFlow: A framework for improving the utility of wastewater signals for disease forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06671)
*Aniruddha Adiga,Jingyuan Chou,Gursharn Kaur,Andrew Warren,Srinivasan Venkatramanan,Baltazar Espinoza,Bryan Lewis,Justin Crow,Alexandra Lorentz,Rekha Singh,Madhav Marathe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于废水的病毒载量（WVL）信号在传染病实时预测中的价值，提出了处理废水数据、刻画其与疾病负担指标关系的原则性方法，并通过时间变化预测模型验证了WVL在COVID-19住院预测中的改进效果，即使在低患病率或延迟报告条件下也能显著提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废水监测（WBS）是有效的疾病监控工具，但WVL与疾病负担相关，其改善实时预测的实际效用仍不明确。在疫情早期阶段，许多指标虽能有效监测疾病传播，但可能因报告疲劳和低患病率而可靠性下降；而在低患病率期间医院负担也可能大幅波动，因此需要准确的负担预测以减轻疾病影响。

Method: 1) 使用熵度量评估WVL的可预测性；2) 通过捕捉时间动态和领先指标行为的因果检验分析WVL与负担指标之间的关系；3) 将这些洞见整合进一个时间变化的预测模型，以适应信号间不断演变的关系；4) 通过模拟评估WVL报告延迟的影响。

Result: 通过对弗吉尼亚州及其卫生区在不同疾病流行期COVID-19住院数据的预测，纳入WVL相对于基线模型提高了预测精度，特别是在关键疫情阶段，预测覆盖率提升了20个百分点。结果表明即使在低患病率或延迟报告条件下，WVL信号仍能改善传染病预测。

Conclusion: WVL信号可以作为一种有价值的信息源来提升传染病实时预测的准确性，即使面临低患病率和报告延迟等挑战，废水监测数据仍有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Wastewater-based surveillance is an effective tool for disease monitoring and can provide early warning of outbreaks. Although wastewater viral loads (WVL) correlate with disease burden, their utility for improving real-time forecasting remains under investigation. During the early phases of an epidemic, many indicators can effectively monitor disease spread, but their reliability may decline because of reporting fatigue and low prevalence. Hospital burden can vary substantially even during low-prevalence periods, making accurate forecasting of burden indicators essential for minimizing disease impacts.
  In this paper, we present principled approaches for processing wastewater data, characterizing its relationship with burden indicators, and generating real-time forecasts. We assess the predictability of WVL using entropy measures. We analyze the relationship between WVL and burden indicators using causality tests that capture temporal dynamics and the leading-indicator behavior of WVL. We incorporate these insights into a time-varying forecasting model that accounts for the evolving relationship between the signals. We also evaluate the effects of delays in WVL reporting through simulations.
  We test the utility of our methods by forecasting COVID-19 hospital admissions across Virginia and its health regions during periods of varying disease prevalence. Incorporating WVL improves forecast accuracy relative to baseline models, particularly during critical epidemic phases, and results in a 20 percentage point improvement in forecast coverage. Our results demonstrate that WVL signals can improve infectious disease forecasting even under conditions of low prevalence or delayed reporting.

</details>


### [94] [A Transferable Autologistic Model for Predicting Rare Failures in Heterogeneous Equipment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06695)
*Islam Benamirouche,Djemel Ziou,Feriel Fass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"共性到目标"的概率模型，用于在异构设备族中预测罕见故障，通过共享跨设备故障模式并稀适应目标设备，显式建模传感器异质性、运行情境和退化动态，输出校准的故障概率估计以支持维护规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性维护中的故障预测面临三大挑战：故障事件罕见、同族设备传感器配置各异、目标应是提前预判而非诊断已发生故障。现有方法难以同时处理这些挑战，需要一种既能跨设备共享故障知识又能适配目标设备的建模方法。

Method: 提出一种共性到目标（common-to-target）的概率模型，该模型在异构设备族中学习共享的故障相关模式，并以稀疏方式适配到目标设备。模型显式整合了传感器异构性、运行情境和退化动力学，从而产生校准的故障概率估计，适用于维护规划决策。

Result: 在包含27台模拟冰箱的合成数据集上评估，该数据集包含变化的传感器配置、运行条件和故障类型，为模型性能提供了受控的评估环境。

Conclusion: 该模型有望在异构设备族中实现可靠的故障预判，通过共享学习与稀疏适配的平衡，产出可用于维护规划的校准概率估计。

Abstract: Predicting failures before they occur remains a major challenge in predictive maintenance, particularly when failures are rare, when equipment of the same family differ in sensor configurations, and when the goal is anticipation rather than diagnosis of an already observed fault. This paper proposes a common-to-target probabilistic model that learns shared failure-related patterns across a family of heterogeneous equipment and adapts parsimoniously to target equipment. The model explicitly accounts for sensor heterogeneity, operating context, and degradation dynamics to produce calibrated failureprobability estimates suitable for maintenance planning. Its performance is evaluated on a synthetic refrigerator dataset comprising 27 simulated refrigerators with varying sensor configurations, operating conditions, and failure types, providing a controlle

</details>


### [95] [Dueling World Models: Advantage-Style Action Channels for Common-Mode Distractor Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06706)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yiming Fei,Zhiruo Zhou,Heikichi Hayashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极简的潜在世界模型去偏方法：借鉴dueling网络中状态基线加动作优势的分解思想，在潜在动力学中减去动作对预测的均值效应，从而分离出纯粹的、可控制的动作通道，消除了动作无关的干扰因素，无需奖励、重建或辅助损失，适用于任何动作条件世界模型（包括冻结的预训练模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型在预测未来状态时，当场景中存在不受智能体控制的运动（distractors）时会发生"动作失明"现象——不同动作的预测变得不可区分，即使训练损失持续下降。现有的解决方法（重建、任务奖励、辅助目标）都引入了额外的机制或假设，作者希望找到一种极简的替代方案。

Method: 借鉴dueling网络将价值函数分解为状态基线（V）和动作优势（A）的思想，在潜在动力学预测中，对给定状态的预测在不同动作上取平均值作为基线，然后从每个动作的预测中减去该均值。这恰好抵消了所有动作共享的、动作无关的变化（干扰因素所在），保留纯动作相关的通道。该操作仅在读出时做一次减法，因此可以无改动地应用于任何动作条件世界模型，包括冻结的预训练模型。

Result: 在网格世界、具有已知因子的合成生成器、干扰连续控制和自然像素Atari等环境中，该隔离通道在纠缠预测器失效的情况下恢复智能体自身的效应，干扰泄漏与零不可区分；事后应用于现成模型时，能突显其原始读出未能显示的动作通道，并在网格世界中转化为目标到达控制。作者还证明了在离散和采样动作集的有限样本中该消除是精确的，并给出了适用边界（干扰运动与动作相关的场景）。

Conclusion: 仅通过读出时的均值减法，即可在潜在世界模型中实现动作与干扰的干净分离，无需奖励、重建或辅助损失，该方法通用性强、实现简洁，且有理论保证。

Abstract: Latent world models plan by predicting future states from an action, but when a scene contains motion the agent does not control, they quietly go action-blind: predictions for different actions become indistinguishable even as the training loss keeps improving. Existing remedies suppress this distraction with reconstruction, task reward, or auxiliary objectives, each adding machinery or assumptions. We show that a minimal alternative suffices, borrowed from the dueling decomposition of value into a state baseline and an action advantage: in latent dynamics, subtracting a prediction's mean effect over actions cancels whatever the actions share--the action-independent variation where distractors live--leaving a clean, controllable channel, with no reward, no reconstruction, and no distractor-specific auxiliary loss. Because this is only a subtraction at readout time, it applies unchanged to any action-conditioned world model, including frozen pretrained ones. Across a gridworld, synthetic generators with known factors, distracting continuous control, and natural-pixel Atari, the isolated channel recovers the agent's own effect where entangled predictors fail, with nuisance leak indistinguishable from zero; applied post hoc it surfaces an action channel in off-the-shelf models that their raw readouts miss, and it converts into goal-reaching control in the gridworld. We prove the cancellation is exact in finite samples for both discrete and sampled action sets, and we state its measured boundary--distractors whose motion tracks the action--together with the remaining limitations in the appendix.

</details>


### [96] [Multi-Level Modeling of Large Language Model Inference Latency and Energy via Hybrid Analytical--Machine-Learning Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06723)
*Saeid Shokoufa,Mohammad Erfan Sadeghi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYMELL是一个混合三层框架，结合解析建模与机器学习来估算LLM推理的延迟和能耗，在NVIDIA H100 GPU上对LLaMA 3 8B的prefill和decode阶段均实现低于5%的误差，支持多种架构（稠密/MoE FFN、MHA/GQA）并实现无需硬件的快速设计空间探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的快速扩展带来了显著的计算成本、能耗和推理延迟问题，精确的估算对于可持续AI部署和硬件感知设计至关重要。

Method: 提出HYMELL混合三层框架：(1) 解析建模估计基本操作； (2) 机器学习预测高层组件； (3) 端到端模型捕获系统级开销，覆盖prefill和decode阶段。支持稠密和MoE FFN，以及MHA和GQA注意力机制。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上评估，HYMELL达到高预测精度，对LLaMA 3 8B的prefill和decode两个阶段误差均低于5%。

Conclusion: HYMELL能够直接从架构参数预测执行成本，实现快速、无需硬件的设计空间探索和能效优化。

Abstract: The rapid scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) has significantly increased computational cost, energy consumption, and inference latency, making accurate estimation essential for sustainable artificial intelligence deployment and hardware-aware design. In this work, we introduce Hybrid Modeling for Energy and Latency of LLMs (HYMELL), a hybrid three-level framework for estimating LLM inference latency and energy by combining analytical modeling with machine learning (ML). HYMELL models LLM execution through a three-level hierarchy: analytical estimation of primitive operations, ML prediction of higher-level components, and an end-to-end model that captures system-level overheads across both prefill and decode phases. The framework supports diverse architectures, including dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) feed-forward networks (FFNs), as well as multi-head attention (MHA) and grouped-query attention (GQA) mechanisms. Evaluated on an NVIDIA H100 graphics processing unit (GPU), HYMELL achieves high predictive accuracy; notably, for LLaMA 3 8B, it attains less than 5% error for both prefill and decode phases. By predicting execution costs directly from architectural parameters, it enables fast, hardware-free design space exploration and energy-efficient optimization.

</details>


### [97] [Solver-Guided Reasoning for Mixed-Equilibrium Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06741)
*Han Wang,Philippe Beardsell,Boning Li,Aaron Sasmita,Shuai Li,Hongyuan Zha,Baoxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略决策树（MDT），将博弈求解器的均衡解转化为稀疏的策略规则，以替代人类数据来提升大语言模型在复杂博弈中的均衡推理能力。在No-Limit Texas Hold'em上，通过查询求解器获得超过2.5亿个混合策略决策，MDT将LLM到均衡的ℓ1距离减少了52.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的推理通常基于人类文本、演示和生成的推理链进行接地，但在复杂博弈的均衡推理中，人类数据往往是次优的。人类玩家常受直觉和启发式指导，会显著偏离博弈均衡，尤其是在混合策略均衡的博弈中，人类数据严重偏向纯策略。因此，直接用人类数据训练LLM会得到较弱的博弈策略，需要新的方法来赋予LLM均衡推理能力。

Method: 提出Mixed-Strategy Decision Tree（MDT），将均衡的隐性最优性转化为人类和LLM都能理解的稀疏策略规则。核心思想是使用博弈求解器（solver）的输出而非人类标注，从而可以扩展到任意新的状态和延续。论文在No-Limit Texas Hold'em上实例化该方法，通过查询求解器获取超过2.5亿个混合策略决策。

Result: MDT结合其他技术，在8种不同的LLM配置上将到均衡的ℓ1距离减少了52.6%。还通过Route-only消融实验验证了基于阴影对比的增量贡献，并通过完整的River-endgame和Liar's Dice实验评估了策略保真度和超越原始NLH通信设置的迁移性。

Conclusion: 使用求解器输出而非人类数据可以有效提升LLM在复杂博弈中的均衡推理能力。MDT方法通过将均衡解转化为可理解的稀疏策略规则，大幅缩小了LLM与博弈均衡之间的距离，并具备良好的策略保真度和跨游戏设置的通用性。

Abstract: Reasoning in large language models (LLMs) is often grounded in human text, human demonstrations, and human-generated rationales. For equilibrium reasoning in complex games, however, relying on human data can be suboptimal. In fact, human play is often guided by intuition and heuristics and can deviate substantially from game equilibrium. This discrepancy is amplified in games with mixed-strategy equilibria, where human data is heavily biased toward pure strategies. Consequently, conditioning LLMs on this data yields weak game strategies. To grant LLMs the reasoning capacity in games, in this work, we study how to elicit equilibrium play using solver output. We propose Mixed-Strategy Decision Tree (MDT), which articulates the silent optimality of the equilibrium into sparse strategic rules that both humans and LLMs could understand. Using solver output rather than human annotation allows us to extend the input to arbitrarily new states and continuations. We instantiate this study on No-Limit Texas Hold'em by querying a solver oracle for over \textbf{250 million mixed-strategy decisions}; MDT together with other techniques \textbf{reduces the $\ell_1$ distance to the equilibrium by $52.6\%$} across $8$ different LLM configurations. A Route-only ablation tests the incremental contribution of the shadow-based contrast, while complete River-endgame and Liar's Dice experiments evaluate strategic fidelity and portability beyond the original NLH communication setting.

</details>


### [98] [KReF: Training-Free Retrieval for Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting and Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06748)
*Yang Zhang,Rui Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KReF是一种无需训练的检索式框架，将检索到的历史未来序列视为查询局部的经验预测分布，通过相似度权重直接定义预测分位数、CRPS和点预测，在六个LTSF基准上取得最优或领先的CRPS与IS90，确立了检索作为LTSF有效且被低估的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率长期时间序列预测依赖训练模型，而无需训练的保形方法通常只围绕已有定点预测构建区间，无法完整表示预测分布，且序列化变体在长时域上存在反馈延迟问题。因此需要一种无需梯度训练、能完整表达预测分布并且适应长期预测的替代方案。

Method: KReF采用检索框架：先对数据进行稳健预处理，然后用手工统计量或冻结的随机傅里叶特征对每个回看窗口进行嵌入，检索相似的"回看-未来"历史对；检索相似度权重直接构成经验预测分布，可推导出预测分位数、CRPS和加权均值点预测；此外利用观测到的查询回看构建概率积分变换映射，并通过验证集选取的扩张/收缩率来调整区间边界。

Result: 在六个LTSF基准和四个预测时域上，KReF在所有12个数据集-嵌入设置中取得最低CRPS，在9个设置中取得最低IS90；其点预测在两个数据集上匹配或超越训练基线；存档-预言机分析显示在更细粒度时域和通道路由下仍有显著的改进空间。

Conclusion: 检索是一种有用且尚待深入探索的LTSF归纳偏置，KReF证明了无需训练即可获得具有竞争力的概率预测，并揭示了细粒度路由等方向存在进一步优化的潜力。

Abstract: Probabilistic long-term time-series forecasting commonly relies on trained models. Training-free conformal methods typically construct intervals around a pre-existing point forecaster and do not natively represent a complete predictive distribution; sequential variants additionally suffer from increasingly delayed feedback at long horizons. We propose KReF, a training-free retrieval framework that treats retrieved historical futures as a querylocal empirical predictive distribution. After robust preprocessing, KReF embeds each lookback using handcrafted statistics or frozen random Fourier features and retrieves similar historical lookback-future pairs. Their similarity weights directly define predictive masses, quantiles, CRPS, and a weighted-mean point forecast. KReF further uses the observed query lookback to construct a probability-integral-transform map and applies validation-selected expansion and shrinkage rates to adapt interval boundaries. Across six LTSF benchmarks and four horizons, KReF obtains the lowest CRPS in all 12 dataset-embedding settings and the lowest IS90 in 9 settings. Without gradient-based fitting, its point forecasts also match or surpass trained baselines on two of six datasets. An archive-oracle analysis further reveals substantial headroom under finer horizon- and channel-wise routing. These results establish retrieval as a useful and underexplored inductive bias for LTSF.

</details>


### [99] [Sub-Quadratic Bisimulation Metrics via Approximate Nearest Neighbors: Coverage-Augmented Guarantees and Computable Two-Sided Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06762)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证书的子二次复杂度双模拟度量计算方法，利用近似最近邻索引选择由精确受限算子更新的状态对，并通过单调上下界包围精确度量。核心分析结果是覆盖增强的任意时刻界：局部索引质量无法单独控制全局误差，因为未覆盖的状态对保留初始化差距。极限误差至多为max(ρ, ε_op/(1−γ))，且精确覆盖备份下下界满足‖d_ann−d‖_∞=ρ。算法返回可观测的夹心宽度作为证书，上下聚类的一致可证明覆盖聚合的精确恢复。实验验证了精确受限算子下的恒等式和包围性质，以及二次与子二次的扩展行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双模拟度量用于量化马尔可夫决策过程中的行为相似性，但其Wasserstein不动点算子每次更新所有状态对，产生二次复杂度（每轮需要O(|S|^2)的成对计算），对于大规模MDP不具可扩展性。现有方法缺乏严格的错误保证和可验证的收敛证书。

Method: 提出证书携带的子二次方法：(1) 利用近似最近邻索引选择精确受限算子需要更新的状态对；(2) 单调上下界运行在每个扫描轮次包围精确度量；(3) 核心分析给出覆盖增强的任意时刻界，将误差分解为局部索引质量项ρ和未覆盖项ε_op/(1−γ)；(4) 返回可观测的夹心宽度作为证书，通过上下聚类的吻合证明覆盖聚合的精确恢复；(5) 提供奖励无关的覆盖下界和自适应下界来证明方法的必要条件。

Result: 实验显示：在|S|=64的分组基准上，精确受限细化在检索覆盖约一半状态对时达到精确度量极限；独立训练的MICo和DBC基线在所有检索预算下均比该极限高22-33倍。Taxi例子展示了无信息嵌入下证书的弃权行为，而2500状态网格世界使用单次二次扫描的12.8%计算量，比仅奖励度量改进28.6%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可扩展双模拟度量计算的第一个严格子二次框架：通过证书机制保证聚合结果的可靠性，证明了覆盖项不可避免的下界，并展示了在实际基准上的显著效率提升。方法是首选的，因为它在降低计算复杂度的同时保留了精确度量的可验证逼近保证。

Abstract: Bisimulation metrics quantify behavioral similarity in Markov decision processes, but their Wasserstein fixed-point operator updates every state pair and incurs quadratic pairwise work. We give a certificate-carrying sub-quadratic method for MDPs with bounded transition support and a useful low-dimensional indexing representation: an approximate-nearest-neighbor index selects the pairs updated by the exact restricted operator, while monotone lower and upper runs enclose the exact metric at every sweep. The main analytical result is a coverage-augmented anytime bound: local index quality alone cannot control global error, because uncovered pairs retain their initialization gap. The limiting error is at most $\max(ρ,\eop/(1-γ))$, and with exact covered backups the lower arm satisfies $\|\dann-d\|_\infty=ρ$. Because $ρ$ depends on the unknown exact metric, the algorithm returns the observable sandwich width instead; agreement of the induced lower and upper clusterings certifies exact recovery of the covered aggregation. A reward-oblivious lower bound shows sub-quadratic index-first coverage cannot remove the coverage term, while a separate adaptive lower bound requires $Ω(|\Scal|)$ pair evaluations. Exact-operator experiments verify the identity and enclosure in every seeded run, and timing experiments recover quadratic versus sub-quadratic scaling under both cheap and full Wasserstein backups. On the grouped $|\Scal|=64$ benchmark, exact restricted refinement reaches the exact-metric skyline once retrieval covers roughly half of all pairs, while independently trained MICo and DBC baselines stay $22$-$33\times$ above that skyline at every retrieval budget. Taxi shows the certificate abstaining under an uninformative embedding, while a $2500$-state gridworld improves over a reward-only metric by $28.6\%$ using $12.8\%$ of one quadratic sweep.

</details>


### [100] [CubicQuant: Parametric Non-Uniform Codebooks for High-Throughput LLM Inference with 1-8-Bit Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06763)
*Xuetian Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CubicQuant是一种参数化非均匀标量量化格式，通过单调三次曲线将均匀间隔的码映射到非均匀重建层级，在保持稠密整数码流的基础上，在每个权重组内自适应调整重建层级，兼顾了表示的灵活性和GPU执行的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理的权重量化需要在自适应重建层级与足够规则以高效执行GPU之间取得平衡。均匀整数量化约束过强，低比特浮点格式结构固定，学习式码本灵活但解码不规则且元数据开销大。需要一种既灵活又规则、可直接在GPU上高效执行的量化格式。

Method: 提出CubicQuant，一种参数化非均匀标量量化格式。通过两个形状参数和一个尺度参数定义的单调三次曲线，将均匀间隔的幅度码映射到非均匀重建层级。该格式支持1-8比特权重负载，以均匀整数量化作为精确特例，有效位宽为B+64/G比特。推导了均匀、高斯、拉普拉斯分布下的总体失真，制定了连续和Dynamic-A8感知的拟合目标，并描述了直接打包权重的GPU执行方案。

Result: 在G=128、每分布15360个样本的有限组实验中，W4 CubicQuant相对最优裁剪的四比特均匀整数量化，在均匀、高斯、拉普拉斯样本上分别降低了3.90%、13.49%、28.14%的重建RMSE；相对最优枚举的四比特有限浮点格式，分别降低了3.90%、9.44%、6.27%。初步H200内核测量显示工作量相关的交叉点：窄GEMV下模型dtype执行更快，行数增长时Dynamic A8更优。

Conclusion: CubicQuant格式展示了表示能力和直接可执行性的双重优势，在下游模型质量和跨设备端到端性能方面仍有待进一步评估。

Abstract: Weight quantization for large-language-model inference must balance adaptive reconstruction levels with representations regular enough for efficient GPU execution. Uniform integers constrain each group to a linear grid. Low-bit floating-point formats use a fixed exponent-mantissa structure, while learned codebooks gain flexibility at the cost of irregular decoding and additional metadata.
  We introduce CubicQuant, a parametric non-uniform scalar format that preserves a dense integer code stream while adapting reconstruction levels within each weight group. A monotonic cubic curve, specified by two shape parameters and one scale, maps uniformly spaced magnitude codes to non-uniform levels. The family spans 1-8-bit weight payloads, contains symmetric uniform integer quantization as an exact special case, and has effective width B + 64/G bits per weight for payload width B and group size G. We derive population distortion under Uniform, Gaussian, and Laplace distributions, formulate continuous and Dynamic-A8-carrier-aware fitting objectives, and describe direct packed-weight GPU execution.
  For finite groups of G=128 with 15,360 samples per distribution, W4 CubicQuant reduced reconstruction RMSE relative to optimally clipped four-bit uniform integer quantization by 3.90% on Uniform, 13.49% on Gaussian, and 28.14% on Laplace samples. Relative to the best enumerated four-bit finite floating-point format, the reductions were 3.90%, 9.44%, and 6.27%. Preliminary H200 kernel measurements show a workload-dependent crossover: model-dtype execution is faster for narrow GEMV, while Dynamic A8 becomes favorable as row count grows. The results establish the format's representational promise and direct executability; downstream model quality and cross-device end-to-end performance remain open evaluation questions.

</details>


### [101] [Hidden Gauge Controls Feature Specialization in ReLU Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06766)
*Tongxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究过参数化ReLU网络中神经元特征所有权（feature ownership）的可控性问题。在固定的初始预测函数下，仅改变正齐次缩放规范（scaling gauge）即可确定哪个重复神经元获得教师特征，导致Θ(D²)级别的专业化时间分离，并证明该效应源于系数与方向变化的不同迁移率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化ReLU网络中，多个神经元的初始功能角色相同，但最终只有一个获得教师特征，其余变得冗余。作者提出了一个根本性问题：这个"特征所有权"（哪个神经元被选中）能否由初始预测器不可见的参数选择来控制？这挑战了"初始函数决定学习动力学"的直觉。

Method: 采用高斯教师-学生模型作为可解框架。固定完整初始函数，仅变化正齐次缩放规范（gauge），形成相反的规范。分析相反规范下特征轨迹的分离，推导出专业化时间的Θ(D²)分离。发展精确的反应-传输（reaction-transport）分解，将效应归因于特征系数变化与方向变化的不同迁移率。理论证明全局选择和功能剪枝，并扩展到可见扰动和小步全批量梯度下降。

Result: 反相规范产生截然不同的特征轨迹和尖锐的Θ(D²)专业化时间分离，这种分离无法通过全局时间变换消除。在任意固定数量的初始重复学生中，将有利规范赋予某一神经元可确定性选择其为所有者，并将其余功能贡献驱至零。在总体和有限样本训练中，预测的损失、对齐、剪枝和耗散轨迹均得到验证。

Conclusion: 初始预测函数既不能决定特征何时被学习，也不能决定哪个神经元来学习它。特征所有权可由规范（gauge）这一初始预测器不可见的参数控制，这揭示了过参数化网络学习动力学中一个此前未被认识的控制自由度。

Abstract: Training changes a network's predictions while allocating task-relevant structure across its internal units. In an overparameterized ReLU network, several neurons can begin with exactly the same functional role, yet one may acquire a teacher feature while the others become redundant. We call the identity of that neuron feature ownership and ask whether it can be controlled by a parameter choice invisible to the initial predictor. In a tractable Gaussian teacher--student model, we fix the complete initial function and vary only a positive-homogeneous scaling gauge. Opposite gauges produce distinct feature trajectories and a sharp $Θ(D^2)$ separation in specialization time that no global change of clock can explain. Among any fixed number of initially duplicate students, assigning the favorable gauge to one neuron deterministically selects it as the owner and drives the remaining functional contribution to zero. An exact reaction--transport decomposition attributes the effect to different mobilities for changing a feature's coefficient and direction. We prove global selection and functional pruning, extend finite-time selection to visible perturbations and small-step full-batch gradient descent, and verify the predicted loss, alignment, pruning, and dissipation trajectories in population and finite-sample training. The initial predictor therefore determines neither when the feature is learned nor which neuron learns it.

</details>


### [102] [ArchEGraph: A Large-Scale Graph Dataset for Geometry-Topology-Physics Aligned Building Energy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06772)
*Yihui Li,Yihui Chen,Kaidi Zha,Xiaoyue Yan,Zhexuan Yu,Shiqi Dai,Jun Xiao,Jun Yin,Ramon Elias Weber,Borong Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ArchEGraph，一个大规模基准数据集，将建筑物表示为具有对齐的几何、拓扑、天气和区域级热负荷的异构图，包含5,481栋建筑、49,326个建筑-天气模拟案例、超过133,000个空间节点和144万个面节点，并定义了图重建和拓扑感知负荷预测两个基准任务及标准化评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗的准确估计对实现碳中和和可持续建筑至关重要，但现有研究缺乏将建筑几何显式映射到性能的大规模数据集，难以支持设计决策分析及机器学习模型的校准。

Method: ArchEGraph将建筑表示为异构图，包含几何、拓扑、天气和区域级热负荷信息。定义了(i)图重建任务（从多边形网格恢复拓扑结构）和(ii)拓扑感知负荷预测任务（利用图结构和时序天气条件预测区域级响应时间序列），并设计了跨建筑和跨气候的泛化实验及标准化评估协议。

Result: 数据集涵盖5,481栋建筑和49,326个验证过的建筑-天气模拟案例，总计超过133,000个空间节点和144万个面节点，展示了显著的几何和拓扑复杂性，为跨建筑和跨气候泛化实验提供了统一的测试平台。

Conclusion: ArchEGraph为研究建筑能量建模中的几何-拓扑-物理耦合提供了统一测试平台，支持可扩展且可泛化的替代模型的开发与评估，有助于为建筑师和工程师提供快速设计反馈。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of building energy use is essential for achieving carbon neutral and sustainable buildings. To better understand the influence of design decisions on building energy use and calibrate machine learning models that can give architects and engineers rapid design feedback, large-scale datasets are needed that explicitly map building geometry to performance. We present ArchEGraph, a large-scale benchmark dataset that represents buildings as heterogeneous graphs with aligned geometry, topology, weather, and zone-level thermal loads. The dataset contains 5,481 buildings and 49,326 validated building-weather simulation cases. In total, it includes over 133,000 space nodes and 1.44 million face nodes, reflecting substantial geometric and topological complexity. Based on ArchEGraph, we define two benchmark tasks: (i) graph reconstruction from polygonal meshes, aiming to recover topological structure from geometric representations; and (ii) topology-informed load prediction, which leverages graph structure and temporal weather conditions to forecast zone-level response time series. We further introduce standardized evaluation protocols for both tasks and conduct cross-building and cross-climate generalization experiments to assess model robustness. ArchEGraph provides a unified testbed for studying geometry-topology-physics coupling in building energy modeling, enabling the development and evaluation of scalable and generalizable surrogate models.

</details>


### [103] [Faster Query-Key Learning Sharpens Attention in Self-Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06776)
*Rahul Vashisht,Harish G. Ramaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了单层自注意力模型中查询-键电路（query-key）和输出-值电路（output-value）的不同参数化方式如何影响注意力模式。通过梯度流分析，证明了因子化（factorization）参数化会隐式地重缩放两个电路的学习率，从而影响注意力集中的锐利程度，同时保持相近的训练损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自注意力层由两个相互作用的电路组成：控制注意力分配的查询-键电路，以及将注意力表示映射到预测的输出-值电路。不同的参数化方式（坍缩与因子化）会导致定性不同的注意力模式，但此前缺乏对这些差异背后机制的系统理解。

Method: 对单层自注意力模型在下一次词元预测任务上进行梯度流分析。推导了输出-值和查询-键参数沿直线移动的闭式动力学，揭示了因子化导致两个电路学习率隐式重缩放的机制，并通过实验验证相对学习率差异对注意力集中的影响。

Result: 因子化参数化会隐式地改变两个电路的学习率；相对于输出-值电路，查询-键电路的学习速度越快，模型会通过增加对相关词元的注意力权重来补偿较慢的输出-值学习，从而产生更锐利的注意力分布。实验证实两个电路的相对学习率差异主导注意力集中程度，同时保持可比的预测性能。

Conclusion: 查询-键电路与输出-值电路之间的相对学习率是决定注意力锐利度的关键因素。通过选择合适的参数化方式，可以在保持预测性能的同时提升注意力可解释性代理指标。

Abstract: A standard self-attention layer consists of two interacting circuits: the query-key circuit that governs attention allocation, and the output-value circuit that maps attended representations to predictions. Collapsed and factorized parameterizations of the query-key and output-value circuits lead to qualitatively different attention patterns. In particular, some parameterizations give sharper attention to task-relevant tokens, at a similar training loss. We analyze how the parameterizations of these circuits shape the parameter trajectories in single-layer self-attention models trained for next-token prediction. Through gradient-flow analysis, we show that factorization induces implicit rescaling of the two circuits' learning rates. We derive closed-form dynamics showing that output-value and query-key parameters move along a line, with relative speeds determined by their learning rates. Faster query-key learning relative to output-value learning thus produces sharper attention, as the model compensates for slower output-value learning by increasing attention mass on relevant tokens. Experiments show that differences in the relative learning rates of the two circuits govern attention concentration. This improves attention interpretability proxies while maintaining comparable predictive performance.

</details>


### [104] [Understanding Differentiable Embeddings Through Differential and Integral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06809)
*Xinyu Zhang,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，证明投影字形、地图连续性评分和基于传输的分析等非线性降维诊断方法都源自同一个由可微嵌入诱导的几何对象，该框架包含微分视角（一阶恢复投影字形、二阶曲率量化线性近似可靠性）和积分视角（沿高维路径跟踪几何，检测路径依赖不一致性），并证明地图连续性是其他分析的前提、积分视角不可化简为任何局部测量，实验验证了曲率信任估计和路径依赖检测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非线性降维嵌入的诊断方法（投影字形、地图连续性评分、输运分析）看似互不相关，缺乏统一理论框架来理解它们何时一致、何时不一致，分析者难以判断嵌入是否可信任。

Method: 提出由每个可微嵌入（无论是隐式优化定义还是显式学习映射）诱导的单一几何对象作为统一框架基础。该框架包含两个互补几何视角：微分视角（一阶项恢复投影字形，二阶曲率量化线性近似的可靠性范围）和积分视角（沿高维路径跟踪同一几何，判断嵌入仅依赖当前状态还是依赖到达路径）。同时证明地图连续性是其他分析的前提条件，并证明积分视角不可被任何局部分析（任意点数、任意阶导数）所还原。

Result: 理论上证明框架对源自嵌入几何的诊断是完备的，且积分视角不可约。在合成与真实数据集上验证理论预测，曲率信任估计在单细胞嵌入上表现准确，积分分析能以现有逐点诊断无法做到的方式区分单值嵌入与路径依赖的优化式嵌入。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性降维诊断提供了统一理论框架，将看似无关的现有方法纳入同一几何体系，阐明了它们之间的逻辑关系（地图连续性为前提），并揭示了积分视角的独特且不可替代的诊断能力。

Abstract: How can an analyst decide whether a nonlinear dimensionality reduction embedding can be trusted? Existing diagnostics provide only partial answers: projection glyphs characterize local sensitivity, map-continuity scores measure local conditioning, and transport-based analyses reveal path-dependent inconsistencies. However, these methods appear unrelated and provide no common framework for understanding when they agree or not. We show that they are all derived from a single geometric object induced by every differentiable embedding, whether defined implicitly through optimization or explicitly by a learned mapping. This framework provides two complementary geometric views of an embedding. The differential view explains local behavior: its first-order term recovers projection glyphs, while its second-order curvature quantifies how far their linear approximation remains reliable. The integral view follows the same geometry along high dimensional paths and determines whether an embedding depends only on the current state or also on the path taken to reach it. We further show that map-continuity is a prerequisite for the other analyses. The framework is theoretically complete for diagnostics derived from the embedding geometry, and we prove the integral view irreducible: no amount of local measurement at any number of points, to any order of derivative, reproduces what it detects. Classical rank-based metrics form a complementary class based on finite-scale neighborhood relationships. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets validate theoretical predictions, demonstrate accurate curvature-based trust estimates on single-cell embeddings, and show that the integral analysis distinguishes single-valued embeddings from path-dependent optimization-based embeddings in ways that existing pointwise diagnostics cannot.

</details>


### [105] [Multiscale Reward Hedging from Correct Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06825)
*Pahan Dewasurendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了连续奖励类下从正确示范中学习的无水平线（horizon-free）保证。核心方法是在每个精度尺度上对容忍最优性测试进行共享投票对冲，从而得到同时尾界，并通过度量熵积分累积隐藏间隔，独立于轮数。多项式熵下得到O(d log A)总间隔和O(d/m)统计率；对有限维线性上下文推荐，任意紧凑菜单获得O(d)遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从正确示范中学习比监督学习更困难，因为预测后学习者只能看到一个有效的正确答案，却不知道自己的答案是否有效，也没有任何奖励信号。现有奖励对冲保证都假设奖励类是有限的，本文的目标是给出连续奖励类的无水平线保证，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 关键创新是在每个精度尺度上对容忍最优性测试进行单一共享投票（one shared vote over tolerant optimality tests）的对冲策略。目标奖励在每个尺度有一个幸存的代理（proxy），预测间隔超过该尺度的预测会使代理加倍。这产生了同时尾界|{t:ℓ_t>2^{-j}}|≤log₂N(G,2^{-j-1})+j，其中G为最优性间隔函数类。通过对尾部的积分，累积隐藏间隔被度量熵积分所界定，与轮数无关。

Result: 多项式熵(A/ε)^d给出O(d log A)总间隔和O(d/m)快速统计率；有限维线性上下文的任意紧凑菜单获得O(d)遗憾，这是首个无需菜单结构限制的有限多项式界（代价是不适当预测）。一维Lipschitz参数曲线下投票可多项式时间计算；固定半径的秩二推荐只需O(KT²)时间。另有Ω(d)下界、低秩和有界ReLU网络推论，以及仅增加示范者累计次优性的鲁棒定理。MovieLens审计在1.7秒内完成，改善了平均潜在间隔。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连续奖励类下从示范学习的首个无水平线保证，通过尺度化容忍测试的共享投票对冲策略实现了与轮数无关的累积间隔界。该框架在多类场景中产生可计算的算法，实验验证了尺度自适应行为，为从示范学习提供了更一般的理论保障。

Abstract: Learning from correct demonstrations is harder than supervised learning when many answers are correct: after predicting, the learner sees one valid answer but not whether its own answer was valid, nor any reward. Existing reward-hedging guarantees consequently assume a finite reward class. We give the first horizon-free guarantee for continuous classes. The key is to hedge in one shared vote over tolerant optimality tests at every accuracy scale. A target reward has one surviving proxy per scale, and a prediction with gap above that scale doubles the proxy. This yields the simultaneous tail bound $|\{t:\ell_t>2^{-j}\}|\leq \log_2\mathcal N(\mathcal G,2^{-j-1})+j$, where $\mathcal G$ is the class of optimality-gap functions. Integrating the tails gives cumulative hidden gap bounded by a metric-entropy integral, independently of the number of rounds. Polynomial entropy $(A/ε)^d$ gives $O(d\log A)$ total gap and a fast $O(d/m)$ statistical rate. For bounded linear contextual recommendation, the result is $O(d)$ regret for arbitrary compact menus. This is the first polynomial finite bound without structural restrictions on the menus, at the price of improper prediction. Although the general vote can be expensive, it is exactly polynomial-time for one-dimensional Lipschitz parameter curves. Fixed-radius rank-two recommendation takes $O(KT^2)$ time for menus of size $K$. We also prove an $Ω(d)$ lower bound, low-rank and bounded ReLU-network corollaries, and a robust theorem that adds only the demonstrator's cumulative suboptimality. A reproducible adaptive stress test illustrates the predicted scale adaptation. After factorization, an exact MovieLens audit runs in 1.7 CPU seconds across ten users and improves mean latent gap over both a demonstrated-rating policy and a proper online baseline. The learner uses only action demonstrations and never observes a reward or a loss.

</details>


### [106] [Graph Machine: Exploring Edge Mechanisms as an Inductive Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06834)
*Lintai Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph Machine架构，通过两种显式边机制（边增强注意力与以边为中心的引用）为Transformer引入更强的迭代遍历潜在关系的归纳偏置，在数独任务中优于Transformer基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在全局基于内容的匹配上表现强大，但推理问题可能受益于更强的对潜在关系进行迭代遍历的归纳偏置，而现有Transformer缺乏显式的边机制来建模和动态更新关系图。

Method: Graph Machine架构包含两种显式基于边的机制：1）边增强注意力（Edge-augmented attention），边用于调制注意力；2）以边为中心的引用（edge-centric referral），节点交换地址以更新边。该模型能够跨层动态且可微地构建和修正关系图。

Result: 在受控的数独设置下，Graph Machine优于Transformer基线；消融研究和机制分析将性能增益归因于边机制。模型还意外发现了一种针对数独几何结构的紧凑的基于边的构建方式。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式边机制作为一种有前景的架构设计方向，值得在更广泛的任务上进行进一步评估。

Abstract: Transformers provide a powerful architecture for global content-based matching, but reasoning problems may benefit from a stronger inductive bias toward iterative traversal of latent relations. We introduce Graph Machine, an architecture with two explicit edge-based mechanisms: Edge-augmented attention, in which edges modulate attention between nodes, and edge-centric referral, in which nodes exchange addresses to update their edges. Conceptually, this enables the model to dynamically and differentiably construct and revise relational graphs across layers. We study this inductive bias using Sudoku under controlled settings and find that Graph Machine outperforms Transformer baselines, with ablation studies and mechanistic analysis attributing the gains to the edge mechanisms. Surprisingly, we found that the model discovers a compact edge-based construction for Sudoku geometry. Our results support explicit edge mechanisms as a promising architectural design, motivating broader evaluation.

</details>


### [107] [Mathematical Principles and Experimental Discoveries of the Emergence of Symbolic Patterns in Artificial Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06839)
*Quanshi Zhang,Qihan Ren,Siyu Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一大类神经网络在不同任务上的推理逻辑可以被重构为稀疏符号交互模式，并证明了两个普适的数学准则驱动了这种符号模式的涌现，为ANN的可解释性和泛化能力提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络通常被视为黑盒模型，可解释性是深度学习中的核心挑战。虽然已有多种工程方法从特征归因、可视化等角度近似解释ANN，但能否将ANN复杂的推理逻辑穷尽且简洁地表达为稀疏符号模式仍然是一个长期未解的问题。更深层的疑问是：符号模式的涌现反映的是自然规律还是偶然现象？

Method: 作者首先提出假设：ANN的推理逻辑可以被重新表述为稀疏符号交互。然后从理论上证明两个跨任务隐含要求的通用数学准则（即稀疏性和其他相关准则）导致了这种稀疏符号交互的涌现。该证明不依赖于任何特定的ANN架构。作者随后在多样化的模型和任务上进行实证验证，检验这两个准则是否在大多数输入样本上成立，并通过样本间和模型间的可迁移性以及解释ANN整体泛化能力来验证交互的忠实性。

Result: 实证结果表明，两个数学准则在多样化模型的大多数输入样本上成立。这些稀疏符号交互具有强样本间和模型间的可迁移性，并能够解释ANN的整体泛化能力。理论分析和大量实验为ANN的符号解释提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 该研究为ANN的符号化解释提供了理论基础，揭示了符号模式在神经网络中的涌现是自然规律而非偶然。这启发了"可通信学习"范式——推理逻辑可以在符号模式层面直接检查和调优，补充了传统的端到端学习范式。由于证明不依赖特定架构，这暗示类似的符号表示也可能在其他类型的黑盒系统中涌现。

Abstract: Artificial Neural networks (ANNs) are often treated as black-box models, making explainability a central challenge in deep learning. Many engineering methods have been proposed to approximately explain the ANN from various perspectives, such as feature attribution and visualization. However, it remains a long-standing open question whether the complex inference logic of an ANN can be explained exhaustively and concisely as sparse symbolic patterns. This raises a deeper inquiry: does the emergence of symbolic patterns reflect a natural law rather than chance? Here, we show that across a broad class of ANNs trained on diverse tasks, their inference logic can indeed be reformulated as sparse symbolic interactions. We further prove that two common mathematical criteria, which are implicitly required across tasks, lead to the emergence of such sparse symbolic interactions. Empirical evidence confirms that the two criteria hold for the majority of input samples in diverse models. Furthermore, the faithfulness of these interactions is also demonstrated by their strong sample-to-sample and model-to-model transferability, as well as their ability to explain the overall generalization power of ANNs. Our theoretical analysis and extensive experiments provide a solid foundation for symbolic explanations of ANNs, and offer novel insights into the ANN's generalization power. Our findings also highlight the potential of communicative learning, a paradigm in which the inference logic of an ANN can be directly inspected and tuned at the level of symbolic patterns, thus complementing traditional end-to-end learning paradigm. Finally, the observed emergence of symbolic patterns in ANNs suggests that similar symbolic representations may also emerge in other types of black-box systems under certain conditions, because our proof does not depend on any specific ANN architecture.

</details>


### [108] [Bridging the Gap Between Hyperdimensional Computing and Kernel Methods via the Nyström Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06860)
*Quanling Zhao,Anthony Hitchcock Thomas,Ari Brin,Xiaofan Yu,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NysHD方法，基于Nyström核近似技术，将任意用户定义的正半定相似性函数转化为超维计算（HDC）中的高维向量映射，从而扩展HDC可处理的问题类型，在图和字符串数据集上分别平均提升11%和17%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超维计算（HDC）是一种源于认知科学的计算范式，将数据表示为高维随机向量，具有严格的数学基础，且易于在FPGA和存内计算等节能并行硬件上实现。然而，HDC在机器学习中的有效性很大程度上取决于原始数据如何映射到高维空间。现有的HDC编码方法缺乏将用户自定义相似性函数融入映射的通用机制，限制了HDC可处理的问题类型。

Method: NysHD方法基于核近似领域的Nyström方法，提供了一种简单通用的配方，将任何用户定义的正半定（PSD）相似性函数转化为HDC中的等效向量映射。通过这种方式，可以将核学习中大量已有的相似性函数设计成果引入HDC框架，从而扩展HDC的应用范围。

Result: 在与现有HDC编码方法的对比实验中，NysHD在图形数据集上平均获得了11%的分类准确率提升，在字符串数据集上平均获得了17%的分类准确率提升。

Conclusion: NysHD为HDC提供了一种基于Nyström方法的高效编码机制，能够将核学习中的成熟经验迁移到超维计算领域，显著提升HDC在图和字符串等非向量数据上的分类性能，拓展了HDC的适用性问题类型。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional computing (HDC) is an approach from the cognitive science literature for solving information processing tasks using data represented as high-dimensional random vectors. The technique has a rigorous mathematical backing, and is easy to implement in energy-efficient and highly parallel hardware like FPGAs and "processing-in-memory" architectures. The effectiveness of HDC in machine learning largely depends on how raw data is mapped to high-dimensional space. In this work, we propose NysHD, a new method for constructing this mapping that is based on the Nyström method from the literature on kernel approximation. Our approach provides a simple recipe to turn any user-defined positive-semidefinite similarity function into an equivalent mapping in HDC. There is a vast literature on the design of such functions for learning problems. Our approach provides a mechanism to import them into the HDC setting, expanding the types of problems that can be tackled using HDC. Empirical evaluation against existing HDC encoding methods shows that NysHD can achieve, on average, 11% and 17% better classification accuracy on graph and string datasets respectively.

</details>


### [109] [SkillAligner: Treating Retrieved Skills as Adaptable Drafts at Execution Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06880)
*Qinfeng Li,Dalin He,Yuntai Bao,Ying Yang,Ruoxi Chen,Xinyan Yu,Lizhou Liang,Ge Su,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillAligner，一个无需训练的推理时技能适应框架，解决检索到的技能与当前任务间的"技能-执行不匹配"问题，通过一次性联合适应将检索到的技能转化为紧凑的执行指南，提升任务性能并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用技能虽承诺为语言智能体提供可复用的程序性知识，但语义相关性并不保证执行效用——检索到的技能可能包含与当前任务、执行环境或其他检索技能相冲突的假设，这一"技能-执行不匹配"问题尚未被系统化地解决。

Method: SkillAligner为无需训练的推理时技能适应框架。在执行前进行一次联合适应：1) 将有用的技能片段专门化到任务需求；2) 对齐其程序性假设与可用的执行接口；3) 通过解决技能间的依赖、冲突和冗余来组合生成的指导；4) 将适应后的内容整合为紧凑的执行指南，并在后续轨迹中复用。

Result: 在多种智能体基准和模型骨干上的大量实验表明，SkillAligner相较于现有技能使用基线能大幅提升任务性能，在实例层面减少技能引发的性能回退，并降低总推理成本。

Conclusion: 将检索到的技能视为可适应的草稿而非固定指令，通过执行前的一次性联合适应即可有效缓解技能-执行不匹配问题，在提升任务完成质量的同时降低计算开销。

Abstract: General-purpose skills promise reusable procedural knowledge for language agents, yet semantic relevance does not guarantee execution utility: a retrieved skill may encode assumptions that conflict with the current task, execution environment, or other retrieved skills. We formalize this problem as the skill--execution misfit. To address it, we propose SkillAligner, a training-free execution-time skill adaptation framework that treats retrieved skills as adaptable drafts rather than fixed instructions. Before execution, SkillAligner performs a one-time joint adaptation that specializes useful skill fragments to task requirements, aligns their procedural assumptions with the available execution interface, and composes the resulting guidance by resolving dependencies, conflicts, and redundancy across skills. The adapted content is consolidated into a compact execution guide and reused throughout the subsequent trajectory. Extensive experiments across diverse agent benchmarks and model backbones show that SkillAligner substantially improves task performance over existing skill-use baselines, reduces skill-induced regressions at the instance level, and lowers total inference cost.

</details>


### [110] [PRISM: Principled Reference Identification for Schrodinger Bridge Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06893)
*Forouzan Fallah,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISM提出了桥参考设计的理论框架，刻画了保持精确可解性的时变高斯参考条件（协方差可交换性），证明了不可见性原理（无限计算下所有可容许参考都恢复真实后验），并在有限步预算下推导了闭式最优噪声谱（正比于传感器破坏的信息谱Pk），同时通过实验揭示了真实图像（非高斯统计）与高斯理论之间的断裂点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schrödinger桥模型中用于恢复干净信号的参考过程（通常是白噪声加手工调节的调度）是启发式选择的，缺乏理论指导。这使得参考设计沦为超参数搜索，研究者不清楚不同参考之间的理论关系，也不知道在有限计算资源下应该如何选择最优参考，因此需要一个系统的参考设计理论。

Method: 1）理论上：刻画时变高斯参考保持逐模态精确可解的条件（瞬时协方差可交换），证明不可见性原理（精确漂移+无限步求解下所有可容许参考等价），并在固定步预算下推导有限步目标函数的闭式形式，证明最优噪声谱正比于传感器退化谱Pk，最优常数x*(T)=(2 ln T)^-1/2(1+o(1))；2）实验上：在Gaussian设定验证理论预测，在FFHQ真实图像上测试失真-感知权衡和频谱定位，并通过2x2机制研究追溯理论失效原因。

Result: Gaussian设定下理论预测的排序和闭式损失下限得到验证。FFHQ上失真-感知权衡与频谱定位结果可迁移，但白噪声优于匹配参考；预注册的换训练机制研究否定了脊白化作为解释；2x2机制研究将失效归因于真实图像的逐模态非高斯统计。

Conclusion: PRISM将参考设计从超参数扫掠转化为Gaussian情形下的解析计算，并精确定位了真实图像违背该理论的机制（非高斯逐模态统计）。此外，理论表明噪声颜色与时间调度可互换，且正则化会向白噪声方向移动最优参考。

Abstract: Schrödinger bridge models restore a clean signal from a degraded observation by following the conditional bridges of a reference process, yet this reference is chosen heuristically, typically white noise with a hand-tuned schedule. We develop PRISM, a theory of bridge reference design. We characterize the time-varying Gaussian references that remain exactly tractable with per-mode schedules: precisely those whose instantaneous covariances commute. We then prove an invisibility principle: with the exact drift and unlimited solver steps, every admissible reference recovers the true posterior. The choice of reference therefore matters only under finite computational resources. For a fixed step budget, we derive the finite-step objective in closed form and prove that every optimal noise spectrum is proportional to Pk, the spectrum of information destroyed by the sensor, with a mode-independent constant x*(T) = (2 ln T)^-1/2 (1 + o(1)). The analysis shows that noise color and temporal scheduling are interchangeable, and regularization provably shifts the optimal reference toward white noise. Experiments in Gaussian settings confirm the predicted orderings and the closed-form loss floors. On FFHQ, the distortion-- perception trade-off and spectral localization transfer, but white noise outperforms the matched reference; a pre-registered study that changes the training regime refutes ridge whitening as the explanation. A 2x2 mechanism study then traces the inversion to the non-Gaussian per-mode statistics of real images. PRISM turns reference design from a hyperparameter sweep into a calculation in the Gaussian regime, and locates exactly where real images break it.

</details>


### [111] [Recent advances in weakly supervised learning: New supervision paradigms, assumption relaxations, and practical solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06896)
*Wei Wang,Gang Niu,Masashi Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了弱监督学习的最新进展，主要涵盖三个方向：置信度差异二分类问题的一致性求解方法、基于松弛假设的互补标签多分类学习、以及部分标签学习的公平评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的成功依赖高质量标注数据，但现实应用中这一条件往往难以满足。弱监督学习旨在利用不完整、不精确或不准确的监督信息训练模型，本文旨在探讨该领域的新监督范式、松弛假设和实用解决方案。

Method: 本文提出了三个具体方法：(1) 针对置信度差异二分类问题，提出一致性求解方法；(2) 针对互补标签多分类学习，基于比现有一致性方法更松弛的数据生成过程假设提出新方法；(3) 为部分标签学习设计了一个评估框架，以促进算法在该领域的公平和真实评估。

Result: 文章系统地介绍了三个弱监督学习子问题：置信度差异分类、互补标签学习和部分标签学习，并分别给出了对应的解决方案和评估体系。

Conclusion: 弱监督学习在多个方向上取得了进展，包括新的监督范式（置信度差异分类）、更松弛的假设（互补标签学习）以及更公平的评估机制（部分标签学习评估框架），为解决真实世界标注数据稀缺问题提供了多种途径。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved great success in recent years thanks to the availability of high-quality, well-annotated training data. However, this requirement is often not met in real-world applications. Weakly supervised learning aims to train an accurate model with incomplete, inexact, or inaccurate supervision. In this chapter, we will discuss recent advances in this field, including new supervision paradigms, relaxed assumptions, and practical solutions. First, we introduce a new weakly supervised binary classification problem called confidence-difference classification and propose consistent approaches to solve it. Next, we investigate complementary-label learning, a weakly supervised multi-class classification problem. Our proposed approaches are based on more relaxed assumptions about the data generation process than existing consistent approaches. Lastly, we present an evaluation framework for partial-label learning, another popular multi-class weakly supervised learning problem, in order to promote fair and realistic evaluation of algorithms in this field.

</details>


### [112] [MiCoPro: End-to-End Mixed Precision HW/SW Co-design with HW-aware Proxy Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06916)
*Zijun Jiang,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MiCo/MiCoPro框架，用于边缘AI场景下的层间混合精度量化(MPQ)探索与端到端部署。该框架通过硬件感知代理(HAP)模型和延迟建模，实现从PyTorch模型到裸机C代码的直接部署，在BitFusion加速器和RISC-V处理器上实现最高40%延迟降低且精度损失小于3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合精度量化方案探索算法在灵活性和效率上受限，难以理解不同MPQ方案对PTQ和QAT的复杂影响，且缺少端到端的MPQ模型优化与部署框架。

Method: 提出MiCo框架，采用新型优化算法在严格延迟约束下搜索精度最优的量化配置；扩展为MiCoPro后引入硬件感知代理(HAP)模型提升预测准确性和硬件通用性，通过目标特定的延迟建模实现从PyTorch到裸机C代码的快速探索与直接部署。

Result: 在BitFusion加速器和SIMD扩展RISC-V处理器上验证了框架的通用性，实现多达40%的延迟降低，精度下降小于3%。

Conclusion: MiCo/MiCoPro框架为边缘AI设备的混合精度量化提供了完整的探索与部署解决方案，兼顾了精度、延迟和硬件通用性。

Abstract: Quantized Neural Networks~(QNN) with low-bitwidth data have proven promising in efficient storage and computation on edge devices. To mitigate accuracy degradation while maximizing speedup, layer-wise mixed-precision quantization~(MPQ) becomes a popular solution. However, existing algorithms for exploring MPQ schemes are limited in flexibility and efficiency. Comprehending the complex impacts of different MPQ schemes on post-training quantization and quantization-aware training results is a challenge for conventional methods. Furthermore, an end-to-end framework for the optimization and deployment of MPQ models is missing in existing work.
  To address these challenges, we propose the MiCo framework, a holistic MPQ exploration and deployment framework for edge AI applications. The framework adopts a novel optimization algorithm to search for accuracy-optimal quantization configurations under strict latency constraints. We further extended the framework to MiCoPro, which introduces a robust Hardware-Aware Proxy (HAP) model to enhance prediction accuracy and hardware versatility. By leveraging target-specific latency modeling, MiCoPro enables rapid exploration and direct deployment from PyTorch models to bare-metal C code. We demonstrate the versatility of our framework on both the BitFusion accelerator and SIMD-extended RISC-V processors, achieving up to 40\% of latency reduction with less than 3\% of accuracy drop.

</details>


### [113] [Walkable to Whom? Capturing Subjective Variability in Walkability Perception Using Multimodal Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06934)
*Moloud Damandeh,Meead Saberi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用户条件化的多模态深度学习框架用于步行性感知评估，基于包含29,870条步行性评分、1,196名受访者的澳大利亚数据集，融合人行道视角图像与个体属性表征，显著提升了评分预测的一致性（QWK从0.29提升至0.47）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有步行性感知研究存在两个主要问题：一是将不同个体的多样化判断简单聚合为统一评分，隐含地假设所有人的感知一致；二是通常使用车载街景图像，无法反映行人实际视角的视觉体验。这限制了对步行环境评估的包容性和准确性。

Method: 构建了一个包含29,870条步行性评分、1,196名受访者的数据集，覆盖澳大利亚城市、郊区和乡村地区的人行道视角图像，并与个体评分者属性关联。提出了首个用户条件化（user-conditioned）多模态深度学习框架，将视觉特征与受访者级表示相融合。此外，还进行了视角对比研究，比较人行道视角与匹配街景视角图像的评分差异。

Result: 视角对比研究发现人行道视角图像获得的步行性评分显著高于匹配的街景视角图像，表明图像来源是感知调查中的实质性设计决策。用户条件化模型较仅图像基线模型的排序一致性提升了65%（二次加权kappa从0.29提升至0.47），证明评估者身份对预测的贡献超出图像内容本身。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持从聚合的、与观察者无关的步行性评分向能代表多样化用户的模型转变，从而实现更具包容性的步行环境评估。

Abstract: Visual perception of walkability varies substantially across individuals, reflecting differences in personal characteristics, experiences, and preferences. Existing studies, however, often reduce these diverse judgements to aggregated scores, implicitly assuming uniform perception, and commonly rely on vehicle-mounted street-view imagery that does not reflect the pedestrian's visual experience. This paper introduces a dataset of 29,870 walkability ratings from 1,196 respondents, linking sidewalk-view imagery across urban, suburban, and regional Australian environments with individual rater attributes, and proposes the first user-conditioned multimodal deep learning framework for walkability perception, fusing visual features with respondent-level representations. A viewpoint-comparison study shows that sidewalk-view images receive significantly higher walkability ratings than matched street-view images, indicating that imagery source is a substantive design decision in perception surveys. The user-conditioned model improves rank agreement with observed ratings by 65% over an image-only baseline (quadratic weighted kappa 0.47 vs. 0.29), demonstrating that who is evaluating an environment carries predictive indication beyond image content alone. These findings support moving from aggregated, observer-independent walkability scores toward models that represent diverse users, enabling more inclusive assessment of pedestrian environments.

</details>


### [114] [ELMZip: Onboard Satellite Image Compression via Extreme Learning Machines for Efficient Downlink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06942)
*Woojin Cho,Junghwan Park,Sangcheol Sim,Steve Andreas Immanuel,Junhyuk Heo,Darongsae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ELMZip框架，基于极限学习机（ELM）和域分解策略，用于小卫星多光谱图像的高效、免分辨率内置神经表征压缩，通过凸最小二乘求解和仅传输紧凑输出权重的非对称传输协议，显著降低下行链路负载并保持高重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小卫星（如CubeSats）获取多光谱图像时面临高数据量和受限通信窗口带来的下行链路瓶颈，传统压缩方法难以适应多波段、多分辨率数据的非线性统计特性，因此需要更高效的机载压缩方案。

Method: ELMZip基于极限学习机（ELM）和域分解策略，将拟合过程建模为使用随机特征单层网络的凸最小二乘问题，避免昂贵的反向传播；采用非对称传输协议，仅传输紧凑的输出权重，而非完整网络参数。

Result: 与传统需要迭代优化并传输完整网络参数的神经表征方法相比，ELMZip在显著提升压缩效率的同时保持高重建保真度，能够实现即时图像重建。

Conclusion: ELMZip使资源受限平台能够最大化数据回报，推进实时AI驱动的对地观测应用。

Abstract: The acquisition of multispectral imagery via small satellites (e.g., CubeSats) presents significant data downlink challenges due to high data volumes and restricted communication windows. While onboard image compression is critical to address this bottleneck, traditional methods often struggle to adapt to the nonlinear statistics of multi-band, multi-resolution data. To overcome these limitations, we propose ELMZip, a novel framework based on Extreme Learning Machines (ELM) and domain decomposition strategies for efficient, resolution-free onboard neural representation. ELMZip formulates the fitting process as a convex least-squares problem using random-feature single-layer networks, thereby eliminating the need for computationally expensive backpropagation. By adopting an asymmetric transmission protocol that sends only the compact output weights, the proposed method significantly reduces the downlink payload. Unlike previous neural representation approaches that rely on iterative optimization and require transmitting full network parameters, ELMZip achieves significant compression efficiency while maintaining high reconstruction fidelity. This capability enables immediate image reconstruction for analysis, allowing resource-constrained platforms to maximize data return and advancing real-time AI-powered Earth observation.

</details>


### [115] [A Rate Separation for Agnostic Direct Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06951)
*Mihir More,Aritra Das,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了agnostic PAC学习曲线中直接和（direct sum）的学习速率是否由单实例学习速率决定的问题，通过构造两个反例类F和G（均具有n^{-1/2}阶的agnostic学习曲线），证明单实例学习速率并不能决定直接和学习速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hanneke, Moran和Waknine提出了一个开放问题：agnostic PAC学习中直接和C^r的学习曲线如何依赖于单实例学习曲线ε_agn(n|C)和参数r。本文旨在回答这个问题，探究单实例学习速率是否足以决定直接和的学习速率。

Method: 构造两个具体的假设类：F为两个常数二元函数组成的类，G由零函数和恒等函数组成。这两个类都具有n^{-1/2}阶的agnostic学习曲线，但它们的结构不同，用于展示即使单实例学习速率相同，直接和学习速率也可能不同。

Result: 证明了单实例学习速率并不能决定直接和的学习速率。两个类F和G虽然都具有n^{-1/2}阶的单实例agnostic学习曲线，但它们的直接和学习速率可能存在差异。

Conclusion: 直接和的学习速率不仅取决于单实例学习速率，还与学习问题本身的具体结构有关。这一结果否定了仅通过单实例学习速率来预测直接和学习速率的可能性。

Abstract: Hanneke, Moran, and Waknine \cite{HannekeMoranWaknine2024} asked how the agnostic PAC learning curve of the direct sum $C^r$ depends on the single-instance learning curve $\epsagn(n\mid C)$ and on $r$. We show that the single-instance learning rate does not determine the direct-sum rate. Let $\F$ be the class of the two constant binary functions and let $\G$ consist of the zero function and the identity function. Both classes have agnostic learning curve of order $n^{-1/2}$.

</details>


### [116] [How Molecular Generative Models Organize Molecular Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06956)
*Raul Ortega-Ochoa,Tejs Vegge,Jens S. Bakander,Luis Mantilla Calderon,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Tonio Buonassisi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将分子身份显式化并沿生成过程拉回（pullback），研究分子生成模型内部如何组织离散化学身份。发现跨三种架构中，模型内部"曲目"（repertoire）呈现分段常数区域，由重现的粗到细边界分隔，其组织方式受表示、身份约定、解码器随机性和坐标度量影响，且训练期间局部化学组织稳定但身份数量持续变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型通常被作为输出空间的采样器来评估，其潜空间被用作化学空间导航的代理，但对模型内部如何在表示中组织离散化学身份知之甚少。理解这种内部排列对于判断潜空间是否可被当作化学可导航空间至关重要。

Method: 将分子身份显式化并通过生成过程进行拉回（pullback），探测产生同一对象的区域，揭示训练模型内部的固定划分（repertoire）。在三种分子生成架构上进行系统比较，考察表示类型、身份约定、解码器随机性和坐标度量等变量的影响。

Result: 内部曲目被组织成分段常数区域，由重现的粗到细边界分隔；其组织取决于所探测的表示、身份约定、解码器随机性和比较坐标的度量；训练期间局部化学组织趋于稳定，但每个邻域内所代表的离散分子身份数量持续变化。

Conclusion: 生成空间的内部组织必须被系统地刻画而非假定，只有在完成表征之后，生成空间才能被视为化学可导航的。

Abstract: Generative models for matter are often evaluated as samplers over output representations, and their latent spaces are commonly used as proxies for navigating chemical space. Much less is known about how these models internally arrange discrete chemical identities within those representations. We study this arrangement by making molecular identity explicit and pulling it back through the generative process. Through these pullbacks we probe the regions that generate the same object, exposing the trained model's internal repertoire: a fixed partition that determines which objects (novel or not) the model can produce.
  Across three molecular generative architectures, we find that this repertoire is arranged into piecewise-constant regions separated by recurring coarse-to-fine boundaries. Its organization depends on the representation probed, the identity convention, decoder stochasticity, and the metric used to compare coordinates. During training, local chemical organization stabilizes while the number of distinct molecular identities represented within each neighborhood continues to change. Internal organization must therefore be characterized, rather than assumed, before a generative space can be treated as chemically navigable.

</details>


### [117] [Density-aware Hierarchical Clustering Based on Element-Categorized Connection Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06990)
*Yuning Yu,José Rodríguez-Piñeiro,Xuefeng Yin,Bin Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元素分类连接子图的密度感知层次聚类方法（DHC-ECS），将层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类有机整合，通过新型簇间相似度度量同时考虑距离、KNN连接子图中的元素分类、核密度估计和子簇内局部连通性，在异构基准数据集上表现出优于基线方法的聚类精度和参数鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统层次聚类的簇间相似度仅基于成对距离计算，忽视了数据密度变化和图结构连通性，导致聚类效果受限。现有方法难以有效同时融合层次聚类、密度聚类和图聚类的优势。

Method: 提出DHC-ECS方法，其核心是引入一种新型簇间相似度度量，该度量融合了四个维度：（1）KNN连接子图中的元素分类；（2）核密度估计；（3）子簇内的局部连通性；（4）传统距离信息。通过该度量在层次聚类的凝聚或分裂过程中自适应地决定簇的合并或划分。

Result: 在异构基准数据集上的大量实验表明，与基线方法（AChameleon、RNN-DBSCAN、McDPC和G-RMS）相比，DHC-ECS在聚类准确性和参数鲁棒性方面均表现出更优的综合性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过利用局部密度和图结构化连通性（即顶点与边的对偶性），所提出的聚类算法在低维数据集上具有巨大潜力，并可能确定内在阈值以减少对人工参数调优的依赖。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental data mining technique for pattern recognition through unsupervised learning. Among various clustering methods, hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering stand out as representative approaches. For hierarchical clustering, it can be categorized into agglomerative and divisive modes to construct clusters in a recursive manner. The key aspect of both modes is the calculation of inter-cluster similarity, which determines whether to merge the sub-clusters into one cluster or divide a current cluster into sub-clusters. Traditionally, the similarity is derived from pairwise distances, often overlooking density variations and structural connectivity in graphs. To address this, we propose a density-aware hierarchical clustering method based on element-categorized connection subgraphs (DHC-ECS), which effectively integrates the hierarchical clustering, density-based clustering, and graph clustering. Particularly, a novel inter-cluster similarity metric is introduced that considers not only distances but also the element categorization in the KNN connection subgraphs, kernel density estimation, and local connectivity within sub-clusters. Extensive evaluations on heterogeneous benchmark datasets demonstrate that DHC-ECS exhibits superior overall performance in terms of clustering accuracy and parameter robustness compared with the baseline methods (including AChameleon, RNN-DBSCAN, McDPC, and G-RMS). The work indicates the great potential of the proposed clustering algorithm for low-dimensional datasets by leveraging local density and graph-structured connectivity (i.e., the duality of vertices and edges), as well as the possibility to determine an intrinsic threshold, reducing the reliance on manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond Foundation Models: Dimension-Aware Neural Architecture Search with Small-Data Representation Models for Cryocooler Lifetime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06993)
