The unnormalized case $α=0$ is treated separately and requires $Lρ<1$. We then introduce a clipped rule which agrees with the constant-$ρ$ rule away from stationary points and becomes proportional to the gradient near them. The clipped method has $\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}\to0$ and the usual $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity bound. Numerical tests on a quadratic function, the Rosenbrock function, and a five-dimensional nonconvex function illustrate the stationarity floor of the unclipped rule and the effect of clipping.

</details>


### [152] [The Blessing and Curse of Curvature in Higher-Order Optimization: Horospherical versus Geodesic Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06719)
*Andi Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hadamard流形上确定性黎曼p阶（p≥2）优化复杂度，区分强h-凸与强g-凸两类目标。对h-凸目标，在任意Hadamard流形上达到欧氏最优速率，且负曲率增大可进一步改进；对g-凸目标，曲率增长时存在信息论障碍，揭示两类凸性在双曲空间上的根本分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要关注一阶/二阶优化在Hadamard流形上的复杂度，但对p阶（p≥2）oracle复杂度以及h-凸与g-凸两种凸性概念在曲率影响下的精确刻画尚不完整。h-凸与g-凸在欧氏空间等价，但在弯曲流形上二者分离，理解这种分离对优化复杂度的影响是核心动机。

Method: 建立p阶黎曼oracle模型，利用horoball局部化技术将优化区域限制到曲率尺度（1/κ）内，从而用曲率信息替代全局几何约束。对上界采用horoball支撑定位，对下界构造双曲空间的hard instances，并统一以参数Q_p=L_p R^{p-1}/μ刻画条件数。

Result: 强h-凸目标：任意Hadamard流形上达到欧氏最优速率Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}，下界匹配；双曲空间上可将条件参数改进为Q_p·min{1,4/(κR)}^{p-1}，负曲率增长可提升速率。强g-凸目标：κR=O(1)时恢复欧氏指数；κR增长时，构造的hard家族需要Ω̃_p(Q_p^{1/p})查询，表明g-凸类存在根本性的信息论障碍。

Conclusion: h-凸与g-凸在Hadamard流形上导致截然不同的p阶优化复杂度：h-凸可利用全局horospherical信息获得甚至优于欧氏的速率，而g-凸在强曲率下受到本质性下界限制。这为黎曼优化中选择合适的凸性概念提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: We study deterministic Riemannian $p$-th-order oracle complexity (for $p\ge2$) on Hadamard manifolds, under strong horospherical ($h$)-convexity and strong geodesic ($g$)-convexity. The two notions agree in the Euclidean space. On a curved Hadamard manifold, $h$-convexity is a stronger notion than $g$-convexity and supplies global horospherical information.
  Writing the $p$-th-order condition parameter $Q_p=L_pR^{p-1}/μ$, we obtain the Euclidean-optimal rate $Q_p^{2/(3p+1)}$ for strongly $h$-convex objectives on every Hadamard manifold, with a matching fixed-curvature lower bound. On hyperbolic space, the resulting horoball supports enable localization to the curvature scale. This replaces the condition parameter $Q_p$ by $Q_p \min\{1, 4/(κR) \}^{p-1}$, subject to a logarithmic localization cost. Thus growing negative curvature ($κR \rightarrow \infty$) can further improve the optimal Euclidean rate under $h$-convexity. For strongly $g$-convex objectives, matching upper and lower bounds recover the same Euclidean exponent when $κR=O(1)$. In contrast, with growing $κR$, we construct a hard family on the hyperbolic space with $Q_p\asymp_p(1+κR)^p$ that requires $\widetildeΩ_p(Q_p^{1/p})$ queries. This reveals a fundamental separation: the same hyperbolic divergence that sharpens horoball localization yields an information-theoretic obstruction for the full $g$-convex class.

</details>


### [153] [Connecting Extreme-Point Generation and Decision Rules in Two-Stage Distributionally Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06821)
*Jin Qi,Chen Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两阶段分布鲁棒优化问题，通过第二阶段对偶问题的极点将决策规则方法和分解生成方法联系起来。每个极点定义了仿射价值片段，兼容的原始基可定义仿射策略片段。作者提出一种极点生成方法来求解第一阶段问题，并通过线性规划程序逐步添加片段以恢复不确定性集上的追索价值，同时可利用这些片段恢复最优追索策略。此外还开发了后验精确性检验，并扩展到退化、秩亏追索和结构化随机追索情形。实验表明算法高效，且完全恢复第二阶段价值所需的极点比仅求解第一阶段问题更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段分布鲁棒优化中的决策规则方法需要预先指定自适应策略的形式，而分解生成方法则迭代地构造第二阶段的数值信息。这两种方法之间缺乏系统性的联系，且决策规则的精确性难以验证，因此需要一种统一视角来理解和衔接这两类方法，并评估决策规则的近似质量。

Method: 提出基于第二阶段对偶问题极点的方法论框架：每个极点定义一个仿射价值片段；利用极点生成方法求解第一阶段问题；设计独立的线性规划程序逐步添加片段直至在不确定性集上完整恢复追索价值函数；基于算法输出构建后验精确性检验以验证传统决策规则；针对退化、秩亏追索和结构化随机追索给出扩展方案。

Result: 所提出的算法在报告实例上计算效率高；实验结果表明，在不确定性集上完整恢复第二阶段价值所需的极点数量多于仅求解第一阶段问题所需的极点数量，说明两者存在计算复杂度的差异。

Conclusion: 通过第二阶段对偶极点统一了决策规则与分解生成两类方法，提供了一种可验证决策规则精确性的后验检验机制，并适用于多种实际扩展情形，为两阶段分布鲁棒优化提供了新的高效求解途径。

Abstract: Two-stage distributionally robust optimization chooses a here-and-now decision and a wait-and-see decision policy that adapts to uncertainty realizations. Decision-rule methods specify the form of this adaptive policy in advance, whereas decomposition-generation methods construct second-stage value information iteratively. We connect these approaches through extreme points of the second-stage dual problem. Each extreme point defines an affine value piece, and a compatible primal basis can define an affine policy piece. Solving the first-stage problem may require only a subset of these pieces. We specialize an extreme-point generation method to solve the first-stage problem. A separate linear-programming procedure adds pieces until it recovers the recourse value over the uncertainty set, and the pieces can also be used to recover the optimal recourse policy. Building on the algorithm output, we develop a posteriori exactness test for conventional decision rules. We give extensions for degeneracy, rank-deficient recourse, and structured random recourse. The proposed algorithm is computationally efficient on the reported instances, and the results show that completing the second-stage value across the uncertainty set requires more extreme points than solving the first-stage problem alone.

</details>


### [154] [Preference-Based Estimation of Achievement Scalarising Function Parameters via Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06881)
*Maria Barbati,Salvatore Greco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于序数回归的方法来估计成就标量化函数的参数（目标权重和参考点），通过决策者提供的偏好信息实现参数推断，并用多目标背包问题的教学案例进行了逐步说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成就标量化函数在多目标优化中广泛应用，但其有效性高度依赖于权重和参考点等参数的恰当设定，而参数估计是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 采用基于序数回归（ordinal regression）的方法论，从决策者提供的偏好信息中推断成就标量化函数的目标权重与参考点参数。

Result: 通过一个多目标背包问题的教学示例，逐步展示了偏好信息如何转化为模型参数，验证了序数回归能够为标量化函数的校准提供灵活且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: 序数回归为标量化函数的参数校准提供了一种灵活且可解释的框架，能够有效支持多目标优化中决策者偏好的获取与利用。

Abstract: Estimating the parameters of an achievement scalarising function is a challenging task in multiobjective optimisation. Achievement scalarising functions are widely used in several contexts, including preference-based optimisation methods and algorithmic procedures for generating or selecting efficient solutions. However, their effectiveness strongly depends on the appropriate specification of their parameters, such as weights and reference points. In this paper, we propose an ordinal-regression-based methodology to estimate the parameters of an achievement scalarising function from preference information provided by the DM. In particular, the proposed approach allows us to infer both the weights associated with the objectives and the reference points characterising the scalarising function. The methodology is illustrated through a didactic example based on a multiobjective knapsack problem, which shows step by step how the preference information is translated into model parameters. The example illustrates that ordinal regression provides a flexible and interpretable framework for calibrating achievement scalarising functions and supporting the elicitation of decision-maker preferences in multiobjective optimisation.

</details>


### [155] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum: Analysis and Synthesis via Integral Quadratic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06915)
*Dennis Gramlich,Carsten W. Scherer,Christian Ebenbauer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用动态积分二次约束（IQCs）框架分析和综合加速随机梯度算法，将小批量梯度随机扰动建模为Lur'e系统中的反馈非线性，提出了刻画梯度二阶统计量的新IQC族（扩展Zames-Falb乘子），并给出了认证指数收敛速率和渐近方差上界的半定规划条件，以及最小化可认证收敛速率的凸综合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度算法在机器学习中广泛应用，但其收敛性分析往往依赖于问题结构特性和启发式论证，缺乏统一的鲁棒控制视角。作者希望借助Lur'e系统的鲁棒控制工具（IQC理论），为加速随机梯度算法提供系统化、可验证的收敛性保证，并进一步指导算法设计。

Method: 方法包含三部分：(1) 提出一族动态IQC来描述小批量梯度的二阶统计特性，扩展了经典Zames-Falb乘子；(2) 建立半定规划（SDP）以认证随机梯度算法的指数收敛速率，并给出约束非消失梯度噪声所导致渐近方差的互补条件；(3) 提出凸综合过程，在可认证收敛速率意义下寻找最优的小批量梯度算法。

Result: 三项主要贡献：一是面向小批量梯度二阶统计量的动态IQC族；二是基于SDP的指数收敛速率认证条件及渐近方差界；三是实现最小可认证收敛速率的凸综合算法设计方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将鲁棒控制中的IQC理论成功拓展到随机优化领域，为分析随机梯度算法的收敛性与设计更优算法提供了严格且可计算的理论框架。

Abstract: This article applies dynamic integral quadratic constraints (IQCs) to the analysis and synthesis of accelerated stochastic gradient algorithms. We consider composite objective functions whose gradient can be approximated via mini-batch sampling and we model the resulting stochastic gradient oracle as a feedback nonlinearity in the spirit of Lur'e systems from robust control. Our first main contribution is a family of IQCs that characterize the second-order statistics of mini-batch gradients, extending the classical Zames--Falb multipliers. Our second contribution is a semidefinite-program for certifying exponential convergence rates of stochastic gradient algorithms, and a complementary condition for bounding the asymptotic variance caused by non-vanishing gradient noise. Our third contribution is a convex synthesis procedure that identifies mini-batch gradient algorithms with the smallest certifiable convergence rate.

</details>


### [156] [Stability of Differential Stochastic Variational Inequalities with History-Dependent Responses and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06923)
*Xiaojun Chen,Jian Guo,Xin Guo,Guan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一类微分随机变分不等式（DSVIs），将常微分方程（ODE）与历史依赖的随机变分不等式（SVI）耦合，建立了该框架下二阶响应的唯一性、可测性和Lipschitz连续性，证明了状态轨迹的存在唯一性；进一步构造了基于样本平均近似（SAA）的算法并证明其一致收敛性，推导了参数变分不等式的1/2-Hölder估计和轨迹稳定性界；将其应用于老年健康监测系统，通过相似性加权复用预计算响应，在保持接近0.97精度的同时将在线批处理时间从86秒降至1秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机变分不等式框架难以刻画具有记忆依赖（历史依赖）的闭环随机系统，特别是涉及时变随机均衡和优化约束的动态过程。需要一种统一的数学框架来建模这类系统，并为其提供理论保证和高效的计算方法。

Method: (1) 提出DSVI框架，将ODE与历史依赖的SVI耦合建模闭环随机系统；(2) 在适当技术条件下证明二阶响应的唯一性、可测性和关于状态的Lipschitz连续性，从而保证状态轨迹的存在唯一性；(3) 基于独立样本路径构造SAA算法并证明近似轨迹的一致收敛性；(4) 推导含移动可行集的参数变分不等式的局部1/2-Hölder估计，以及基于初始状态差和Wasserstein距离的量化轨迹稳定性界；(5) 提出相似性加权复用预计算响应的迁移学习方法。

Result: 理论方面，建立了DSVI框架下轨迹的存在唯一性、SAA的一致收敛性以及参数迁移的稳定性界。实验方面，在老年健康监测系统中，相似性加权复用方法达到接近全量重算基准（0.97）的精度，同时将在线批处理时间从86秒大幅降低至不到1秒；扰动和延迟更新实验验证了算法对传感器噪声的鲁棒性，并刻画了响应新鲜度、预测精度和计算成本之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为历史依赖的DSVI系统提供了完整的理论支撑（唯一性、收敛性和稳定性）与高效的计算框架（SAA和迁移学习），展示了在实时性要求高的闭环随机系统（如老年健康监测）中的实用价值，为DSVI框架下的高效迁移学习提供了理论和计算基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study a class of differential stochastic variational inequalities (DSVIs), in which an ordinary differential equation (ODE) is coupled with history-dependent stochastic variational inequalities (SVI). This framework models closed-loop stochastic systems with time-varying random equilibria and includes optimization-constrained ODEs as special cases. Under appropriate technical conditions, we establish uniqueness, measurability, and Lipschitz continuity with respect to the state of the second-stage response, and consequently the existence and uniqueness of the induced state trajectory.
  Moreover, we construct a sample average approximation (SAA) based on independent sample paths and prove uniform convergence of the approximate trajectories. For transfer between related stochastic environments, we derive a local $1/2$-Hölder estimate for parametric variational inequalities with moving feasible sets and a quantitative trajectory-stability bound in terms of the initial-state difference and the Wasserstein distance between exogenous path laws. Numerical experiments illustrate the SAA convergence and transfer-stability results. We further apply the framework to an elderly-health monitoring system. Similarity-weighted reuse of precomputed responses achieves an accuracy close to the full-recomputation benchmark of 0.97, while reducing the online batch runtime from 86 seconds to less than one second. Perturbation and delayed-update experiments additionally characterize robustness to sensor noise and the trade-off between response freshness, predictive accuracy, and computational cost. These results provide theoretical and computational support for efficient transfer learning in history-dependent DSVI systems.

</details>


### [157] [Certified High-Dimensional Wasserstein Robust Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07032)
*Chung-Han Hsieh,Rong Gan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化的可信赖可扩展近似方法。在one-norm地面度量下，通过对效用函数进行支持超平面逼近并对支撑子问题对偶化，将原指数规模的半无限凸规划转化为多项式规模的线性规划，并提供了统一的近似误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒投资组合优化虽然具有理论上的吸引力（通过标准对偶可得到半无限凸规划），但其计算复杂度随资产维度呈指数增长，在实际场景（如数百至上千资产）中无法直接求解。需要一个既有理论保证又可扩展到高维的近似求解方法。

Method: 首先针对长期（long-only）投资组合和box支撑集、one-norm地面度量，给出一个精确的样本特定顶点重构作为指数规模的基准；然后用支持超平面对效用函数进行上包络逼近（majorization），并对支撑子问题进行对偶化，得到有限超平面-对偶形式；在one-norm度量与多面体投资组合约束下，该形式为多项式规模的线性规划。

Result: 理论上一阶Wasserstein歧义（order-one）下的均匀效用逼近误差同时界定了鲁棒值误差和原始鲁棒问题的近最优性间隙。实验验证了该认证近似的有效性，展示了月度476资产再平衡能力以及扩展到1000资产的计算规模。

Conclusion: 通过对效用函数的结构化逼近与对偶化，成功将高维Wasserstein分布鲁棒组合优化从指数复杂度降为多项式复杂度，且保持了可认证的近似质量，为大规模实际投资组合优化提供了可行的计算工具。

Abstract: We develop a certified, scalable approximation for high-dimensional Wasserstein distributionally robust portfolio optimization. For expected-utility maximization under order-one Wasserstein ambiguity, standard duality yields a semi-infinite convex program. For long-only portfolios with box support under the one-norm ground metric, an exact sample-specific vertex reformulation provides an exponential-size computational benchmark. We then majorize the utility by supporting hyperplanes and dualize the support subproblems, obtaining a finite hyperplane--dual formulation over compact polyhedral supports. Under the one-norm ground metric and polyhedral portfolio constraints, this formulation is a polynomial-size linear program. The uniform utility-approximation error bounds both the robust-value error and the near-optimality gap for the original robust problem. Experiments validate the certified approximation and demonstrate monthly 476-asset rebalancing and computational scalability to 1,000 assets.

</details>


### [158] [Free-Horizon Newton Method for Nonlinear Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07108)
*Gyeongrok Ha,Kenji Fujimoto,Ichiro Maruta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的轨迹优化方法，用于非线性l1范数最优控制问题，将控制时域视为自由变量，实现控制时长与控制输入l1范数的联合优化，无需预先指定控制时域即可获得高保真最优轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在固定控制时域下最小化输入的l1范数，无法同时优化能量效率和时间效率。控制时域的选取通常需要人工预先指定，这限制了轨迹优化的灵活性和最优性。

Method: 将控制输入和控制时域同时作为设计变量，扩展基于牛顿的算法以适配该目标函数，利用关于两个变量的梯度信息进行联合优化。

Result: 数值仿真中，该方法成功恢复了航天器Hohmann转移的解析解，并为地月转移确定了一个最优控制时域，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法允许在没有预指定控制时域的情况下获得高保真最优轨迹，实现了能量与时间效率的联合优化，在航天轨迹优化等领域具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel trajectory optimization method for nonlinear l1-optimal control problems in which the control horizon is treated as a free variable, allowing both the control duration and the l1-norm of the control input to be evaluated. Unlike traditional approaches that minimize the l1-norm of the input under a fixed control horizon, the proposed method treats both the control input and control horizon as design variables, enabling joint optimization of energy and temporal efficiency. The method extends a Newton-based algorithm to accommodate this objective, leveraging gradient information with respect to both variables. This formulation enables one to find high-fidelity optimal trajectories without pre-specifying the control horizon. The effectiveness and robustness of the method are demonstrated through numerical simulations, in which the proposed algorithm recovers the analytical solution to the spacecraft Hohmann transfer, and determines an optimal control horizon for an Earth-Moon transfer.

</details>


### [159] [Establishing Boundary KKT Convergence of Mirror Descent through Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07248)
*Kuangyu Ding,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了镜像下降算法在非凸问题中（无需排除边界极限）收敛到KKT点，其成立条件联合约束了目标函数、Legendre核与可行域几何。关键工具是一个度量展平的重新参数化S，它具有可定义的边界延拓，通过KL论证结合S的连续性恢复原始序列到KKT点的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像下降算法在非凸问题中的收敛性分析此前通常需要额外的边界条件或排除边界极限情形，而实际应用中边界行为往往不可避免。本文旨在建立更一般、可验证的收敛条件，统一处理目标函数、核函数与可行域几何之间的相互作用。

Method: 提出度量展平重新参数化S，该映射具有可定义的边界延拓；对重新参数化后的目标函数应用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (KL)论证，先建立S(x_k)的收敛性；再利用S的逆映射的连续性，还原出原始序列x_k收敛到KKT点。条件联合耦合了目标函数、Legendre核和可行域几何。

Result: 在无需排除边界极限的情况下证明了镜像下降在非凸问题中收敛到KKT点。该框架成功应用于Shannon熵、Fermi-Dirac熵以及幂核等具体实例。

Conclusion: 该工作为镜像下降在非凸问题中的收敛性提供了统一且可验证的理论保证，未来可推广至更一般的约束几何、非可分核，以及Bregman近端点算法、Bregman ADMM等更广泛的Bregman类方法及其非精确变体。

Abstract: We prove that mirror descent converges to a KKT point for the nonconvex problem without excluding boundary limits. The result holds under verifiable conditions that jointly couple the objective, the Legendre kernel, and the feasible geometry. The key ingredient to establish the convergence is a metric-flattening reparameterization \(S\) that admits a definable boundary extension. Applying the KL argument to the reparameterized objective yields convergence of \(S(x_k)\). Continuity of \(S^{-1}\) then recovers convergence to the KKT point of the original sequence. We further apply our general framework to some concrete examples: Shannon entropy, Fermi--Dirac entropy, and power kernels. Future work may consider more general constraint geometries and genuinely nonseparable kernels, and extend mirror descent to broader Bregman-type methods, e.g. Bregman proximal point algorithms and Bregman ADMM, and their inexact variants.

</details>


### [160] [Inverse reinforcement learning for indefinite mean-field social optimization with multiplicative noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07252)
*Ying Cao,Xun Li,Bing-Chang Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性二次均场（MF）社会优化中的逆强化学习问题，针对具有乘性噪声和不定成本权重的系统，提出了基于模型和模型无关的两种逆RL算法，用于从专家演示中恢复未知的社会成本权重并复现最优控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆强化学习研究多基于标准凸性假设，而实际MF社会优化问题中存在乘性噪声和不定成本权重，违反标准凸性假设，给理论分析和算法设计带来挑战。此外，系统动力学未知时如何从数据中恢复成本权重也是一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 首先提出一种基于模型的双循环逆RL算法，分别处理个体和MF动力学，并证明了其收敛性和闭环可镇定性质；同时刻画了恢复成本权重的非唯一性特征。随后，利用积分RL和最小二乘辨识开发了模型无关的逆RL算法，仅需满足温和秩条件的实测轨迹数据即可工作。

Result: 理论分析证明了基于模型的逆RL算法的收敛性和闭环可镇定性，并揭示了成本权重恢复的非唯一性；模型无关算法通过积分RL和最小二乘辨识实现了不依赖系统动力学的成本权重恢复；数值仿真验证了两种方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为具有乘性噪声和不定成本权重的MF社会优化逆RL问题提供了完整的理论框架和可实现的算法，填补了该领域在非标准凸性条件下的研究空白。

Abstract: This paper studies the inverse reinforcement learning (RL) problem for linear-quadratic mean-field (MF) social optimization. The considered system features multiplicative noise and indefinite cost weights, which violate standard convexity assumptions and pose analytical challenges. The goal is to recover unknown social cost weights from expert demonstrations and reproduce the optimal control policies. This requires solving coupled stochastic algebraic Riccati equations and Lyapunov equations with unknown system dynamics. To this end, we first propose a model-based inverse RL algorithm with two sequential loops that separately handle individual and MF dynamics, and we prove its convergence and closed-loop stabilizability. Moreover, we characterize the non-uniqueness of the recovered cost weights. To eliminate reliance on system dynamics, we develop a model-free inverse RL algorithm using integral RL and least-squares identification, which requires only measured trajectory data satisfying mild rank conditions. Finally, numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed approaches.

</details>


### [161] [Metric Non-Collapse in Learned World Models for Control: Approximation Theory, Finite-Sample Geometric Guarantees, and Deterministic Planning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07265)
*Alain Bensoussan,Minh-Nhat Phung,Minh-Binh Tran*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为非线性确定性控制系统的度量保真学习世界模型建立了三部分数学理论：近似理论（构造光滑精确潜变量实现并验证B样条类的有限容量C^{1,1}逼近性质）、有限样本几何（引入编码器局部-全局度量铰链防止无穷小塌缩与全局折叠）、确定性规划迁移（度量非塌缩诱导相容Lipschitz潜成本，半共轭给出轨迹与成本界）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习世界模型在控制系统中应保持度量保真以确保规划与优化迁移可靠，但现有方法缺乏严格的数学保障，尤其在有限样本下如何防止潜空间塌缩/折叠以及如何保证训练误差、统计误差与优化误差可分离方面存在理论空白。

Method: 三部分理论：(1) 近似理论：构造光滑精确潜变量实现，对范数约束的张量积B样条类证明容量无关正则预算下的C^{1,1}逼近性质；(2) 有限样本几何：提出编码器局部-全局度量铰链（方向项与分离对项分别防止无穷小塌缩和全局折叠），在规则可观测因子、下Ahlfors覆盖及均匀C^{1,1}预算条件下，当有限样本偏差低于度量裕度阈值时，证明经验极小化器为逐点co-Lipschitz并满足一致近似受控半共轭估计，且L^2到L^∞指数在Lipschitz正则性下最优；(3) 规划迁移：度量非塌缩诱导相容Lipschitz潜成本，半共轭保证均匀轨迹与有限时域成本界及优化器迁移保障。数值实验采用有界潜范围、归档固定度量样本、有限容量系数盒样条代理及潜模型预测控制。

Result: 所有经验近似极小化器（高于可计算单侧正则化阈值）逐点co-Lipschitz且满足均匀近似受控半共轭估计；近似误差、统计误差与训练优化误差严格分离；迁移中轨迹与成本界成立且优化器可迁移；数值实验在受控摆锤上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 本文为度量保真的学习世界模型提供了严格且自洽的数学框架，证明了在合理正则条件下近似、泛化与优化误差可分离，且度量保真可保证从潜空间到原系统的规划迁移可靠性。

Abstract: We develop a three-part mathematical theory for metrically faithful learned world models for nonlinear deterministic control systems. First, on the approximation-theoretic side, we construct smooth exact latent realizations and verify the required finite-capacity $C^{1,1}$ approximation property for norm-constrained tensor-product B-spline classes with capacity-independent regularity budgets. Second, on the finite-sample geometric side, we introduce an encoder-only local--global metric hinge whose directional and separated-pair terms prevent infinitesimal collapse and global folding. Although the prediction loss is observation-based, evaluation of this penalty uses state-metric supervision through observable-state distances and tangent directions. Under a regular observable-factor assumption, lower-Ahlfors coverage, and uniform $C^{1,1}$ budgets, provided the explicit finite-sample deviation lies below the metric-margin threshold, every approximate empirical minimizer above a computable one-sided regularization threshold is pointwise co-Lipschitz and satisfies a uniform approximate controlled-semiconjugacy estimate, with approximation, statistical, and training-optimization errors kept separate. The $L^2$-to-$L^\infty$ exponent used in this step is sharp under Lipschitz regularity. Third, for deterministic planning transfer, metric non-collapse induces compatible Lipschitz latent costs, while semiconjugacy gives uniform trajectory and finite-horizon cost bounds and an optimizer-transfer guarantee; learned latent cost heads enter through explicit compatibility errors. Numerical experiments use bounded latent ranges, archived fixed metric samples, a finite-capacity coefficient-box spline proxy motivated by the theorem, and latent model-predictive control on a controlled pendulum.

</details>


### [162] [On the Optimal Laplacian Jordan Structure for Synchronizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07286)
*Luca Dieci,Cinzia Elia,Alessandro Pugliese*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有给定图结构的微分方程网络，研究如何选取Laplacian矩阵以获得最佳的同步性能，即同时保证同步解渐近稳定（主稳定函数为负值）、最小化Laplacian特征值的归一化散布以及尽可能短的暂态过程。在三对角Laplacian类中给出了满足这三项标准的充要条件，并推广到带状Laplacian，最后通过大量数值实验验证理论结果并与已有工作进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络同步研究中，Laplacian矩阵的选择直接影响网络的同步能力（可同步性）。现有工作通常只关注单一指标（如特征值比或主稳定函数），而忽略了暂态性能和特征值扩散程度等多个维度。本文旨在系统性地解决如何在给定图结构约束下优化Laplacian矩阵以同时满足多个同步性能指标的问题。

Method: 作者在三对角Laplacian矩阵类中，从三个维度（主稳定函数负值性、特征值归一化散布最小化、暂态最短化）出发，推导出满足这三个标准的必要且充分条件，并将结果推广到带状（banded）Laplacian矩阵。理论分析辅以广泛的数值模拟进行验证和对比。

Result: 给出了三对角Laplacian下满足同步稳定性、特征值最小散布和最短暂态的充要条件，并将其推广至带状Laplacian情形。数值实验验证了理论结果，并与已有方法进行了比较，展示了所提准则在实际网络同步优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为给定图结构下的微分方程网络提供了Laplacian选择的系统性准则，能够同时满足稳定同步、最优特征值分布和快速暂态收敛三个目标，对网络同步性设计具有理论和工程指导意义。

Abstract: In this work, for a network of differential equations with a prescribed graph structure, our goal is to show how to select the Laplacian of the network in order to obtain the most favorable outcome insofar as synchronizability. That is, we will want to: (i) guarantee asymptotic stability of a synchronous solution (as measured by a negative value of the master stability function), (ii) minimize the normalized spread of the Laplacian eigenvalues, and (iii) have a transient as short as possible. Within the class of tridiagonal Laplacians, we give both necessary and sufficient conditions for satisfying our three criteria above, and give extension to banded Laplacians as well. Finally, we give extensive numerical results to elucidate our theoretical results and to compare to existing works.

</details>


### [163] [Sampling and Optimization meet Enhanced Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07329)
*Yuan Gao,Siming He,Eitan Tadmor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两类具有快速收敛特性的动力学，利用输运-扩散动力学的增强耗散机制实现向Gibbs概率测度（即玻尔兹曼分布）的快速收敛，并基于此设计数值采样算法及相应的粒子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs概率测度在采样与优化问题中处于核心地位，但如何高效地数值实现这一概率测度（即从玻尔兹曼分布中采样）仍然是一个重要挑战。作者希望通过引入具有增强耗散机制的新动力学来加速向Gibbs测度的收敛，从而为高效采样提供理论基础和算法支撑。

Method: 作者引入两类输运-扩散动力学，其快速收敛的机理来源于增强耗散（enhanced dissipation）效应。基于这些增强动力学，进一步设计了针对目标Gibbs测度的数值采样算法，并给出了相应的粒子系统作为替代性数值采样方案。

Result: 文章实现了两类具有增强耗散特性的动力学，证明其可快速收敛至目标Gibbs测度，并成功据此设计出数值采样算法及配套粒子系统，为后续高效采样器的构建奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 本文为Gibbs测度的快速采样提供了新的动力学框架，通过增强耗散机制实现快速收敛，并给出了对应的数值算法和粒子系统实现，具有较大的理论价值与实用潜力。

Abstract: It is well known that the computational realization of Gibbs probability measures, $e^{-\mathbb{U}(\mathbf{x})}/Z$, plays a central role in sampling and optimization. In this paper, we introduce two types of dynamics that exhibit rapid convergence towards these Gibbs measures. The mechanism driving this rapid convergence is the enhanced dissipation associated with these transport-diffusion dynamics. Motivated by these enhanced dynamics, we design numerical algorithms for sampling from the target Gibbs measure. Finally, we provide the corresponding particle systems that may yield other effective numerical samplers.

</details>


### [164] [Solving Monotone Linear-Quadratic Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07336)
*Alberto Bemporad,Tatiana Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究N人凸二次博弈的广义纳什均衡问题，在伪梯度仅单调的条件下，将变分广义纳什均衡（v-GNE）的计算等价转化为一个凸二次规划（QP），并提出加速近端点方案和加速投影梯度方法，二者均达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义纳什均衡问题在工程和经济领域应用广泛，但现有方法在处理单调线性二次v-GNE问题时收敛速度有限或计算开销大，缺乏高效且理论上保证快速收敛的求解方案。

Method: 首先利用博弈者的联合KKT条件，将求解v-GNE等价地转化为求解单一凸二次规划问题；随后通过正则化该QP得到ε-近似v-GNE，其次优性随正则化参数线性消失；在此基础上提出加速近端点方案和加速投影梯度法，两种方法均在第k次迭代达到O(1/k²)的近似v-GNE精度；进一步证明当博弈的Jacobian可逆时可降维到更低维度的QP。

Result: 两种提出的方法均达到O(1/k²)的收敛速率，理论分析和数值实验均表明所提方法在求解单调线性二次v-GNE问题上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过等价QP转化与加速优化技术，可在仅要求伪梯度单调的条件下高效求解v-GNE问题，且收敛性能大幅超越现有方法；可逆Jacobian条件还可进一步降低计算维度。

Abstract: We consider generalized Nash equilibrium problems among $N$ players with convex quadratic costs and shared affine constraints, assuming only that the game's pseudogradient is merely monotone. We show that computing a variational generalized Nash equilibrium (v-GNE) is equivalent to solving a single convex quadratic program (QP) derived from the players' joint Karush--Kuhn--Tucker conditions. Building on this, we show that the regularization of such a QP yields an $\varepsilon$-approximated v-GNE with suboptimality vanishing linearly in the regularization parameter. Next, we propose an accelerated proximal-point scheme and an accelerated projected-gradient method, both attaining an $\mathcal O(1/k^2)$-approximated v-GNE at the $k$-th iteration. We also demonstrate that an invertible Jacobian of the game allows for reduction to a lower-dimensional QP. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments show the proposed methods substantially outperform the existing approaches to solve monotone linear-quadratic v-GNE problems.

</details>


### [165] [Uncovering expert objectives in production planning via inverse optimization: An industrial case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07398)
*Shivi Dixit,Rishabh Gupta,Adam Kelloway,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的逆优化框架，用于从专家规划者的历史决策中推断生产规划问题的目标函数。通过将生产规划建模为混合整数线性规划，并采用基于次优损失（suboptimality-loss）的逆优化方法学习目标权重，在Dow公司的真实工业案例中验证了方法的有效性。实验发现避免库存短缺和维持稳定周期长度是专家决策的主导因素，时间与产品相关的扩展方法进一步提升了预测精度并揭示了偏好的演变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业生产规划常依赖优化模型，但定义合适的目标函数颇具挑战。实践中规划者需平衡竞争目标、应对不确定性、兼顾难以量化的定性业务偏好，导致许多优化模型无法匹配专家行为，限制了信任与采用。

Method: 将生产规划问题建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP），未知目标函数表示为假设成本项的加权和；采用基于次优损失（suboptimality-loss）的逆优化方法从历史生产计划中学习目标权重；并设计了时间和产品相关的扩展版本以捕捉偏好的动态变化。

Result: 在Dow公司的真实工业案例中，推断出的权重表明避免库存短缺和维持稳定周期长度主导了专家规划者的决策；时间和产品相关的扩展方法进一步提高了预测精度，并揭示了规划者偏好的演变；专家访谈确认了这些发现的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 逆优化能够将隐性的人类专业知识转化为可解释的模型，为复杂工业系统构建更准确、更可信的决策支持工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Production planning in the manufacturing industry often relies on the use of optimization models, but defining an appropriate objective function can be a challenge. In practice, planners must balance competing goals, manage uncertainty, and account for qualitative business preferences that are difficult to quantify. As a result, many optimization models fail to match expert behavior, limiting trust and adoption. In this work, we propose a data-driven inverse optimization framework to infer the objective function implicitly captured in expert planners' decisions. We formulate the production planning problem as a mixed-integer linear program, where the unknown objective function is represented as a weighted sum of hypothesized cost terms. A suboptimality-loss-based inverse optimization method is then applied to learn the objective weights from historical production plans. The proposed approach is applied to a real industrial case provided by Dow, where the inferred weights reveal that avoiding inventory shortages and maintaining consistent cycle lengths dominate the planners' decision-making. Time- and product-dependent extensions further improve predictive accuracy and uncover evolving priorities. Expert interviews confirm the practical validity of these insights. Overall, this study shows that inverse optimization can transform tacit human expertise into interpretable models, enabling more accurate and trusted decision-support tools for complex industrial systems.

</details>


### [166] [A Domain-Specific Harness for End-to-End Automation of Optimization Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07407)
*Heechang Kim,Ernest K. Ryu,Shuvomoy Das Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoOPT，一个用于端到端自动化优化研究的领域特定框架，将最优一阶方法的发现组织为四个阶段：数值设计（BnB-PEP）、符号发现（LLM辅助推导解析描述与收敛证明）、形式化验证（Lean 4证明助手）、以及人工解读与撰写。作者在两个案例上验证了该框架：一是"lemniscate加速"方法，对光滑凸函数梯度范数最小化达到最优O(1/N⁴)速率；二是ITEM-f方法的解析描述，首次以解析形式给出了其加速线性收敛因子(1−√(μ/L))²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化研究中的方法发现通常依赖专家直觉和大量人工推导，缺乏系统化的自动化工具。虽然数值优化技术（如PEP/BnB-PEP）可以揭示最优方法的数值特征，但从数值结果到解析描述、收敛证明再到形式验证之间存在巨大鸿沟，需要耗费大量人力。作者希望构建一个端到端自动化的框架，弥合数值发现与数学证明之间的差距。

Method: AutoOPT框架包含四个阶段：(1) 数值设计：使用BnB-PEP（Branch-and-Bound Performance Estimation Problem）方法数值搜索最优一阶方法；(2) 符号发现：利用前沿大语言模型（LLMs）从数值模式中推导解析描述和收敛证明；(3) 形式化验证：在Lean 4证明助手中对收敛定理进行机器验证；(4) 人工解读与撰写：由研究者进行解释和撰写论文。框架设计将数值计算、AI推理和形式化验证有机结合。

Result: 在两个独立案例上验证了框架：(1) lemniscate加速方法，一种新的加速梯度方法，在N步后以O(1/N⁴)的最优速率降低光滑凸函数的梯度范数平方，其常数由lemniscate常数ϖ（经典椭圆积分常数）控制；(2) ITEM-f的解析描述，该方法此前仅以数值形式存在，现在给出了其对于L-光滑、μ-强凸最小化问题的加速线性收敛因子(1−√(μ/L))²。两个案例的收敛定理均在Lean 4中形式化并机器验证。

Conclusion: AutoOPT展示了优化研究中自动化发现的可行性：通过将数值设计、LLM驱动的符号发现和形式化验证相结合，可以自动发现新的优化方法及其收敛理论，并确保数学正确性。这项工作为优化研究提供了一种可扩展的自动化范式，并在过程中发现了两个新的/更完整的数学结果（lemniscate加速方法和ITEM-f的解析形式）。

Abstract: We present AutoOPT, a domain-specific harness for end-to-end automation of optimization research. AutoOPT organizes the discovery of optimal first-order methods into four stages: numerical design through the BnB-PEP methodology; symbolic discovery of the analytic description and a convergence proof through frontier large language models (LLMs); formal verification in the Lean 4 proof assistant; and human interpretation and write-up. We demonstrate the framework on two case studies, each of independent interest. The first, lemniscate acceleration, is a new accelerated gradient method for minimizing the gradient norm of a smooth convex function: after $N$ gradient steps it reduces the squared gradient norm at the optimal $O(1/N^{4})$ rate, with a constant governed by the lemniscate constant $\varpi$, a classical elliptic-integral constant. The second is the analytic description of ITEM-f, a method previously known only numerically: for $L$-smooth, $μ$-strongly convex minimization it contracts the function-value gap at an accelerated linear rate with a per-step factor $(1-\sqrt{μ/L})^{2}$. The convergence theorems of both case studies are formalized and machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [167] [Wasserstein Policy Gradient for Entropy-Regularized Linear-Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07433)
*Zhaoyu Zhu,Rui Gao,Shuang Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究熵正则化的折扣线性二次（LQ）控制问题中的Wasserstein策略梯度（WPG）算法。通过Bellman验证论证表明无约束问题存在线性高斯最优策略，且WPG梯度恰好切于此策略类，因此WPG精确地归结为一个关于反馈增益和动作协方差的有限维ODE。作者证明该ODE全局适定且从任意可行初始值指数收敛，收敛指数在熵温度趋于零时具有正极限，且不含exp(-c/τ)形式的微扰因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein策略梯度是一种在动作空间中对条件动作分布进行传输更新的强化学习算法，但其收敛性质的理论分析尚不充分。作者选择熵正则化折扣LQ控制这一可解析处理的问题框架，旨在严格刻画WPG的精确动力学和收敛行为。

Method: 首先通过Bellman验证论证证明无约束（非熵正则化）LQ问题具有线性高斯最优策略。然后证明折扣占用加权下的状态条件Wasserstein梯度恰好与这个策略类相切，从而使WPG从有限维参数化出发仍然保持在该参数空间内，精确归结为反馈增益和动作协方差的ODE。最后对该ODE进行全局适定性和指数收敛性分析。

Result: 证明该ODE从任意可行初始值都全局适定且指数收敛。对每个固定的LQ问题，当熵温度趋于零时收敛指数存在正极限，且不包含exp(-c/τ)形式的指数小因子，仅保留与控制问题条件数相关的通常依赖。

Conclusion: WPG在熵正则化LQ控制中具有精确的有限维动力学描述，其收敛速率不会因熵温度趋零而出现指数退化（即无小参数奇点），这为Wasserstein策略梯度方法在更广泛问题中的理论分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Wasserstein policy gradient (WPG) updates state-conditional action laws by transport in the action space. We study entropy-regularized discounted linear-quadratic (LQ) control. A Bellman verification argument shows that the unrestricted problem has a linear-Gaussian optimal policy, and the discounted-occupancy-weighted statewise Wasserstein gradient is tangent to this policy class. WPG therefore reduces exactly to a finite-dimensional ODE for the feedback gain and action covariance. We prove that this ODE is globally well posed and converges exponentially from every admissible initialization. For each fixed LQ problem, the exponent has a positive limit as the entropy temperature tends to zero and contains no perturbative factor of the form $\exp(-c/τ)$, while retaining the usual dependence on the conditioning of the control problem.

</details>


### [168] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价函数与下半连续prox-bounded函数之和，其中期望代价的积分核可能非光滑、非凸。该方法通过逐步细化的样本均值近似期望代价，并采用Armijo型线搜索选择步长以确保充分下降性质。在仅要求样本量序列非降无界的宽松条件下，建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛和所有聚点稳定性的保证，并在KL性质下进一步升级为整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点，对指数型KL函数和多项式增长的样本量给出了明确的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法通常要求正则项的(弱)凸性以及随机oracle方差的均匀有界性，这些假设在实际问题中过于严格。本文旨在放宽这些限制，使框架能够处理更广泛的非光滑、非凸问题，包括光滑损失与凸函数的差函数等结构。

Method: 提出近端随机次梯度方法：(1) 用逐步细化的样本平均近似期望代价；(2) 采用Armijo型线搜索的自适应步长选择确保充分下降；(3) 利用非光滑的局域化下降引理作为核心结构假设；(4) 结合KL性质分析整条轨迹收敛；(5) 推导特定条件下的显式收敛速率。

Result: 建立了在样本量序列仅需非降且无界条件下的几乎必然收敛性（函数值）和所有聚点的稳定性；在KL性质下获得整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点的结果；对指数型KL去奇异化函数和多项式增长的样本量，导出了函数值和迭代序列的显式多项式收敛速率（至多差对数因子）。这些收敛保证即使在光滑情形下也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适用的优化问题形式更加广泛，不需要正则项的(弱)凸性，也不需要随机oracle方差的均匀有界假设，收敛保证在光滑情形下也具有新颖性。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>

### [169] [Emergence of chaos and fractality in the basin boundary of subcritical shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06537)
*Baoying Wang,Roger Ayats,Kengo Deguchi,Alvaro Meseguer,Fernando Mellibovsky*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文从动力学系统角度研究Taylor-Couette流中亚临界转捩中边缘态的混沌化机制，揭示了由异宿纠缠和异宿环两步机制驱动的边缘态从简单精确相干结构向混沌态转变的普适过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在剪切流亚临界转捩中，层流与湍流吸引子之间的盆地边界及其上控制长期动力学的边缘态具有根本性意义。低雷诺数下边缘态通常为简单精确相干结构，但在高雷诺数下盆边界动力学对初始条件敏感，其潜在机制尚不明确。

Method: 采用最小计算域对Taylor-Couette流动的亚临界区间进行数值研究，通过分析行波型精确相干结构与独立产生的混沌鞍之间的异宿纠缠及异宿环的形成过程，揭示边缘态混沌化的机制。

Result: 发现边缘态混沌化的两步机制：第一步，行波型精确相干结构与混沌鞍之间形成异宿纠缠，使盆地边界纳入混沌鞍并继承其分形结构，但边缘态本身仍保持简单精确相干结构；第二步，精确相干结构与混沌鞍之间形成异宿环，导致边缘态转变为混沌。

Conclusion: 给定参数下观察到简单非混沌边缘态并不保证邻近参数下仍如此，因为边缘态的混沌化由异宿纠缠和异宿环两步机制所决定。

Abstract: From a dynamical systems perspective of subcritical transition in shear flows, the basin boundary separating the laminar and turbulent attractors, along with the edge state that governs the long-term dynamics on that boundary, are of fundamental interest. As the Reynolds number is increased from small values, a multiplicity of simple exact coherent structures (ECS) appear in phase space, of which one often undertakes the role of the edge state. At higher values of the Reynolds number, however, the dynamics on the basin boundary and the edge state are sensitive to initial conditions, as shown by a wealth of numerical and experimental studies. The mechanism behind this transition remains unclear. To address this, we examine the subcritical regime of Taylor-Couette flow using a minimal computational box and reveal a generic mechanism whereby the edge state becomes chaotic. The first step in this process involves the formation of a heteroclinic tangle between a travelling-wave-type ECS and an independently engendered chaotic saddle. The interaction causes the basin boundary to incorporate the saddle, thus inheriting its fractal structure, while the edge state itself remains the simple ECS. The transition of the edge state to chaos requires a second step: the formation of a heteroclinic cycle involving the ECS and the chaotic saddle. In consequence, the observation of a simple non-chaotic edge state at given values of the parameters is no guarantee that the same situation will hold at nearby values.

</details>


### [170] [Nonisothermal Shock Structure and Universal Flow-Index Thresholds in a Hyperbolic Power-Law Fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06547)
*Tommaso Ruggeri*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了双曲幂律松弛模型（Rational Extended Thermodynamics）中非等温激波剖面的存在性与分类，发现先前等温情形中的两个流量指数阈值在完整非等温动力学下依然成立，虽然非等温剖面问题在结构上与等温情形有本质不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究仅在等温假设下确定了激波剖面的流动指数阈值，但真实物理过程涉及温度变化、能量平衡与热力学耦合。本文旨在检验这些阈值在完整的非等温动力学下是否仍然稳健，并厘清温度效应对激波结构的影响。

Method: 将非等温剖面问题归结为恢复能量平衡的常微分方程组，利用热力学稳定性条件（p_θ≥0）与约化Hugoniot压力的严格凸性，推导了精确的全局特征序恒等式，证明非平衡特征速度在未扰动上游态取得严格全局最小值；进而利用Boillat-Ruggeri定理对亚临界与超临界Mach数分别分析C^1剖面的存在性与次激波结构的必要性。

Result: 对于1<M_0<M*存在单调连续剖面，而M_0>M*时不存在C^1剖面且任何允许的分段光滑连接必须包含次激波。激波厚度分类仍然不变：m=2为弱激波阈值，m=1为近临界阈值；指数在本类本构中不依赖本构，但有限极限值与前置因子依赖状态方程、内能及温度依赖的一致性系数。Tait-Murnaghan例子中，固定量纲粘性-松弛尺度时，参考温度升高会降低临界Mach数，并减少解析激波厚度。

Conclusion: 非等温耦合虽改变了剖面问题的结构并排除非平凡的等温压缩剖面，但先前的激波存在性阈值与厚度分类在完整的非等温动力学下保持成立，体现出这些阈值的稳健性；定量结果则依赖于具体热力学本构。

Abstract: We investigate whether the two flow-index thresholds previously found for isothermal shock profiles persist when the full nonisothermal dynamics is taken into account in a hyperbolic power-law relaxation model of Rational Extended Thermodynamics. The nonisothermal profile problem is structurally different from its isothermal counterpart: restoring the energy balance determines the temperature along the traveling wave and feeds it back into the pressure, the relaxation production, the temperature-dependent consistency coefficient, and the characteristic structure. Under thermodynamic stability, $p_θ\ge0$, and strict convexity of the reduced Hugoniot pressure, no nontrivial constant-temperature compressive profile can satisfy the full equations. We derive an exact global characteristic-ordering identity and prove that the positive nonequilibrium characteristic speed has its strict global minimum at the unperturbed upstream state. Consequently, a monotone continuous profile exists for $1<M_0<\Mst$, whereas for $M_0>\Mst$ the Boillat--Ruggeri theorem excludes a $C^1$ profile and any admissible piecewise-smooth connection must contain a subshock. Despite the thermomechanical coupling, the shock-thickness classification remains unchanged: $m=2$ is the weak-shock threshold and $m=1$ the near-critical threshold as $M_0\nearrow\Mst$. The corresponding exponents are constitutive-independent within the present class, while finite limiting values and prefactors depend on the equation of state, internal energy, and temperature-dependent consistency coefficient. For the Tait--Murnaghan example, increasing the reference temperature lowers the critical Mach number when the dimensional viscous--relaxation scale is fixed and, for the thermally thinning law considered, reduces the resolved shock thickness.

</details>


### [171] [Effect of Chordwise Flexibility Distribution on Wave-Assisted Flapping Foil Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06561)
*Lokesh Silwal,Neel Karani,Anchal Sareen*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了柔性分布位置（弦向位置）对波浪驱动扑动翼推进性能的影响，发现全柔性构型在推进效率上最高可提升约164%，而中部柔性构型在大幅高频条件下推力最高可提升约66%，揭示了柔性分布通过调节尾迹相干性和动量传递来调控推力与效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已知柔性能够增强扑动推进性能，但柔性沿弦向的分布位置对性能的影响机制尚不清楚。理解这一关系对于优化波浪驱动无人水面航行器的推进系统设计至关重要。

Method: 通过沿尾向移动铰接/弯曲位置系统改变有效柔性长度，在保持弯曲刚度和总弦长不变的前提下，于静水环境中进行实验。在两种沉浮频率（0.8 Hz和1.25 Hz）和两种无量纲沉浮幅度（h*=0.13和0.22）条件下，同步测量水动力、流场和尾部运动学，量化性能并阐明流固耦合与流体-粒子相互作用。

Result: 全柔性构型在所有条件下效率最高（提升约164%），归因于增强的射流持续性和增大的流向涡间距；中部柔性构型在最大频率和幅度条件下推力最高（提升约66%），源于近尾迹射流速度和动量通量的显著增加。

Conclusion: 柔性沿弦向的分布位置是扑动推进的关键设计参数，通过调节尾迹相干性和动量传递来主导推力与推进效率之间的权衡关系，为波浪驱动无人水面航行器的性能与续航增强提供了基于物理的指导原则。

Abstract: This study investigates the influence of the spatial distribution of flexibility along a propulsor on thrust generation and propulsive efficiency in wave-assisted flapping foils. While flexibility is known to enhance propulsive performance, the role of its chordwise placement remains poorly understood. Here, the effective flexible length is systematically varied by shifting the flexure location along the tail while maintaining constant flexural rigidity and total chord length. Experiments are conducted in quiescent flow at heave frequencies of 0.8 Hz and 1.25 Hz, and non-dimensional heave amplitudes of h* = 0.13 and 0.22. Simultaneous measurements of hydrodynamic forces, flow fields, and tail kinematics are used to quantify performance and elucidate the underlying fluid-structure and fluid-particle interactions. The fully flexible configuration consistently achieves higher propulsive efficiency (up to approximately 164%) across all conditions, which is attributed to enhanced jet persistence and increased streamwise vortex spacing, indicative of a more coherent and sustained momentum jet. In contrast, the mid-flexible configuration yields substantially higher thrust (up to approximately 66%) at the largest heave frequency and amplitude, driven by a pronounced increase in near-wake jet velocity and momentum flux. These results demonstrate that the chordwise distribution of flexibility governs the trade-off between thrust and propulsive efficiency by modulating wake coherence and momentum transfer. The findings establish flexibility placement as a key design parameter in flapping propulsion and provide physics-based guidelines for enhancing the performance and endurance of wave-driven unmanned surface vehicles.

</details>


### [172] [A Symplectic Theory of Turbulence Closure: Hidden Reservoir Dynamics, Endogenous Stochastic Transport, and Kraichnan Dual Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06606)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,James C. McWilliams*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出辛几何闭合（SGC），一种基于多层随机模型几何约化的二维湍流亚网格参数化方法。通过隐藏辛自由度储库与解析流场间的约束哈密顿交换实现几何稳定的闭合，理论等价于Kraichnan直接相互作用近似，并重现经典k^-5/3逆能量级联和k^-3正涡度级联谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维湍流的亚网格尺度参数化必须保持使双级联成立的几何结构，但经典闭合与数据驱动闭合往往缺乏实现在线稳定性所需的对称性。这导致现有方法在数值或物理上不稳定，因此需要一种能几何上保证稳定性的闭合方案。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 从多层随机模型中几何约化出辛几何闭合（SGC）；(2) 引入未解析辛自由度隐储库，通过约束哈密顿交换与解析流场反馈交互；(3) 由辛泛函G生成交互，保持增强涡度，实现Navier-Stokes-β核的拉回有界性；(4) 在双曲型黏性实现中获得紧随机拉回吸引子；(5) 消除储库得到有限记忆欧拉理论，在一圈线重整化阶恢复Kraichnan型直接相互作用近似的算子架构；(6) 嵌套Jacobi顶点兼容随机伽利略变换，实现O(p^4)四阶红外抑制。

Result: SGC具有以下关键结果：(1) 几何强制稳定性，无需人工调整即可保证动态系统的有界性；(2) 储库消除后，受限欧拉传递理论在应变诱导形变（而非扫掠）控制的退相关下，自洽地支持经典k^-5/3逆能量级联和k^-3正向涡度级联；(3) 嵌套Jacobi顶点天然实现均匀扫掠模式的四阶红外抑制；(4) 为受几何约束、保持稳定性的数据驱动闭合提供原理性基础。

Conclusion: SGC提供了Kraichnan程序的一种欧拉几何实现，统一了几何约束稳定性与经典湍流统计理论，并为后续开发几何约束且保持稳定性的数据驱动亚网格闭合模型奠定了原理性基础。

Abstract: Subgrid-scale parameterization of 2D turbulence must preserve the geometry enabling the dual cascade. Classical and data-driven closures often lack the symmetries required for online stability. We introduce the Symplectic Geometric Closure (SGC), derived from a geometric reduction of Multilayer Stochastic Models. Its core is a hidden reservoir of unresolved symplectic degrees of freedom feeding back on the resolved flow through a constrained Hamiltonian exchange.
  Stability is enforced geometrically. The interaction is generated by a symplectic functional $\mathcal{G}$ that preserves augmented enstrophy, yielding pullback boundedness for the Navier--Stokes--$β$ core and a compact random pullback attractor in the hyperviscous realization. The same generator produces an emergent Hamiltonian subgrid velocity whose forcing is exactly the Lie transport of resolved vorticity. Unresolved stochastic activity thus renormalizes the advecting geometry while maintaining the Hamiltonian structure of incompressible 2D motion.
  Eliminating the reservoir yields a finite-memory Eulerian theory. At one-loop line-renormalized order, reservoir contractions reproduce the operator architecture of Kraichnan-type Direct-Interaction Approximation theory. Crucially, the nested Jacobian vertex is compatible with Random Galilean transformations and imposes an intrinsic fourth-order infrared suppression, $\mathcal{O}(p^4)$, of uniform sweeping modes within the Eulerian interaction.
  With decorrelation controlled by strain-induced deformation rather than sweeping, the dressed Eulerian transfer theory self-consistently admits the classical $k^{-5/3}$ inverse-energy and $k^{-3}$ forward-enstrophy cascades. SGC thus provides an Eulerian geometric realization of Kraichnan's program and a principled foundation for geometrically constrained, stability-preserving data-driven closures.

</details>


### [173] [Transient Electrical Response Beyond Quasistatic Capacitance at Mechanically Excited Droplet--Dielectric Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07153)
*Pramodt Srinivasula,Rameez Raja Khan,Diwakar Singh,Gaurav Bhutani*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了机械形变下导电液滴的动态电润湿瞬态行为，发现传统准静态可变电容模型虽能预测接触面积和电容演化，却无法解释实验中周期机械激励下电流与电功率的不对称瞬态响应。研究表明瞬态电荷演化由液滴电液动力学与介电界面极化耦合控制，需超越仅基于接触线动力学的准静态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准静态可变电容模型假设电荷瞬时平衡，仅由接触面积演化决定电学响应，虽能描述循环稳态电机械响应，但其对瞬态界面电学动力学的适用性尚未被充分验证，亟需探究。

Method: 将汞液滴约束于聚合物介电涂层电极（PTFE或PVDF）与对置铜电极之间，在2 Hz频率下施加多种波形的周期机械激励，同步测量接触面积、电容、瞬态电流和瞬时电功率，并用一阶界面介电电荷弛豫动力学进行现象学解释。

Result: 接触面积与电容演化符合瞬时表面能最小化预测，证实液-液界面力学为准静态；但瞬态电流与电功率呈现与激励波形无关的不对称激发/弛豫相位，无法仅由瞬时几何电容推导，表明瞬态电荷演化包含慢速介电充电后随周期机械振荡时间尺度进行的介电弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 瞬态电润湿由液滴电液动力学与介电界面极化的耦合作用支配，需建立超越准静态可变电容模型、纳入介电弛豫的构成本构描述。

Abstract: Dynamic electrowetting of conducting droplets under mechanical deformation is conventionally modeled as a quasi-static variable-capacitance system, in which the electrical response is assumed to be governed solely by the evolution of the droplet--electrode contact area. Under the assumption of instantaneous charge equilibration, this framework successfully describes the cyclic steady-state electromechanical response of the system. However, its validity for transient interfacial electrical dynamics remains largely unexplored. Here, the transient electrowetting response of mercury droplets confined between a polymeric dielectric-coated electrode (PTFE or PVDF) and an opposing copper electrode is investigated under periodic mechanical excitation with multiple waveforms at 2 Hz. The measured contact area and corresponding capacitance evolve closely as predicted from instantaneous surface-energy minimization, confirming that the liquid-interface mechanics remain quasi-static. In contrast, the measured transient current and instantaneous electrical power exhibit pronounced asymmetric excitation and relaxation phases that are independent of the excitation waveform, demonstrating that transient charge evolution cannot be inferred from the instantaneous geometric capacitance alone. This transient behavior is phenomenologically interpreted using constituent first-order interfacial dielectric charge-relaxation kinetics, indicating that the measured current arises from slow dielectric charging followed by dielectric relaxation over the timescale of the imposed periodic mechanical oscillations during discharging. These findings establish that transient electrowetting is governed by the coupled interplay of droplet electrohydrodynamics and dielectric interfacial polarization, requiring a constitutive description beyond quasi-static variable-capacitance models based solely on contact-line dynamics.

</details>


### [174] [Autoregressive rollout error in latent-space reduced-order models of bluff-body wakes is accumulated phase drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07189)
*Suvam Samanta,Sachidananda Behera*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文发现自回归降阶模型在钝体尾流（Re=100-800）中的滚动误差主要是高度结构化的相位误差而非非结构化噪声。该模型几乎完美重建极限环几何（振幅误差<0.15%），但以略错误的速度遍历周期，导致长期误差累积。相位漂移呈线性，可在无需重训练的条件下通过每个潜坐标单参数离线校正，且相位拟合的信噪比可作为校正成功的诊断指标（r=0.85）。相较于仅在稳态极限环上有效的周期基线，该方法在非定常入流下仍能消除约一半滚动误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归降阶模型在长期滚动预测中误差累积严重，已有研究通常将此误差视为非结构化噪声。作者旨在揭示这类误差的真实结构，从而开发更有效的误差校正方法。

Method: 使用卷积自编码器LSTM模型预测钝体尾流（Re=100-800），对滚动误差进行结构分解，分离相位误差与振幅误差。提出基于每个潜坐标单参数（相位拟合）的离线校正方法，并利用相位拟合信噪比作为校正成功率的诊断指标。

Result: 95%-98%的误差为纯相位误差，集中于涡脱落频率；模型以0.15%以内精度复现极限环振幅但遍历速率稍有偏差。单参数相位校正可离线消除相位漂移，在校准窗口上的拟合信噪比与校正成功率相关性达r=0.85（50个网络）。在非定常入流下，该方法仍能去除约一半滚动误差，而周期基线方法失效（误差超过一个数量级）。

Conclusion: 自回归降阶模型的长期滚动误差并非随机噪声，而是由线性相位漂移主导的结构化误差。基于相位连贯性的单参数校正能在无需重训练的情况下有效减少滚动误差，且适用于非定常流动，优于仅限周期基线的传统方法。

Abstract: Autoregressive reduced order models suffer from compounding long horizon rollout errors, typically treated as unstructured noise. We demonstrate that for bluff body wakes across Re=100 to 800, this rollout error is highly structured and reveals what these models actually learn. For a convolutional autoencoder LSTM model, 95 % to 98% of the error is pure phase error, peaking sharply at the vortex shedding frequency. The network reproduces the attractor geometry almost exactly, matching limit cycle amplitudes within 0.15%, but traverses the cycle at slightly the wrong rate. This timing error, accumulating to just a few thousandths of a cycle over the entire rollout, drives the long horizon error even while one step validation errors appear virtually perfect. Because phase error drifts linearly, it can be corrected offline without retraining using just one parameter per latent coordinate, fitted on a short calibration window. The signal to noise ratio of this phase fit serves as a diagnostic that reliably predicts correction success (r=0.85 across 50 networks). While simple periodic baselines match this performance on stationary limit cycles, they fail by over an order of magnitude when applied to wakes driven by slowly varying inflows. Conversely, our phase correction requires only phase coherence, successfully removing roughly half of the rollout error during non stationary flow.

</details>


### [175] [Efficient three-dimensional variational data assimilation of multi-plane PIV data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07469)
*Uttam Cadambi Padmanaban,Samaresh Midya,Ping He,Bharathram Ganapathisubramani,Sean Symon*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文采用离散伴随方法进行三维变分数据同化（3DVar），通过优化时间平均动量方程来同化稀疏立体PIV实验数据，并结合伴随局域化技术将控制变量空间限制在用户定义子域内，在12%控制空间下实现64%的峰值内存缩减，成功恢复复杂三维分离流动的非对称拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高雷诺数下车辆钝体尾迹的三维分离流动是一个重要的工业问题，但直接数值模拟和实验数据同化面临巨大的计算和内存开销。稀疏实验数据（如12个跨流平面的立体PIV测量）难以直接约束大规模三维分离流场，亟需高效的变分数据同化方法在精细网格上处理工业级问题。

Method: 提出离散伴随方法驱动的3DVar框架：1）以时间平均动量方程为约束，优化控制变量以匹配稀疏PIV实验数据；2）引入伴随局域化（adjoint localisation）技术，将控制变量空间限制在用户定义子域，减少离散伴随方法在空间变化控制变量上的内存占用；3）采用Spalart-Allmaras湍流模型作为基线，展示同化场对该模型的改进；4）进行数据效率研究，逐步减少用于同化的平面数以评估数据覆盖要求。

Result: 将控制变量限制在完整控制空间的12%时，峰值内存最大降低64%，同时同化场在平均速度和最优动量强迫场方面保持可比拟的精度。局域化伴随案例优于基线Spalart-Allmaras湍流模型，正确恢复了复杂三维回流泡的不对称拓扑结构。同化的雷诺剪切应力与实验吻合良好，同化平均压力与面内涡量场在物理上一致。数据效率研究显示，数据覆盖必须延伸至主回流泡末端才能充分约束近尾迹动力学。

Conclusion: 伴随局域化带来的计算效率对于在精细网格上同化三维分离流动的稀疏实验数据至关重要，使3DVar方法能够扩展至具有工业应用价值的实际问题。

Abstract: We perform three-dimensional variational data assimilation (3DVar) using a discrete adjoint approach to optimise the time-averaged momentum equations. The experimental data consist of sparse stereoscopic particle image velocimetry (PIV) measurements collected along $12$ cross-stream planes in the wake of a vehicle-like bluff body at a Reynolds number $Re_L = 5.64 \times 10^5$ based on the streamwise body length. Adjoint localisation is proposed and implemented to reduce the memory footprint of the discrete adjoint method for spatially-varying control variables in 3DVar by confining the control variable space to a user-defined subdomain. Restricting the control variable to $12$ % of the full control space yields a maximum reduction in peak memory of $64$ %, while producing assimilated fields of comparable fidelity with respect to mean velocity and the optimised momentum forcing field. The localised adjoint case improves upon the baseline Spalart--Allmaras turbulence model and recovers the correct asymmetric topology of the complex three-dimensional (3D) recirculation bubble. The assimilated Reynolds shear stress agrees well with the experiment, and the assimilated mean pressure is shown to be physically consistent when correlated with the in-plane vorticity fields. A data efficiency study is also performed, in which the number of planes provided for assimilation is progressively reduced, demonstrating that the data coverage must extend at least to the end of the primary recirculation bubble to adequately constrain the near-wake dynamics. The efficiency that adjoint localisation affords is crucial for assimilating sparse, experimental data for 3D separated flows on fine meshes that can tackle industrial problems of interest.

</details>
